Abstract: The gold standard datasets for mapping connectomes are electron microscopy volumes of densely labeled neural tissue at nanometer resolution. Yet reconstructing and proofreading neuronal arbors and annotating all synapses requires pipelining multiple software tools that are often fragmented, inconsistently maintained, or proprietary, hindering reproducibility and automation. Here, we introduce Catena, an open-source, comprehensive, developer-centric software suite for connectomics that integrates modules for 3D neuron and organelle segmentation, synapse detection, microtubule tracking, and neurotransmitter inference. Catena organizes its modules in composable, chunk-wise processing pipelines in a completely documented, extensible, and adaptable design. We further reduce compute and ground-truth data requirements with pretrained machine learning models, facilitating fine-tuning. Catena ships fully containerized modules that encapsulate evolving dependencies for consistent execution across workstations and clusters. By consolidating open components, shareable models, and containerized runtimes, Catena delivers a reproducible and scalable approach to mapping cellular connectomes from electron microscopy volumes. Code and documentation: https://github.com/Mohinta2892/catena.git

</details>


### [69] [The Role of Radiometric Features in Cross-Site Leaf-Wood Segmentation of LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21903)
*Roman Kaharlytskyi,Derek T. Robinson,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了现有叶片-木质分割方法为追求跨传感器兼容性而弃用辐射特征（强度、回波数等）的设计，通过在Heidelberg数据集（地基TLS，1550nm）训练、在加拿大Ontario新数据集（RPA-LS，905nm）上测试，证明辐射特征可显著提升跨站点、跨平台的泛化能力与结构连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单株树木的叶片-木质分割是定量结构模型（QSM）与非破坏性生物量估算的关键前提。现有方法通常排除辐射特征（如强度、回波号）以最大化跨传感器兼容性。作者质疑这一设计选择，旨在评估在跨站点、跨平台场景下的真实泛化性能。

Method: 采用跨站点、跨平台评估框架：在公开的Heidelberg数据集（地基TLS，1550nm）上训练，在加拿大Ontario的新数据集（RPA-LS，905nm）上测试。对比仅使用几何特征的方法（含在高密度LiDAR数据集上训练的先进深度学习模型）与引入辐射特征（强度、回波号、回波数量）的方法，并分析其对木质召回率与结构连通性的影响。

Result: 仅几何方法（包括在高密度LiDAR数据集上训练的先进深度学习模型）无法泛化到航空扫描的稀疏、俯视几何形态，F1分数≤0.56；引入辐射特征后F1提升至0.61，且木质召回率从0.16提高到0.35，增幅达119%。此外，仅几何方法常导致树干与树枝组件碎片化，而辐射特征保留了更好的结构连通性。

Conclusion: 几何模式依赖于视角、易过拟合扫描模式，而辐射特征编码物理材质属性，能在不同传感器与环境中泛化。因此，保留辐射特征可获得更连贯的树木结构，更利于QSM重建。

Abstract: Leaf-wood segmentation of individual trees from LiDAR point clouds is essential for quantitative structure models (QSMs) used in non-destructive biomass estimation. Existing segmentation methods typically exclude radiometric features (e.g., intensity, return number) to maximize cross-sensor compatibility. We challenge this design choice by evaluating cross-site and cross-platform generalization: training on the public Heidelberg dataset (terrestrial TLS, 1550nm) and testing on a novel dataset from Ontario, Canada (RPA-LS, 905nm). Results show that geometry-only methods - including state-of-the-art deep learning models trained on high-density LiDAR datasets - fail to generalize to the sparse, top-down geometry of aerial scans, achieving F1 scores <= 0.56. Incorporating radiometric features (intensity, return number, number of returns) improves F1 to 0.61, but more critically, increases wood recall by 119% from 0.16 to 0.35. Furthermore, geometry-only approaches often result in fragmented stem and branch components. We find that leveraging radiometric features preserves greater structural connectivity, resulting in more coherent architectures that are better suited for QSM reconstruction. We demonstrate that while geometric patterns are view-dependent and prone to overfitting scan patterns, radiometric features encode physical material properties that generalize across disparate sensors and environments.

</details>


### [70] [Info3R: Information-Adaptive Test-Time Training for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21938)
*Sunghyun Baek,Hanna Bae,Minchan Kwon,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Info3R，一种面向在线3D重建的信息自适应测试时训练方法，通过信息感知的状态更新与动态状态重置，缓解长序列中的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的在线3D重建方法在处理长图像流时，忽略了两个关键信号：每个新到帧的重要性，以及模型内部状态的信息饱和度。这导致在长序列评估中出现显著性能退化。

Method: 1) 信息感知的状态更新：根据每帧的冗余度与信息量调节状态更新的强度；2) 动态状态重置：以状态更新的累积幅度和模型预测置信度为触发条件，并配合 anchor-to-world 对齐，从而恢复状态的“可塑性”（吸收新观测的能力）。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、视频深度估计和3D重建任务上取得一致性提升，并大幅缓解长序列评估中的性能退化。在 KITTI Odometry 上，其平均 ATE 比 LongStream 低约 1.68 倍，显示出在长距离户外序列上的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Info3R 通过显式建模帧重要性与状态饱和程度，实现了自适应的状态更新与重置，有效提升在线3D重建在长序列场景下的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently achieved strong performance on 3D reconstruction from images, and recent works extend them to process video streams in an online manner for real-world deployment. However, existing methods overlook two key signals when handling long image streams: the importance of each incoming frame and the information saturation of the model's internal state. In this paper, we propose Info3R, a novel information-adaptive test-time training method for the online 3D reconstruction. We introduce an information-aware state update that modulates the state update strength based on the redundancy and informativeness of each incoming frame. To restore the state's plasticity -- its capacity to incorporate new observations -- we propose a dynamic state reset, triggered by the cumulative magnitude of state updates and the model's prediction confidence and accompanied by an anchor-to-world alignment. Our method achieves consistent improvements on camera pose estimation, video depth estimation, and 3D reconstruction, while substantially mitigating the performance degradation in the long sequence evaluation. Notably, on KITTI Odometry, our method achieves on average 1.68x lower ATE than LongStream, demonstrating its robustness on extended outdoor sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIME 是一种面向自动驾驶 VLA 模型的反馈机制：通过情境记忆聚合过去感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为，使感知查询具备意图驱动能力，在仅增加约0.41%参数的情况下，于 Bench2Drive 闭环基准取得 SOTA 驾驶得分与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶 VLA 模型主要沿“感知—推理—规划”层级进行前馈推理。尽管感知模块内部保留时间递归，但早期感知对下游推理和导航目标不可见，只能无差别处理视觉输入，难以优先关注由先前决策所指示的关键线索。因此需要打通从下游意图到早期感知的反馈通路。

Method: 提出 PRIME（learned feedback mechanism），将 VLA 的感知查询条件化于一种新颖的“情境记忆”（Situational Memory）。该记忆在 L 步窗口内聚合过去感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为的潜在表示，并通过交叉注意力机制注入感知查询，实现意图驱动的感知注意力。方法计算开销小，最多仅增加 29.7M 参数，占 7.3B 基础模型的 0.41%。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 闭环基准上，PRIME 取得 SOTA 驾驶得分 Driving Score = 82.47，较 ORION 提升 +4.73；成功率为 60.00%，提升 +5.38 个百分点。该驾驶得分是在 Think2Drive 演示数据上训练的已发表 VLA 模型中最高的报告结果。

Conclusion: PRIME 通过将过去感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为压缩为情境记忆，并以交叉注意力反馈至感知查询，有效弥补了早期感知与下游意图之间的鸿沟。其以极低参数增量显著提升闭环驾驶性能，表明意图驱动感知反馈是提升自动驾驶 VLA 模型的有效方向。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [72] [Traffic Sign Recognition for Autonomous Driving Using Branched YOLOv2 and Geometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22060)
*Arefeh Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于 YOLOv2 的交通标志识别系统，同时完成检测与分类；通过分支式中间预测层实现早退推理，并利用无监督贝叶斯分割生成的几何模板校验视觉相似标志。在自建 GTSDB+GTSRB 数据集上，分支结构在 mAP 不变（0.680）下将运行时间降至 0.647 s，而推理阶段几何校验将 mAP 提升至 0.713。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别是自动驾驶与高级驾驶辅助系统的重要感知任务，系统需高效地同时定位交通标志并判定其语义类别。现有方法在计算效率与视觉相似标志的细粒度分类准确性方面仍存在不足，因此本文研究如何在 YOLOv2 框架下兼顾效率与分类精度。

Method: 以 YOLOv2 为基础构建同时检测与分类的 TSR 系统，并研究两类互补改进：其一，在 YOLOv2 中加入中间预测层，形成分支架构，使简单样本可提前终止推理以降低计算量，并比较整图分支与单元格级分支策略；其二，引入几何信息以降低视觉相似标志间的分类错误，采用无监督贝叶斯图像分割生成二值表示，并在 YOLOv2 边界框内与类别特定的几何模板比较，该信息可用于推理阶段或作为训练额外信号。数据集由 GTSDB 与 GTSRB 通过无缝克隆和受控图像变换构建，覆盖 10 类交通标志，含 3,000 个训练样本和 300 个测试样本。

Result: 所选分支架构运行时间为 0.647 s、mAP 为 0.680；基线 YOLOv2 为 0.6607 s、mAP 0.680。推理阶段加入几何校验后 mAP 提升至 0.713；几何特征训练变体达到 0.697 mAP，运行时间 0.6608 s。结果表明分支结构在不损失 mAP 的情况下降低推理时间，而几何校验可显著提高检测精度。

Conclusion: 分支早退与几何模板验证是提升 YOLOv2 交通标志识别效率与精度的有效互补手段；其中推理阶段几何校验获得最高 mAP，几何特征训练变体在精度与时间上取得折中。自建融合数据集验证了该方案在十类交通标志任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) is an important perception task for autonomous driving and advanced driver-assistance systems, where a system must both localize traffic signs and determine their semantic classes efficiently. This work presents a TSR system based on YOLOv2 for simultaneous detection and classification. Two complementary modifications are studied. First, YOLOv2 is extended with intermediate prediction layers, forming a branched architecture that can terminate inference early for easy cases and reduce computation time. Both whole-image and cell-wise branching strategies are investigated. Second, geometric information is introduced to reduce classification errors between visually similar signs. An unsupervised Bayesian image-segmentation method produces binary representations that are compared with class-specific geometric templates inside YOLOv2 bounding boxes. This information is used either during inference or as an additional signal during training. A dedicated dataset is constructed by combining GTSDB and GTSRB samples using seamless cloning and controlled image transformations. Experiments cover ten traffic-sign classes, with 3,000 training and 300 test samples. The selected branched architecture reports 0.647 s runtime and 0.680 mAP, compared with 0.6607 s and 0.680 mAP for baseline YOLOv2. Geometric verification during inference increases mAP to 0.713, while the geometric-feature training variant achieves 0.697 mAP with a reported runtime of 0.6608 s.

</details>


### [73] [OmniVBench: A Benchmark and Large-Scale Dataset for Omni Reference-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22069)
*Wenxue Li,Peiyan Guan,Haoyang Jiang,Junxian Cai,Hualuo Liu,Chunjie Zhang,Chong Guan,Kai Huang,Songlian Li,Taiyi Wu,Yongjian Yu,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Yu Liu,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniVBench 基准与 Omni-R2V Dataset 数据集，用于评估和训练面向"全能参考到视频生成（omni R2V）"的模型。基准涵盖 7 类任务族、18 项细粒度任务，并引入基于因子（factor-grounded）的评估协议，含 12,172 个针对具体样例的检查项；数据集由专业视频素材构建，含 340K 训练样本，支持多样参考类型与多参考组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考到视频（R2V）生成正朝更通用、更灵活的参考控制发展，形成 omni R2V 新范式。但现有基准存在两方面不足：一是测试用例覆盖的参考类型与组合有限；二是评估协议多聚焦整体参考一致性，忽视参考因子是否被正确保持、解耦与路由（routing）。同时，omni R2V 训练数据构建成本高，导致合适训练资源稀缺。

Method: 1) 构建 OmniVBench：在参考类型、细粒度控制任务与参考组合上扩展评估范围，覆盖 7 个任务族、18 项细粒度任务，涉及内容、运动、风格、结构、叙事及多参考设定；2) 提出基于因子的评估（factor-grounded evaluation），包含 12,172 个样例级检查项，判断预期参考因子是否被忠实保持、是否正确解耦并绑定到目标、是否按指令正确实现；3) 构建 Omni-R2V Dataset：以大规模专业视频语料为主，含 340K 处理后训练样本，覆盖多样参考类型与多参考组合；4) 设计任务专用流水线进行"参考-目标"配对构建，提供可扩展的数据构建方案。

Result: 对先进的开源与闭源 R2V 模型进行大规模评估，在 OmniVBench 上揭示出跨任务族与评估维度的明显性能差距，凸显当前 R2V 模型仍存在的局限。

Conclusion: 本文通过 OmniVBench 与 Omni-R2V Dataset 弥补了 omni R2V 评测与训练资源缺口：前者提供覆盖广泛参考类型与细粒度任务、且基于因子的细粒度评估体系；后者提供工业级、可扩展构建的训练资源。二者共同为 omni R2V 研究与模型改进提供基础设施，并表明现有模型在该范式下仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Reference-to-video (R2V) generation is evolving toward increasingly general and versatile reference control, giving rise to the emerging paradigm of omni R2V generation. However, existing benchmarks fall short of these emerging capabilities: their test cases cover limited reference types and compositions, and their evaluation protocols largely assess holistic reference consistency, overlooking whether reference factors are properly preserved, disentangled, and routed. Meanwhile, the high cost of constructing omni R2V training data makes suitable training resources scarce. To address these gaps, we introduce OmniVBench and the Omni-R2V Dataset for evaluating and training omni R2V models. OmniVBench expands R2V evaluation across broader reference types, fine-grained control tasks, and richer reference compositions, covering 7 task families and 18 fine-grained tasks spanning content, motion, style, structure, narrative, and multi-reference settings. We introduce factor-grounded evaluation with 12,172 case-specific checklist items, assessing whether intended reference factors are faithfully preserved, correctly disentangled and bound to their targets, and properly realized according to the instruction. We further introduce the Omni-R2V Dataset, bringing industrial-grade training resources for diverse R2V tasks to the broader research community. Drawing primarily on a large-scale corpus of professional video footage, it comprises 340K processed training samples spanning diverse reference types and multi-reference compositions. We develop task-specific pipelines for reference-target pair construction, offering a practical and scalable recipe for omni R2V data construction. Extensive evaluation of advanced open- and closed-source R2V models reveals clear performance gaps across task families and evaluation dimensions on OmniVBench, highlighting remaining limitations of current R2V models.

</details>


### [74] [MintAct: A Unified Visual Agent for Digital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22083)
*Mingfei Gao,Rui Tian,Haiming Gang,Bohan Zhai,Le Zhang,Yuanzheng Gong,Di Feng,Ege Özsoy,Kaixin Ma,Vishwesh Kirthivasan,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Anders Boesen Lindbo Larsen,Afshin Dehghan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MintAct是一系列2B/4B/8B规模的视觉语言模型，统一了UI定位、跨移动端/桌面/Web的多步导航与视觉工具使用，并通过可扩展环境与异步强化学习基础设施，在同等模型规模下达到领域专用模型水平并取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UI智能体多依赖领域专用模型，难以统一覆盖UI定位、多步导航和视觉工具使用等多类能力；同时，跨域训练所需的环境异构、反馈噪声和off-policy漂移，使可扩展的数据采集与在线RL训练面临挑战。

Method: 围绕环境、数据和训练配方进行设计：构建可承载数百个并发实例的异构领域后端环境，同时支持轨迹数据采集与在线RL；采用异步RL训练框架，显式控制跨域训练分布，并在噪声环境反馈和off-policy漂移下保持稳定；在2B、4B、8B三个规模上训练模型。

Result: MintAct在UI定位、移动端/桌面/Web多步导航和视觉工具使用等广泛基准上，与各领域专用模型性能相当；在OSWorld-Verified上达到48.9，并在可比模型规模下取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 统一视觉语言模型结合可扩展环境与异步RL基础设施，能够有效整合多域UI能力，为通用GUI智能体提供可扩展的训练范式。

Abstract: We present MintAct, a family of vision-language models that unifies UI grounding, multi-step navigation across mobile, desktop, and web, and visual tool use, trained at 2B, 4B, and 8B scales. Through careful design of our environments, data, and training recipes, MintAct models match the performance of per-domain specialists across all of these capabilities. To enable this, we develop a scalable environment and reinforcement learning (RL) infrastructure. On the environment side, we host hundreds of concurrent instances across heterogeneous per-domain backends, serving both trajectory data collection and online RL. To enable efficient and scalable RL training, an asynchronous framework keeps explicit control over the cross-domain training distribution and remains stable under noisy environment feedback and off-policy drift. Experimental results show that MintAct achieves state-of-the-art performance (48.9 on OSWorld-Verified) across a wide range of benchmarks at comparable model sizes.

</details>


### [75] [Training-Adaptive Convolutional Sparse Coding via Information Bottleneck for Robust Visual Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19122)
*Meng'en Qin,Yinchen Liu,Mingxuan Cui,Youlu Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练自适应的卷积稀疏编码框架：将 CSC 的 FISTA 展开，并把稀疏系数作为可微变量与网络参数联合学习；从信息瓶颈视角解释其控制信息保留与压缩的权衡，并引入无标签后训练策略在固定主干参数下调整压缩强度，以提升扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉信号需要紧凑而充分的表示以支撑鲁棒下游预测。卷积稀疏编码（CSC）能显式抑制冗余并保留信号内容，但其稀疏系数通常固定且需人工选择，难以适应训练与不同输入条件。

Method: 将 CSC 优化用 FISTA 展开为网络，把稀疏系数设为可微变量，与网络参数联合学习。基于信息瓶颈框架，稀疏项促进表示压缩，重构项与任务损失共同保留任务相关信息。进一步提出无标签后训练策略：在主体网络参数固定时，针对受损输入调整压缩强度。

Result: 在 CIFAR 和 ImageNet 上，方法在干净数据识别上具有竞争力，并在多种输入扰动下显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 可学习的稀疏系数使 CSC 表示在压缩与任务信息保留之间实现训练自适应权衡，结合无标签后训练可有效增强视觉表示对输入扰动的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visual signals require compact yet sufficient representations for robust downstream prediction. Convolutional sparse coding (CSC) provides an explicit mechanism for suppressing redundant components while preserving signal content, but its sparsity coefficient is typically fixed and manually selected. We propose a training-adaptive convolutional sparse coding framework for robust visual signal representation. Specifically, we unfold the CSC optimization with the Fast Iterative Shrinkage-Thresholding Algorithm (FISTA) and treat the sparsity coefficient as a differentiable variable jointly learned with the network parameters. From the information bottleneck perspective, this coefficient controls the trade-off between information retention and compression: the sparsity term promotes compact representations, while the reconstruction term together with task loss preserves task-relevant signal content. We further introduce a label-free post-training strategy that adjusts the compression strength for corrupted inputs with the main network parameters fixed. Experiments on CIFAR and ImageNet demonstrate competitive clean-data recognition and greatly improved robustness under different input perturbations.

</details>


### [76] [AnyviewMeter: Adapting Robotic Reward Models with Camera Geometry and Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20106)
*Yuang Tu,Runjia Tan,Yujie Yan,Jinghan Hu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnyviewMeter：一种几何条件化的机器人奖励模型适配框架，将相机几何与多视角同步信息注入预训练奖励模型，以提升任务进度标量奖励预测在视角变化与遮挡下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型从视觉观测评估任务执行，但其预测会随相机视角和遮挡变化而改变，即使任务状态未变。将预训练奖励模型适配到局部任务时，必须考虑该任务如何被观测，因此需要引入相机几何与多视角联合证据。

Method: AnyviewMeter 以标量奖励表示任务进度，结合低秩微调、token 对齐的 Plücker 射线与同步块注意力。射线条件化将相机几何注入视觉特征以及注意力的 query 和 key；块注意力在预训练解码器内融合同步视角。框架通过参数高效适配预训练 Robometer 模型，支持单视角奖励预测与联合多视角评估。

Result: 在 PickCube 上，单视角适配在每个相机组中均改善进度预测，并在视野变化下相对 RGB 微调将平均绝对误差降低约 21%。在模拟操作任务中，联合多视角预测相对平均单视角 RGB 预测将进度误差降低 41–69%，并在约 88% 的任务-相机组中改善时序排序。在固定相机与腕部相机的真实任务中，平均绝对误差相对平均 RGB 微调降低约 21%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，相机几何与联合视觉证据是任务特定机器人奖励适配的有效组成部分，可提升奖励模型在视角变化和多视角设置下的进度预测准确性与时序一致性。

Abstract: Robotic reward models evaluate task execution from visual observations, but their predictions can change with camera viewpoint and occlusion even when the underlying task state is unchanged. Adapting a pretrained reward model to a local task therefore requires accounting for how that task is observed. We introduce AnyviewMeter, a geometry-conditioned adaptation framework for robotic reward models that represent task progress as a scalar reward signal. It combines low-rank fine-tuning with token-aligned Plucker rays and synchronous block attention: ray conditioning incorporates camera geometry into visual features and attention queries and keys, while block attention fuses synchronized views inside the pretrained decoder. The framework supports both single-view reward prediction and joint multi-view evaluation through parameter-efficient adaptation of a pretrained Robometer model. On PickCube, single-view adaptation improves progress prediction in every camera group and reduces mean absolute error under a changed field of view by approximately 21% relative to RGB fine-tuning. Across simulated manipulation tasks, joint multi-view prediction reduces progress error by 41-69% compared with averaging single-view RGB predictions and improves temporal ordering in approximately 88% of task-camera groups. On real tasks with fixed and wrist-mounted cameras, mean absolute error decreases by approximately 21% relative to averaged RGB fine-tuning. These results support camera geometry and joint visual evidence as useful components of task-specific robotic reward adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的稀疏预填充方法 RBS-Attention，通过“质心基分支 + 救援分支”的双分支块选择缓解均值稀释问题，在 H100 上实现最高 20.65× 预填充注意力加速、11.92× vLLM 预填充注意力加速和 5.97× 128K 端到端首 token 加速，同时保持接近稠密注意力的长上下文质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大模型推理受预填充阶段制约，稠密自注意力需在生成前处理整个提示。稀疏块选择虽可降本，但仅用块质心代表整块会掩盖块内高相关 token，作者将这一失效模式称为“均值稀释”（mean dilution）。

Method: RBS-Attention 是一种免训练稀疏预填充方法，包含两个互补选择分支：质心基分支捕获平均相关性；救援分支利用最大 key 块半径及其随提示、层、头变化的分布，识别可能被低估的块。两分支分别独立阈值化并组合掩码，以控制救援块贡献，同时保持规则块稀疏 FlashAttention 执行。

Result: 在 H100 GPU 上，RBS-Attention 于 128K、Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8 上实现 20.65× 独立预填充注意力加速、11.92× vLLM 预填充注意力加速和 5.97× 端到端首 token 时间加速。在稠密 Qwen3-32B 上，RULER 总体准确率为 88.65，对比稠密注意力 89.52；LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench 和 Video-MME 提供额外质量评估。支持性实验测量了实际保留率、在匹配密度下比较选择器，并刻画了块大小、阈值和内存行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，半径自适应双分支选择是长上下文预填充的有效方案：在显著降低预填充开销的同时，质量损失较小，兼顾效率与长上下文任务表现。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [78] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出注意力感知路由（AAR），利用滑动窗口注意力权重的时序与频谱特征增强路由器，在冻结主干、仅训练路由参数的前提下提升推理性能，并揭示路由与注意力的耦合回路及深度敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE语言模型中，路由器通常仅依据token的隐状态选择并加权专家，所利用的上下文信息有限，未能充分反映模型的整体上下文状态。

Method: 提出AAR：从代表模型上下文状态摘要的注意力权重滑动窗口中提取时序与频谱特征，用以增强路由器；这些特征与隐状态解耦。训练时完全冻结基础Transformer，仅更新路由参数，从而将路由作为唯一变量进行隔离研究。

Result: 在OLMoE上，AAR相比仅路由的SFT基线在GSM8K上提升+3.37个百分点。路由与注意力构成耦合回路：第l层的路由变化经残差流传播，放大第l+1层的注意力汇（attention sink），在未直接更新注意力机制的情况下重塑注意力。AAR还减少了长程发散生成——错误答案变短，而正确答案长度不变。此外AAR具有强深度敏感性：不加区分地跨层应用会损害事实检索，而数学推理收益在网络较深层引入时得以保持。

Conclusion: 路由与注意力构成耦合回路，且存在跨深度的“检索—推理”张力，使逐层选择性的AAR成为探测不同层注意力所携带路由相关信息的可控探针。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [79] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaLR：将推理重构为受约束的隐空间优化，结合因果拓扑矩阵与隐式微分进行梯度引导的"思维修正"，在并行生成中实现中间步骤的动态自纠错，取得扩散语言模型SOTA性能并优于强自回归基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归（AR）模型存在局部贪心问题，而扩散语言模型（DLM）往往缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构。作者希望融合二者优势、克服各自缺陷。

Method: 提出Causal Latent Revision（CaLR）框架，把推理形式化为受约束的隐空间优化。方法采用来自专家模型的因果拓扑矩阵（CTM）以及隐式微分，执行梯度引导的"思维修正"以强制逻辑一致性，从而在并行生成过程中对中间步骤进行动态自纠错。

Result: 在复杂基准上取得扩散语言模型的SOTA性能，超越强自回归基线，并在Sudoku等受约束任务中展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaLR通过因果结构约束与隐空间优化的结合，有效弥补AR的局部贪心与DLM因果结构不足，验证了兼顾并行生成与逻辑一致性的推理范式潜力。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [80] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将DINOv3 ViT以三阶段参数高效框架适配到合成孔径声呐自动目标识别（SAS ATR），结果显示仅LoRA适配阶段带来主要增益，后续硬负样本挖掘与监督对比学习相对匹配对照均无显著提升，说明单一高效适配已足够，堆叠式精修不必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAS ATR对先进海军能力具有支撑作用，但深度学习应用受限于目标图像稀缺、背景杂波强以及需要人在回路评估。作者希望利用自然图像预训练的DINOv3 ViT，以参数高效方式弥合自然图像预训练与水下声学传播之间的域差距。

Method: 提出三阶段参数高效适配框架：阶段1冻结ViT主干，仅用LoRA适配；阶段2用硬负样本挖掘增强对岩石、沉积物等类人造目标声学仿目标的决策边界；阶段3用监督对比学习（SupCon）分离目标与杂波表征。评估使用海上SAS数据，采用任务级地理划分，在85%测试召回率下比较各实验分支，并以3个随机种子重复。

Result: LoRA是主要效应：在相同冻结主干下，AUPRC从0.300提升至0.679±0.027；秩4即可达到该结果，且仅训练0.26%权重。两个精修阶段均未超过匹配对照：硬负样本挖掘相对等量随机课程使AUPRC变化-0.0045±0.0119；SupCon相对前一阶段变化+0.0002±0.0096。

Conclusion: 零结果说明负样本挖掘作用于编码器已经拟合的数据，监督阶段也已施加了大部分目标—杂波几何结构。因此，一个高效适配阶段已足够，堆叠式精修并不能带来额外收益。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [81] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作从注意力图的信息流拓扑出发，利用Forman-Ricci曲率刻画注意力图中的信息瓶颈，并融合注意力头的半局部与全局信息流特征，实现单次前向传播的幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法多依赖注意力权重或多响应对比，难以从信息流结构层面揭示幻觉产生的机制；作者希望区分幻觉与非幻觉响应背后的注意力拓扑差异。

Method: 分析注意力图的Forman-Ricci曲率以识别信息瓶颈；提出同时捕获幻觉相关注意力头的半局部与全局信息流特征的方法；在多个LLM和基准上进行评估，并与基于注意力及多响应基线比较。

Result: 所提单次前向方法在两个幻觉检测基准上稳定优于现有基于注意力和多响应基线，并在多种LLM架构上取得有竞争力的性能；分析表明因果生成中token间上下文共享受损与幻觉强相关。

Conclusion: 幻觉响应常表现为过度依赖自注意力、从早期token扩散式检索上下文或信息过度压缩，尤其在最终Transformer层；注意力图拓扑可作为检测和解释LLM幻觉的有效信号。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [82] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究微调如何重塑LLM的内部表征，发现EAP识别的关键组件集中于特定层（功能局部化），但其分布与表征变化最大的层基本不相关；跨任务组件重叠并不带来性能迁移，任务性质不同时反而可能导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为适配LLM到各类下游任务的常用方法，但其如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏理解。作者希望探究微调对内部表征（注意力模式、逐层激活）的影响，并检验这些变化是否与驱动任务性能的关键组件（由EAP识别）相关联。

Method: 作者考察微调如何改变LLM的内部表征，包括注意力模式和逐层激活，并将其与EAP（Edge/Activation Patching类方法）识别的任务相关组件（如注意力头和logit层激活）进行对照分析，同时比较不同任务间EAP组件分布及跨任务性能迁移情况。

Result: EAP识别的组件集中在特定层，表明模型内部化任务行为存在一定程度的功能局部化；这些组件的跨层分布与微调中发生最大表征变化的层基本不相关；跨任务EAP组件重叠并不能转化为跨任务性能迁移（尤其当任务性质不同，如分类 vs 生成时）；更具体地，当一个任务上微调导致与另一任务EAP组件高度重叠时，会引发另一任务性能下降。

Conclusion: 微调引起的最显著表征变化与任务关键组件的分布并不一致，说明表征变化程度不等同于功能重要性；此外，EAP组件的重叠可能带来负迁移，提示多任务适配中需警惕任务间干扰。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [83] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TinyCeNN-LM 提出一种质量门控的预训练语言模型注意力替换/结构转换框架，通过表示保真度与 NLL 双阈值决定逐层接受或回滚，在 SmolLM2-135M 与 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上实现保守可验证的转换，并控制困惑度与缓存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预训练语言模型中替换注意力机制本质上是兼容性问题：看似合理的替代层可能改变后续层所依赖的表示，导致模型行为失配。因此，论文不追求通用注意力替换或速度提升，而是研究如何安全、可验证地进行结构转换。

Method: 提出 TinyCeNN-LM 的“质量门控后训练转换”框架，采用受 CeNN 启发的细胞循环层，具备有界局部处理、紧凑循环记忆、路由、融合以及“接受或回滚”验证机制。研究三种实现：Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion、PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32。其中严格 PDelta3 转换仅在表示与 NLL 指标均通过固定阈值时接受该层。

Result: 在 SmolLM2-135M 上，第 0–2 层被接受，累计 ΔNLL=+0.01209；第 3 层虽 NLL 可接受，但因表示保真度不达标被拒绝。在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上，全注意力层 3、7、11 被接受，最终 ΔNLL=+0.02073。Integrated Memory 将困惑度控制在 -0.07% 至 +0.93% 范围内，总缓存最多减少 6.01%。对转换后的 Qwen 版本进行 200 条下游抽样健全性检查，总体准确率为 28.5%–32.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持保守的、质量门控的结构转换，而非普适的注意力替换或速度提升；逐层验证与回滚机制对于维持预训练模型兼容性至关重要。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [84] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 InterviewPlayground：一个以临床医生为依据、基于记忆增强患者模拟器的开放式 AI 精神科接诊评估平台；在 6 名临床医生、25 分钟的试点中，GPT 类 LLM 接诊访谈者比临床医生恢复更多临床相关条目（88.0% vs. 38.9%），但产生更多无访谈依据的临床推断（56.8% vs. 27.8%），且更少刻画已识别的安全关切（33.3% vs. 66.7%），为部署该任务的质控评估奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助精神科接诊系统在临床使用前，卫生系统需要可常规执行、可操作的质控评估方法，以对照其临床标准评价工具。由于临床医生接诊风格不同，评估需满足三点：(1) 支持跨访谈方法比较；(2) 最小化临床医生负担；(3) 测量与卫生系统部署相关的临床性能。

Method: 构建以临床医生为依据的评估平台，核心为面向开放式 AI 访谈的记忆增强患者模拟器 InterviewPlayground。使用专家撰写的临床情景（vignettes）创建交互式患者，搭建模拟接诊平台，并设计接诊相关评估维度。开展试点：6 名临床医生进行 25 分钟评估，与基于 GPT 的 LLM 接诊访谈者对比。

Result: LLM 接诊访谈者恢复患者情景中嵌入的临床相关条目比例更高（88.0% vs. 38.9%）；但产生更多不基于访谈的临床推断（56.8% vs. 27.8%）；对已识别的安全关切的刻画更少（33.3% vs. 66.7%）。

Conclusion: 该平台为 AI 精神科接诊任务的部署质控评估奠定基础。结果提示：LLM 在临床相关信息采集覆盖面上有优势，但在无依据临床推断和安全关切处理方面存在不足；后续需扩大样本并完善评估，以支持常规质量保证。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake. In a pilot of 6 clinicians in a 25-minute assessment compared to a GPT-based LLM intake interviewer, the LLM recovered more of the clinically relevant items embedded in the patient vignettes (88.0% vs. 38.9%), but made more clinical inferences not based on the interview (56.8% vs. 27.8%), and characterized identified safety concerns less often (33.3% vs. 66.7%), setting the stage for deployed quality assurance for this task.

</details>


### [85] [Can Agents Design Better Chips with a Higher Level Abstraction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21157)
*Zijian Ding,Yang Zou,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨LLM智能体是否可通过更高层次抽象来设计芯片，比较了直接RTL设计、基于智能体的HLS设计、编译后HLS优化、HLS后RTL优化四种流程，并提出将“基于智能体的HLS设计”与“HLS后RTL优化”结合的AHRR工作流。在11个任务的基准上，AHRR相对直接RTL设计取得2.6倍几何平均加速，表明HLS抽象与RTL精修互补，是智能体芯片设计的有前景方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体用于芯片设计时多直接在RTL层操作，作者希望验证：利用更高层次的抽象（如HLS）能否让智能体设计出更优芯片。为此需要系统比较不同设计抽象层级与优化阶段的组合效果，并以易部署的FPGA作为端到端评估平台，同时指出所研究的设计流程权衡大体与目标工艺无关。

Method: 对比四类设计流程：Direct RTL Design、Agent-based HLS Design、Post-Compiler HLS Refinement、Post-HLS RTL Refinement；并将Agent-based HLS Design与Post-HLS RTL Refinement组合为AHRR（Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement）。在包含11个任务的多样化基准套件上进行端到端评估，以FPGA为实现平台。

Result: AHRR在11任务基准套件上相对Direct RTL Design取得2.6倍几何平均加速。案例研究表明：HLS能够将设计知识提炼为智能体可利用的抽象，而RTL精修可恢复低层次优化机会，两者结合形成互补优势。

Conclusion: AHRR是一种有前景的智能体芯片设计工作流：通过HLS高层抽象提升智能体设计能力，再以RTL精修弥补低层优化空间，从而优于直接RTL设计。代码与评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for chip design, but most existing approaches operate directly at RTL. We ask whether agents can design better chips by leveraging higher-level abstractions. We compare Direct RTL Design, Agent-based HLS Design, Post-Compiler HLS Refinement, and Post-HLS RTL Refinement, and combine Agent-based HLS Design with Post-HLS RTL Refinement as Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement (AHRR). We use FPGAs as a practical, easy-to-deploy platform for end-to-end evaluation, but note that the design-flow tradeoffs we study are largely independent of the target technology. Across a diverse 11-tasks benchmark suite, AHRR achieves a 2.6$\times$ geometric-mean speedup over Direct RTL Design across our benchmark suite. Case studies show that HLS distills design knowledge into abstractions that agents can leverage, while RTL refinement recovers lower-level optimization opportunities. Together, these results make AHRR a promising workflow for agentic chip design. The code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/ZijD/AHRR.

</details>


### [86] [SpecOpt: Contact-Diff Reasoning for Agentic Molecule Optimization Toward Binding Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21165)
*Thao Nguyen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"特异性优化"(SpecOpt)这一新的分子设计任务，即对已有药物进行受限结构修饰，在保持结构相似性与成药性的前提下提高其对目标靶标相对已知脱靶的结合偏好；作者构建了基于ChEMBL的基准数据集，并开发了一个结合对接、残基感知接触比较与大语言模型提议修改的智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脱靶蛋白结合是小分子药物产生不良反应的主要来源，但现有基于结构的分子设计方法大多专注于从头生成选择性化合物，而非改善已有、特征明确的药物的选择性。因此需要一个专门针对已有化合物提升选择性的设计任务与系统评估方法。

Method: 1) 定义SpecOpt任务：对已有化合物进行受限结构修饰，提高其对目标靶标相对脱靶的结合偏好，同时保持结构一致性和成药性。2) 构建基准数据集：从ChEMBL化合物-靶标相互作用数据出发，通过整理的药物机制注释确定目标靶标，通过实测活性确定脱靶。3) 开发智能体框架：将化合物分别与目标靶标和脱靶对接，通过残基感知的原子-蛋白接触比较对接姿态，并将差异相互作用提供给大语言模型以提出针对性结构修饰。4) 候选分子仅在满足分子相似性、ADMET及目标-脱靶对接选择性标准时保留。5) 通过消融实验分析关键优化信号。

Result: 在915个化合物上，该智能体为84.8%的化合物改善了目标-脱靶结合差距，使平均差距从-0.72 kcal/mol提升至+0.47 kcal/mol，同时与起始化合物的平均Tanimoto相似度保持在0.72。消融实验表明，残基特异性接触信息是关键优化信号：将残基身份替换为二值接触指示符后，全部29个消融化合物的改善均消失。

Conclusion: 研究确立了SpecOpt为一个独立的分子设计问题，并证明残基感知的差异相互作用是提升已有化合物特异性的有效信号。

Abstract: Off-target protein binding is a major source of adverse effects for small-molecule drugs, yet most structure-based molecular design methods focus on generating selective compounds de novo rather than improving the selectivity of existing, well- characterized drugs. We introduce specificity optimization (SpecOpt), a molecular design task that seeks constrained structural modifications to an existing compound that increase its binding preference for an intended target over known off-targets while preserving its structural identity and drug-like properties. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct a ChEMBL-derived benchmark from compound-target interaction data, identifying intended targets through curated drug-mechanism annotations and off- targets through measured activities. We then develop an agentic framework that docks each compound against its intended target and off-targets, compares the resulting poses through residue-aware atom-protein contacts, and provides these differential interactions to a large language model to propose targeted structural modifications. Candidates are retained only if they satisfy molecular similarity, ADMET, and target-off-target docking selectivity criteria. On 915 compounds, the agent improves the target- off-target binding gap for 84.8% of compounds, shifting the mean gap from -0.72 to +0.47 kcal/mol while maintaining a mean Tanimoto similarity of 0.72 to the starting compounds. Ablation studies identify residue-specific contact information as the critical optimization signal: replacing residue identities with binary contact indicators eliminates improvement on all 29 ablation compounds. These results establish SpecOpt as a distinct molecular design problem and demonstrate residue-aware differential interactions as an effective signal for improving the specificity of existing compounds.

</details>


### [87] [Implicit Rule Induction with Test-Time Task Embeddings in ARC-like Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21181)
*Adrien Deliège,Claas Beger,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Melanie Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究 VARC 中的测试时任务嵌入，提出两步测试时训练（TTT）协议：先仅微调任务嵌入（Embed-TTT），再冻结嵌入并微调骨干网络。结果表明 Embed-TTT 能获得更优任务嵌入，使其更对齐底层规则，并揭示 ARC 类任务中规则归纳与规则执行的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC 及相关基准用于评估 AI 能否解决新颖推理任务，但往往无法判断成功是来自对预期底层规则的推断，还是依赖捷径。因此需要更清晰地区分规则归纳与规则执行，并设计能区分分布内与分布外规则的评测基准。

Method: 在 Vision ARC（VARC）中，模型由预训练骨干网络和表示变换规则的可训练嵌入组成。原始 VARC 对骨干和任务嵌入联合进行测试时训练。本文提出两步 TTT：第一步仅微调任务嵌入（Embed-TTT）；第二步冻结该嵌入，仅微调骨干网络。在 ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC 以及两个具有已知规则的受控数据集上，结合嵌入检索、线性探测、定性关系分析、参数比例分析以及规则几何结构与组合泛化实验进行评估。

Result: Embed-TTT 一致地改善任务嵌入：更对齐底层任务规则，提升基于嵌入的检索，并支持对已知规则进行准确线性探测；在 ARC-AGI-1 上能识别更多语义有意义的测试-训练任务关系。仅优化任务嵌入（不足模型参数的 0.01%）已能解决 ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC 和 Mini-ARC 中非平凡比例的任务，完整两步流程进一步提升最终性能。Embed-TTT 还能恢复参数化规则的底层几何结构，并学习支持规则级插值的组合能力，但不支持外推。

Conclusion: 研究支持在 ARC 类评测中更清晰地区分规则归纳与规则执行，并强调需要构建更好区分分布内与分布外规则的基准。两步 TTT 与 Embed-TTT 为诊断任务嵌入和规则学习提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus and related benchmarks evaluate whether AI models can solve novel reasoning tasks, but often leave unclear whether success reflects inference of the intended underlying rule or reliance on shortcuts. We address this gap by studying test-time task embeddings in Vision ARC (VARC), a model in which a pre-trained backbone is complemented by a trainable embedding representing the transformation rule. In the original VARC, test-time training (TTT) is jointly applied to the backbone and task embedding. Here we introduce a novel two-step TTT protocol: first finetune only the task embedding (Embed-TTT), then freeze it and finetune the backbone. Across ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and two controlled datasets with known rules, Embed-TTT consistently yields improved task embeddings, ones that align better with underlying task rules, improve embedding-based retrieval, and enable accurate linear probing of known rules. Qualitatively, Embed-TTT identifies more semantically meaningful relations between test and train tasks on ARC-AGI-1. We also show that optimizing only task embeddings (less than 0.01% of model parameters) already solves a non-trivial fraction of ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and Mini-ARC tasks, while the full two-step pipeline improves final performance. Finally, we show that Embed-TTT recovers the underlying geometric structure of parametric rules and learns compositional capabilities that enable rule-wise interpolation, but not extrapolation. These findings support a clearer separation between rule induction and rule execution in ARC-like evaluations, motivating benchmarks that better distinguish in-distribution from out-of-distribution rules.

</details>


### [88] [AI-GRACE: A Use-Case Operationalization Framework for Agentic AI: From Organizational Objectives and Obligations to Deployment Capabilities and Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21192)
*John Cuneo,David Chun,Gaurav Khanna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AI-GRACE（Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence）框架，将组织治理与代理式 AI 的技术实现连接起来，用于按用例确定需验证、控制与观测的内容，并以零售银行虚构案例演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署代理式 AI 的组织不能只判断模型是否可信，还需明确特定用例要交付预期成果并满足合规义务时，应验证、控制和观测什么。现有治理与技术实施之间缺乏可操作、可追溯的衔接。

Method: 采用设计科学（design science）构建方法贡献，结合情境化方法工程（situational method engineering）支持上下文裁剪与复用；基于专业观察并对标准与文献进行目的性综合。框架先确立目标与义务，再在七个风险域（含使命与价值实现）评估风险，推导部署前保证、运行时控制与证据要求，进而指导能力资格认定、差距评估和逻辑架构。提出 Agent Operating Envelope（规定允许动作与升级条件）和 Risk-Aligned Independence Levels（RAIL，概括授权自主性）。以虚构零售银行应用示例说明。

Result: 形成 AI-GRACE 用例操作化框架，输出可追溯依据，帮助组织判断必须实施什么、已支持什么、仍有哪些未解决问题；并通过虚构零售银行案例展示其应用方式。

Conclusion: 该框架为代理式 AI 的治理、风险、保证、控制与证据提供结构化、可追溯的操作路径。其是否改善部署决策、效率与复用仍需实证评估。

Abstract: Organizations deploying agentic artificial intelligence must determine more than whether a model is trustworthy; they must establish what to validate, control, and observe for a use case to deliver its intended outcome while meeting applicable obligations. This paper proposes AI-GRACE (Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence) as a use-case operationalization framework connecting organizational governance with technical implementation. The proposal draws on professional observations and a purposive synthesis of standards and literature, using design science to frame the method contribution and situational method engineering to guide contextual tailoring and reuse. The framework establishes objectives and obligations and then assesses risks in seven proposed domains, including mission and value realization. It derives requirements for assurance before deployment, runtime controls, and evidence, which guide capability qualification, gap assessment, and a logical architecture. An Agent Operating Envelope specifies permitted actions and escalation conditions, while Risk-Aligned Independence Levels (RAIL) summarize the authorized independence. A fictional retail banking application illustrates the method. The contribution is a traceable basis for deciding what an organization must implement, what it already supports, and what remains unresolved. Empirical evaluation must establish whether it improves deployment decisions, efficiency, and reuse.

</details>


### [89] [Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21208)
*Ana Nunez,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoVer，一种单策略 GRPO 框架，通过信息增益奖励与三阶段多样性筛选，解决自博弈代码生成中测试"宽松化崩溃"与"集中偏差"两大问题，在五个基准上显著提升 pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈方法让单一语言模型同时充当代码编写者与测试作者，有望使代码生成的强化学习摆脱固定测试集。但其存在两个相互耦合的病态问题：（1）宽松化崩溃（permissiveness collapse）——pass-rate 奖励会被平凡、无区分度的测试所最大化；（2）集中偏差（concentration bias）——i.i.d. 采样的测试聚集在众数输入上，抬高了估计器方差。本文旨在同时解决这两种失效模式。

Method: 提出 CoVer（Co-trained Coder and Verifier），一个单策略 GRPO 框架。其一，采用信息增益（IG）奖励：以自生成测试的通过/失败向量与"分级、锚定真值的正确性信号 y∈[0,1]^m"之间的互信息来评分每个测试，并由二者协方差符号进行门控，使仅具正向区分度的测试获得奖励。其二，设计三阶段多样性感知筛选：通过无效性过滤、输入字符串过滤、执行剖面过滤，将候选池修剪为行为上非冗余的测试套件，从而在固定执行预算下提升 IG 估计器的有效样本量。

Result: 在五个基准（LiveBench、MBPP、LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、CodeForces）上，CoVer 相较 Qwen2.5-Instruct 骨干，在 7B 规模上提升 one-shot pass@1 达 +5.8 分，在 14B 规模上提升 +7.1 分，并在两种规模下均取得所有对比方法中最高的宏平均成绩。作为 CodeT 排序流水线中的即插即用骨干，CoVer-7B 额外提升 +3.5 分。

Conclusion: CoVer 通过信息增益奖励与多样性感知筛选有效缓解了宽松化崩溃与集中偏差，显著提升代码生成性能；其作为排序骨干还能带来额外增益，证明协同训练对生成与选择具有双重收益。

Abstract: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.

</details>


### [90] [Ability-Residual Decoupled Modeling for Affective Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21214)
*Boyuan Zhao,Meng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出能力残差解耦框架用于情感认知诊断：显式建模学生、题目、概念、学生-概念及低秩学生-题目等未建模认知残差，避免残差泄漏到情感表征造成“情感污染”，再用情感模块调制猜测/失误效应，并用Q矩阵约束的概念残差注意力聚合与题目相关的概念残差。在四个数据集与六种认知诊断主干上验证了响应预测提升与情感对齐改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断仅从作答日志推断概念掌握，但作答还受情绪、投入度、疲劳等非认知因素影响。情感认知诊断试图引入情感状态，但既有方法常假设主干已解释能力、题目与概念效应，剩余误差主要归因于情感。作者指出该假设在真实教育数据中不充分：题目校准偏差、系统性概念偏差、个性化学生-概念偏差以及潜在学生-题目匹配会形成稳定认知残差；若不显式建模，这些残差会泄漏进情感表示，导致情感污染。

Method: 提出能力残差解耦框架。模型先通过学生、题目、概念、学生-概念以及低秩学生-题目组件捕获未建模的认知残差，再利用情感模块调制猜测/失误效应；同时设计受Q矩阵约束的概念残差注意力机制，自适应聚合仅与题目相关的概念残差。

Result: 在ASSIST2017、ASSIST2012、ASSIST2009和Junyi四个数据集上，结合六种认知诊断主干进行实验，报告的比较中响应预测取得增益；在有情感标签时情感对齐总体改善。消融实验、泄漏探测、主成分分析可视化、长尾分析和案例研究进一步表明：能力残差吸收稳定认知偏差，减少情感分支中的认知污染，并提升认知诊断模型的鲁棒性与预测精度。

Conclusion: 显式建模并解耦能力残差，可缓解情感认知诊断中的情感污染问题，使情感分支更纯净，同时提升响应预测准确性与模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis infers students' concept mastery from response logs. However, students' responses are not determined by mastery alone: non-cognitive factors such as emotion, engagement, and fatigue can also affect performance. Affective cognitive diagnosis therefore extends conventional cognitive diagnosis by incorporating affective states. Existing methods often assume that the cognitive diagnosis backbone has already explained ability, item, and concept effects, so the remaining errors can be attributed mainly to affect. We argue that this assumption can be insufficient in real educational data: item calibration bias, systematic concept bias, personalized student-concept deviations, and latent student-item matching can form stable cognitive residuals. Without an explicit modeling pathway, these residuals may leak into affective representations, producing affect contamination. To address this problem, we propose an ability-residual decoupled framework for affective cognitive diagnosis. The model first captures unmodeled cognitive residuals through student, item, concept, student-concept, and low-rank student-item components, and then uses an affective module to modulate guess/slip effects. A Q-matrix-constrained concept residual attention mechanism adaptively aggregates only item-relevant concept residuals. Experiments on ASSIST2017, ASSIST2012, ASSIST2009, and Junyi with six cognitive diagnosis backbones show response-prediction gains across the reported comparisons and generally improved affect alignment when affect labels are available. Ablation studies, leakage probes, principal component analysis visualization, long-tail analysis, and case studies further indicate that ability residuals absorb stable cognitive bias, reduce cognitive contamination in the affective branch, and enhance the robustness and predictive accuracy of cognitive diagnosis models.

</details>


### [91] [A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Framework for Perceptual Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21221)
*Hongyan Wei,Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可端到端微分的“神经-软符号”框架，将视觉感知与任务规划统一到同一计算图中，用连续软符号状态和可微 soft-T_P 转移算子优化动作序列，并让规划梯度反传以精炼感知表征；在 Blocksworld 基准上取得更高成功率且计算/时间开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知规划任务需要同时具备两项能力：准确感知不确定场景，以及按逻辑规则规划有效动作序列。传统方法通常先把感知结果离散化为符号事实再规划，这会丢弃感知不确定性，并切断任务级反馈对感知模块的反向影响。

Method: 提出通用的全可微神经-软符号框架，将视觉感知与任务规划置于单一计算图中。框架维护连续的软符号状态，把领域规则提升为可微的 soft-T_P 转移算子，并在短规划时域上优化动作 logits。规划目标产生的梯度还可更新感知参数，使与任务相关的感知表征在规划过程中得到精炼。

Result: 在 Blocksworld 上，方法解决 LatPlan-40 的 40/40 任务和 PlanBench-600 的 596/600 任务，对比 LatPlan 的 33/40 与推理模型基线的 587/600，同时所需计算量和时间显著更少。在感知不确定性消融中，成功率从冻结感知的 59% 提升到 83%。此外，在 Blocksworld 场景上开展任务与运动仿真，验证了解码任务计划与下游机器人运动执行之间的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过可微软符号规划实现了感知与规划的联合优化，既保留感知不确定性，又利用任务级梯度反馈提升感知质量；实验表明其在规划成功率、计算效率及执行级验证方面均优于或补充了现有方法。

Abstract: Perceptual planning tasks require two key capabilities: accurately perceiving uncertain scenes and planning valid action sequences following logical rules. Conventional methods convert perception into discrete symbolic facts and then plan, discarding perceptual uncertainty and severing task-level feedback to perception. We introduce a generic, fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic framework that connects visual perception and task planning within a single computational graph. The framework maintains a continuous soft symbolic state, lifts domain rules into a differentiable soft-$T_P$ transition operator, and optimizes action logits over a short planning horizon. Gradients from the planning objective can also update the perception parameters, allowing task-relevant perceptual representations to be refined during planning. On Blocksworld, our method solves 40/40 LatPlan-40 tasks and 596/600 PlanBench-600 tasks, compared with 33/40 for LatPlan and 587/600 for the reasoning-model baseline, while requiring substantially less computation and time. In the perceptual-uncertainty ablation, our method improves the success rate from 59\% with frozen perception to 83\%. We further conduct task-and-motion simulations on Blocksworld scenes, providing an execution-level validation of the compatibility between decoded task plans and downstream robotic motion execution.

</details>


### [92] [CogGym: Towards Large-Scale Comparative Evaluation of Human and Machine Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21259)
*Lance Ying,Jinzhou Wu,Yingshan Susan Wang,Shivam Aarya,Luca M. Schulze Buschoff,Harry Chen,Katherine M. Collins,Andrea de Varda,Shuhao Fu,Sean Dae Houlihan,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang,Samuel Kiegeland,Tetsu Kurumisawa,Rongzhi Liu,Ryan Liu,Ningshan Ma,Kathryn McGregor,Younes Strittmatter,Polina Tsvilodub,Jacob Hoover Vigly,Sarah Wu,Enjie Xu,Yiling Yun,Kelsey Allen,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Brian Christian,Evelina Fedorenko,Michael C. Frank,Michael Franke,Tao Gao,Samuel J. Gershman,Robert D. Hawkins,Jennifer Hu,Julian Jara-Ettinger,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Sydney Levine,Tal Linzen,Hongjing Lu,Timothy O'Donnell,Desmond C. Ong,Steven T. Piantadosi,Rebecca Saxe,Eric Schulz,Tianmin Shu,Felix A. Sosa,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tan Zhi-Xuan,Tomer Ullman,Fei Xu,Ilker Yildirim,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Thomas L. Griffiths,Tobias Gerstenberg,Kevin Smith,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CogGym 框架，将 258 个认知科学实验标准化为 EML，系统比较 50 个 LLM 与人类在常识推理上的行为；发现模型规模和新近度带来更好的人类判断拟合，但拟合度远低于人类信度，且常识推理进展慢于数学、编程等形式推理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 与认知科学共同致力于理解和建模人类智能，因此需要回答模型响应在多大程度上类似人类、又在哪些方面系统性偏离。然而，人类任务范围广、实验范式多样，现有比较难以做到可扩展、严格且可复现。

Method: CogGym 是一个基于认知科学的可扩展统一框架，采用半自动化、human-in-the-loop 流程，将多样实验范式标准化为任务无关的 Experiment Markup Language（EML），从而在匹配实验试次上进行大规模、可复现、忠实的比较。首版整理并标准化来自 100 篇论文的 258 个认知实验，聚焦人类常识推理，并评测 50 个大语言模型与人类响应的拟合。

Result: 发现明显的 scaling trend：更大、更新的 AI 模型能更好复现人类判断。但模型在常识推理任务上的提升显著慢于数学、编程等形式推理基准；模型-人类拟合仍远低于人类 split-half 信度（文本、图像、视频分别为 R²=0.93、0.95、0.92），最佳模型仅达到文本 R²=0.59、图像 R²=0.58、视频 R²=0.43。

Conclusion: CogGym 被定位为持续更新的 living evaluation framework，可不断纳入新的认知科学实验，用以刻画模型行为在何处类似人类、在何处系统性偏离，以及这些模式如何随模型与实验的发展而变化。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling human intelligence are parallel goals shared by artificial intelligence (AI) and cognitive science. As AI systems grow increasingly capable, in what ways do model responses resemble human responses, and where do they systematically diverge? The sheer breadth and diversity of the tasks humans can perform and think about pose a challenge for scalable and rigorous comparison between humans and models. We introduce CogGym, a scalable, unified framework grounded in cognitive science for systematically comparing model and human behavior on matched experimental trials. CogGym uses a semi-automated, human-in-the-loop pipeline to standardize diverse experimental paradigms into a task-agnostic Experiment Markup Language (EML), enabling reproducible and faithful comparison at scale. For initial release, we curate and standardize 258 cognitive experiments from 100 papers that focuses on human commonsense reasoning, and evaluate 50 large language models against human responses. We find a clear scaling trend where larger and more recent AI models better reproduce human judgments. Yet AI models' improvement on such common reasoning tasks is considerably slower than the gains observed on formal-reasoning benchmarks like math and coding, and model--human fit remains well below human splithalf reliability ($R^2 = 0.93$ on text, $0.95$ on image, and $0.92$ on video) with the best models achieving $R^2 = 0.59$ on text, $0.58$ on image, and $0.43$ on video experiments. We intend for CogGym to provide a living evaluation framework that continually incorporates new cognitive science experiments to characterize where model behavior resembles human behavior, where it systematically diverges, and how those patterns change as models and experiments evolve.

</details>


### [93] [PlaceReasoner-Beta: Reasoning-Driven Macro Placement and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21263)
*Qiufeng Li,Chengxuan Wang,Rongqian Chen,Quan Cheng,Yihui Ren,Chia-Tung Ho,David Z. Pan,Tian Lan,Weidong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PlaceReasoner-Beta，一个验证器引导的多智能体宏单元布局框架，将宏布局从一次性黑盒代理优化重构为闭环推理问题；并发布开放端到端基准 PlaceReasoner-Bench。方法结合 VLM 规划、几何验证、物理验证与布线后优化，在 16 个任务上以布线后 PPA/DRC 评估，取得 DRC-clean 方法中最优时序，1:1 与 2:1 下 TNS 相对经典基线分别降低 61.2% 与 53.0%，且多数设计布线线长更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLSI 宏布局方法主要优化手工代理目标（如预估线长），采用一次性数值优化，难以统一融入视觉布局上下文、可编码设计经验以及下游物理设计反馈，导致代理目标与最终 PPA/DRC 脱节。

Method: 将宏布局建模为闭环推理问题，构建验证器引导的多智能体框架：VLM 规划器依据版图图像、宏规格与连接结构生成候选布局；几何验证器保证物理合法性与专家布局原则；物理验证器利用早期实现反馈精炼候选；布线后优化器利用最终 PPA 进一步改进有潜力的布局。同时构建 PlaceReasoner-Bench，基于开放 RTL、EDA 工具与工艺库，包含 8 个设计、2 种宽高比共 16 个固定版图与 I/O 分配任务，仅宏位置与朝向可变，以布线后 PPA 与 DRC 评估。

Result: 在 PlaceReasoner-Bench 上，PlaceReasoner-Beta 在所有方形任务的 DRC-clean 方法中取得最佳时序：相比经典基线，1:1 下布线后 TNS 降低 61.2%，2:1 下降低 53.0%；尽管从未显式优化布线线长，在多数设计上仍缩短了布线线长。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在物理设计反馈下对空间结构进行推理，可超越代理目标优化，提升端到端布局质量；验证器引导的多智能体闭环范式与开放基准为可复现的宏布局研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Automated macro placement remains a fundamental challenge in VLSI physical design. Despite decades of research, existing approaches predominantly optimize hand-crafted proxy objectives, such as estimated wirelength, and typically produce placements through one-shot numerical optimization, limiting their ability to incorporate visual layout context, codified design expertise, and downstream physical-design feedback in a unified loop. We present PlaceReasoner-Beta, a verifier-guided multi-agent framework that reformulates macro placement as a closed-loop reasoning problem rather than black-box optimization. A vision-language model (VLM) planner generates candidate placements from the floorplan image, macro specifications, and connectivity structure; a geometric verifier enforces physical legality and expert placement principles; a physical verifier refines candidates using early implementation feedback; and a post-route optimizer further improves promising layouts using final PPA. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce PlaceReasoner-Bench, a fully open end-to-end benchmark built from open RTL designs, EDA tools, and technology libraries. It comprises 8 designs at two aspect ratios, yielding 16 tasks with fixed floorplans and I/O assignments, so methods differ only in macro positions and orientations and are evaluated using routed PPA and DRC rather than pre-route proxies. Across the benchmark, PlaceReasoner-Beta achieves the best timing among DRC-clean methods on all square tasks, reducing post-route TNS by 61.2% at 1:1 and 53.0% at 2:1 relative to the classical baseline field. It also shortens routed wirelength on most designs despite never explicitly optimizing it, demonstrating that reasoning over spatial structure under physical-design feedback can improve end-to-end layout quality beyond proxy-objective optimization.

</details>


### [94] [Efficient Benchmarking in Production: A Study of an Evolving LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21267)
*Yining She,Lei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生产环境中LLM智能体的高效周期性评估问题，基于574次历史运行对比了随机采样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集与IRT自适应测试四种方案，发现多维2PL自适应测试保真度最高，但最终因运维简便部署了难度分层固定子集，并验证其可跨智能体家族迁移且校准窗口可短至一天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM智能体在迭代演进过程中需要反复评估，但每次运行完整基准测试成本高昂。因此需要一种既能保证评估保真度、又能降低评估成本的高效周期性评估方法，并需要结合实际部署经验给出可操作的建议。

Method: 利用574次生产基准历史运行数据，按时间顺序划分为校准期与留出期，系统对比四类评估策略：随机采样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集，以及基于IRT（项目反应理论）的自适应测试。其中重点考察多维2PL自适应测试的效果，并对固定子集进行跨智能体家族与不同校准窗口的稳定性验证。

Result: 多维2PL自适应测试获得最佳整体评分保真度：仅执行200道题（占完整运行的38.5%）即可达到1.03个百分点的MAE。尽管自适应测试精度最高，实际部署却选择了难度分层固定子集，原因是其运维更为简便；实验表明该子集无需重新校准即可迁移至另外五个智能体家族，且在短至一天的校准窗口下仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 对于生产智能体的周期性评估，应综合考虑保真度与运维简便性：自适应测试在保真度上最优，而难度分层固定子集在可迁移性与校准稳定性上更具工程实用价值。基于此部署经验，作者给出了面向生产智能体重复评估的实践建议。

Abstract: Production LLM agents are evaluated repeatedly as they evolve, but full agent benchmarks are costly to rerun. We study efficient recurring evaluation for a production analytics agent serving tens of thousands of monthly active users and report first-hand deployment experience. Using 574 historical runs of the production benchmark, split chronologically into calibration and held-out periods, we compare random sampling, historical caching, fixed representative subsets, and IRT-based adaptive testing. The results show that multidimensional 2PL adaptive testing achieves the best overall score fidelity: executing 200 questions, 38.5% of a full run, yields 1.03 pp of MAE. We nevertheless deployed difficulty-stratified fixed subsets because of their operational simplicity, and show they transfer without recalibration to five other agent families and remain stable across calibration windows as short as one day. Drawing on this deployment experience, we report practical recommendations for recurring production-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [GameASG-Bench: Benchmarking Autonomous Software Generation for Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21293)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Yi Chen,Shusheng Xu,Fan Li,Huan Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GameASG-Bench：一个把"行为可测试性"纳入生成任务的游戏开发基准，包含 47 个浏览器原生游戏生成任务（12 个主要品类、2D/3D），通过 L1 静态检查与 L2 浏览器执行检查进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件生成（ASG）旨在将人类需求转化为可执行应用，但生成出应用并不等于其交互组件满足指定的行为需求；现有评测往往只看平均通过率，掩盖了任务级的合规缺口。

Method: 在生成前声明评测接口规范，固定合法起始场景、玩家级动作、稳定快照、拒绝行为与不变量，同时保留私有实现空间；评测分两层：(i) L1 静态检查评估源码级合规；(ii) L2 浏览器执行检查结合语义观测、真实输入与运行时证据。将该协议实现为 47 个浏览器原生游戏生成任务，覆盖 12 个主要品类与 2D/3D 交互，每个任务配有可执行检查与独立验证的参考实现。

Result: 围绕四个关键问题（端到端智能体性能、工具访问与标称回合预算、推理强度、执行框架选择）开展实验。在 9 个智能体栈中，最高平均 L2 检查通过率为 93.2%，但最高严格任务成功率（需满足全部 L1 及相关 L2 前置与核心需求检查）仅为 55.3%（26/47）。对 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，完整工具访问与更大标称回合预算带来更多严格任务成功，而严格任务成功率对推理强度并非单调。两个测试框架各自达到 18 个严格任务成功，但仅 10 个任务在两者下都成功。

Conclusion: 结果表明高平均检查通过率会掩盖任务级合规缺口，严格任务成功率是更严格、更能暴露短板的评测指标；行为可测试性应作为生成任务的组成部分。

Abstract: Autonomous software generation (ASG) aims to turn human requirements into executable applications, but delivering these applications does not necessarily establish that their interacting components satisfy the specified behavioral requirements. We introduce GameASG-Bench, a benchmark that makes behavioral testability part of the generation task for game development. Our design declares an evaluation interface specification before generation, fixing legal starting scenarios, player-level actions, stable snapshots, rejection behavior, and invariants while leaving private implementations open. Concretely, we include: (i) static L1 checks that assess source-level compliance; and (ii) browser-executed L2 checks that combine semantic observations with real input and runtime evidence. We implement this protocol as 47 browser-native game-generation tasks spanning 12 primary genres and both 2D and 3D interaction, each with executable checks and an independently verified reference implementation. Our experiments answer four key questions about end-to-end agent performance, tool access and nominal turn budget, reasoning effort, and harness choice. Across nine agent stacks, the highest observed mean L2 check pass rate is 93.2%, yet the highest observed strict task success rate, requiring all L1 and applicable L2 prerequisite and core requirement checks, is only 55.3% (26/47 tasks). For DeepSeek-V4-Flash, full tool access and larger nominal turn budgets yield more strict task successes, while the strict task success rate is not monotonic in reasoning effort. Both tested harnesses achieve 18 strict task successes, but only ten tasks succeed under both. These results expose task-level compliance gaps that high average check pass rates actually obscure.

</details>


### [96] [LEGIT: Credentialing Protocol for Trustworthy AI Agent Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21325)
*Steve Drew,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEGIT凭证协议，将认证、声誉与市场分配连接起来，使买家可验证AI agent的能力与成本，并分析声誉操纵所需押金与费用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的agentic市场由能力各异的AI agent为买家自主完成专门任务，但买家难以判断哪个agent最适合其任务；已报告的基准分数难以验证，也难以跨任务、软件和预算进行比较。

Method: LEGIT凭证协议连接认证、声誉与提议的市场分配。认证通过签名记录，将测得质量与每解决任务成本绑定到agent配置、任务领域、评估预算和证据；声誉将过往任务结果记录链接到同一身份，并受反馈可靠性约束。买家和agent可验证凭证记录并查看可选的可视化档案。补充分析在给定女巫攻击模型下量化声誉操纵所需押金与费用。

Result: 评估揭示了在观察任务成功率相近时不同agent配置之间的成本差异，并表明比较结果取决于评估预算。这些结果支持将性能测量绑定到受测配置和资源限制。补充分析量化了在特定女巫攻击模型下实施声誉操纵所需的押金和费用。

Conclusion: LEGIT通过签名认证与身份关联声誉，为agent市场提供可验证的凭证和分配基础；其结论强调性能测量必须绑定到具体配置与资源限制，并通过押金/费用机制提高声誉操纵成本。

Abstract: Agentic marketplaces are emerging where AI agents with varying capabilities autonomously complete specialized tasks for buyers. A major challenge of such marketplaces is that buyers cannot easily determine which agent will perform best on their tasks. Reported benchmark scores may be difficult to verify or compare across tasks, software, and budgets. We introduce LEGIT, a credentialing protocol connecting certification, reputation, and proposed marketplace allocation. Certification binds measured quality and cost per solved task to an agent configuration, task domain, evaluation budget, and evidence through a signed record. Reputation links records of past task outcomes to the same identity, subject to the reliability of the reported feedback. Buyers and agents can verify credential records and inspect optional visual profiles. Evaluations reveal cost differences between agent configurations with similar observed task success, and show that comparisons depend on the evaluation budget. These results support binding performance measurements to the tested configuration and resource limits. A complementary analysis quantifies the deposits and fees required for reputation manipulation under a stated Sybil attack model.

</details>


### [97] [Offline Multimodal Large Language Models for Decision Support in Air Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21390)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Jelton A. Cunha,Gabriel Dietzsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在隔离、无互联网、强安全约束环境下，将离线大语言模型作为决策支持工具，提出模块化检索增强架构，支持文本与图像输入，并可溯源至原始条令来源。以4名巴西空军图像分析人员开展试点：人工制作侦察目标报告（REMIR）认知负荷较高（心理需求6.0/7、努力5.0/7）；系统在条令知识测评中得分8/10，与人工持平，耗时7.1分钟，远低于人工平均26.5分钟。最后提出未来人工与AI辅助工作流的系统评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空等专业操作依赖复杂规则、既定程序和时间敏感分析，且常处于连接受限、安全约束严格、无外部计算资源的环境中。分析人员需结合书面条令与图像资料，因此需要可离线部署、支持多模态输入、交互自然且知识可溯源的LLM决策支持工具，以降低认知负荷并提升任务效率。

Method: 提出一种适用于无互联网连接的模块化检索增强生成（RAG）架构，支持技术手册中的文本与图像输入，通过自然语言交互提供可追溯至原始来源的条令知识。试点研究纳入4名巴西空军图像分析人员，包含两项任务：（i）基于电子目标识别条令的知识测评，比较人工与系统在同一测试上的表现；（ii）测量无AI辅助手工制作侦察目标报告（REMIR）的认知负荷。同时描述未来系统比较人工与AI辅助工作流的评估协议。

Result: 手工制作REMIR任务认知负荷较高，其中心理需求为6.0/7，努力为5.0/7。所提系统在知识测评中得分8/10，与人工得分持平；完成时间为7.1分钟，人工平均为26.5分钟。该结果为后续AI辅助评估建立了基线。

Conclusion: 离线RAG-LLM在隔离受限环境中具有作为可溯源决策支持工具的潜力，可在保持与人类相当准确率的同时显著缩短任务时间。未来需按所提协议系统比较人工与AI辅助工作流，并在更大样本和更多任务类型中验证泛化性、可靠性与认知负荷变化。

Abstract: Air operations rely on complex rules, established procedures, and time-critical analysis under limited connectivity and strict security constraints. In such environments, analysts must combine written doctrine with images, often without access to external computing resources. This paper studies offline large language models as decision support tools, deployed in isolated and restricted environments to give analysts access to doctrinal knowledge that remains traceable to its original sources through natural language interaction. We describe a modular retrieval-augmented architecture suitable for operation without Internet connectivity, supporting both text and image input from technical manuals. As a first step toward evaluating this architecture, we report a pilot study with four image analysts of the Brazilian Air Force, combining (i) a doctrinal knowledge assessment based on their electronic-target identification doctrine, comparing human and proposed system performance on the same test, and (ii) a measurement of the cognitive workload involved in manually producing a reconnaissance target report (Relatório de Missão de Reconhecimento - REMIR) without AI assistance. The results show a demanding manual task, especially in terms of mental demand (6.0/7) and effort (5.0/7), while the proposed system matches the human score (8/10) and completes the assessment in 7.1 minutes (compared to a human average of 26.5 minutes), establishing a baseline for future AI-assisted evaluation. Finally, we describe a future evaluation protocol to systematically compare manual and AI-assisted workflows.

</details>


### [98] [DENSE: Distilling Agent Trajectories into Evidence-Grounded Shortcut Trees for Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21423)
*Siyuan Liu,Fan Yu,Dongyu Ru,Yizhu Liu,Yifan Yang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 DENSE，将智能体执行轨迹蒸馏为基于证据的嵌套捷径树，在无事后结果标签条件下生成可复用反馈；并提出 REFIT 评估协议。在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，DENSE 在四种接收模型中取得非特权反馈方法中最高的严格通过率，并减少重跑 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体部署会产生大量执行轨迹，但任务特定验证与专家标注难以规模化。作者希望在不依赖事后结果标签的情况下，从轨迹中的局部进展、恢复与未完成需求等证据中提炼可复用反馈，降低对外部监督的依赖。

Method: 提出 DENSE（Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions），将执行证据组织为“基于证据的嵌套捷径树”：压缩冗余尝试，利用恢复证据跨层级调和问题，对已完成分支进行摘要，对未解决分支继续展开，从而把可复用进展与剩余义务关联起来。同时提出 REFIT，一种源配对协议，在事后结果盲条件下比较来自共享初始轨迹的反馈，并重置环境与模型上下文以对同一任务进行新尝试。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，DENSE 在四种接收模型中取得所测试非特权反馈方法中最高的严格通过率。相对于初始执行，严格通过率提升 7.12–15.64 个百分点，同时重跑中观察到的接收方 token 减少 19.0–43.6%。GPT-5.5 消融实验支持将嵌套子任务分析、捷径构建与问题调和相结合。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于证据的轨迹复用可推动智能体自我改进，在提升任务表现的同时减少对外部监督的依赖，并提高重跑效率。

Abstract: Online agent deployments produce abundant execution traces, while task-specific verification and expert annotation are costly to scale. We study how to distill these traces into reusable feedback without post-hoc outcome labels, drawing on their evidence of local progress, recovery, and unfinished requirements. We introduce DENSE (Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions), which organizes this evidence into evidence-grounded nested shortcut trees. DENSE compresses redundant attempts, reconciles issues across levels using recovery evidence, and summarizes completed branches while expanding unresolved ones, linking reusable progress to remaining obligations. We introduce REFIT, a source-paired protocol comparing feedback from shared initial trajectories under post-hoc outcome blindness, with environments and model contexts reset for fresh attempts at the same tasks. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, DENSE achieves the highest strict pass rate among tested non-privileged feedback methods across four recipient models. Relative to initial executions, strict pass rate improves by 7.12-15.64 pp, with 19.0-43.6% fewer observed recipient tokens in reruns. GPT-5.5 ablations support combining nested subtask analysis with shortcut construction and issue reconciliation. These findings point toward agent self-refinement through evidence-grounded trajectory reuse with less reliance on external supervision.

</details>


### [99] [GVPO++: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21432)
*Kaichen Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dingqian Hong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GVPO（Group Variance Policy Optimization），将 KL 约束奖励最大化的解析解融入梯度加权，避免重要性采样，缓解后训练不稳定，并可自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏（OPD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（如 GRPO）依赖重要性采样，导致训练不稳定，制约实际部署；同时需要兼顾理论最优性与采样灵活性。

Method: GVPO 将 KL 约束奖励最大化的解析解引入梯度加权方案，使梯度等价于“隐式奖励中心距离”与“实际奖励中心距离”之间的均方误差；无需重要性采样即可支持灵活采样分布，并扩展到 on-policy distillation 及一类扩展 OPD 目标。

Result: GVPO 具备两项优势：(1) 保证唯一最优解，且严格对应 KL 约束奖励最大化目标；(2) 支持灵活采样分布，不依赖重要性采样；同时可自然扩展到 OPD 并优化更广泛的扩展 OPD 目标族。

Conclusion: GVPO 将理论保证与实践适应性统一，为可靠、通用的 LLM 后训练与在线策略蒸馏提供了新范式。

Abstract: Post-training plays a pivotal role in enhancing the reasoning capabilities and task-specific expertise of large language models (LLMs). Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling.
  We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained reward maximization into its gradient weighting scheme. This formulation provides an intuitive interpretation: GVPO's gradient corresponds to the mean squared error between the central distance of implicit rewards and that of actual rewards. GVPO offers two key advantages: (1) it guarantees a unique optimal solution, exactly to the KL-constrained reward maximization objective, and (2) it enables flexible sampling distributions without requiring importance sampling.
  Beyond general post-training, we show that GVPO naturally extends to on-policy distillation (OPD). Furthermore, GVPO enables the optimization of a broad family of extended OPD objectives, providing a principled foundation for diverse objective design. By unifying theoretical guarantees with practical adaptability, GVPO establishes a new paradigm for reliable and versatile LLM post-training and on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [100] [Risk-Aware Occupancy for Safety-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21470)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,Yiren Zhao,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"风险感知占据"（risk-aware occupancy）这一稠密表征，将全局场景占据、地图交通约束与未来动态智能体占据统一编码为BEV地图，并设计端到端网络ROIDrive实现安全导向的轨迹规划，在nuScenes上显著降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统多采用稀疏表征（将环境感知表述为对象、车道线等离散元素），在拥挤、遮挡场景中面对非结构化障碍、不确定区域和复杂交互时存在安全隐患。作者希望以显式且统一的方式刻画与规划相关的风险。

Method: 提出稠密表征"风险感知占据"，联合编码三类信息：全局场景占据、地图衍生的交通约束、未来动态智能体占据，统一为一张BEV地图，在空间与时间两个维度上捕捉轨迹规划所需的碰撞风险证据。设计端到端网络ROIDrive：通过独立分支预测风险感知占据，并将其注入规划查询（planning queries）以生成安全导向的轨迹。同时构建基于nuScenes与occ3d-nuScenes的RiskOcc4D-nuScenes数据集以量化安全问题。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，风险感知占据在开环评测中带来相对碰撞降低：UniAD指标下52.9%，ST-P3指标下35.0%。

Conclusion: 用统一稠密的风险感知占据表征替代稀疏表征，并通过独立分支预测并注入规划查询，可有效提升端到端驾驶的规划安全性；所提数据集为安全量化提供了基准。

Abstract: Sparse representation formulates the environment perception for the end-to-end driving system as a set of discrete elements like objects and lane lines. This formulation meets safety risks in crowded, occluded scenes dealing with unstructured obstacles, uncertain regions, and intricate interactions. In this paper, we propose a dense representation, risk-aware occupancy, to characterize planning-relevant risks in an explicit and uniform manner. It jointly encodes global scene occupancy, map-derived traffic constraints, and future dynamic agent occupancy into a unified BEV map. The unified BEV map captures the risk evidence for trajectory planning in both spatial and temporal dimensions. We design an E2E network, ROIDrive, to realize risk-aware occupancy. It predicts risk-aware occupancy with an independent branch and injects it into planning queries for safety-oriented trajectory generation. In addition, to quantify the safety problem, we introduce RiskOcc4D-nuScenes built upon nuscenes and occ3d-nuscenes. Our risk-aware occupancy yields relative open-loop collision reductions of 52.9% under the UniAD metric and 35.0% under the ST-P3 metric on nuScenes.

</details>


### [101] [Driving on Registers, Reasoning on Risk: Risk-Aware Occupancy for Register-Based End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21486)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,YuKai Qin,Yiren Zhao,Lidong Yu,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RRDrive，用风险感知占用（risk-aware occupancy）作为密集、时序对齐、可轨迹查询的表示，提升端到端自动驾驶多模态轨迹预测的候选生成与排序，并构建RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM数据集及外部RiskOcc预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态轨迹预测受稀疏场景表示限制，证据不完整导致候选轨迹生成质量低，且几何相似轨迹之间排序不可靠。在register-based基线上，差/劣候选占候选集的19.74%，oracle-best候选平均仅排第33.9位。

Method: RRDrive引入风险感知占用作为密集、时序对齐、可轨迹查询的场景表示：其全局结构引导高质量多模态候选生成，候选条件风险查询（candidate-conditioned risk queries）支持细粒度选择。同时构建带自动风险标注的RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM，并进一步开发外部RiskOcc预测器，直接从传感器输入估计风险感知占用。

Result: RRDrive取得选定轨迹PDMS 0.951，较基线0.937相对提升1.5%；候选平均PDMS提升7.7%。在困难场景中，候选PDMS提升30.2%，好候选间Spearman相关系数提高0.41，从0.26升至0.67。外部RiskOcc预测器表现具有竞争力，验证了该表示的可行性。

Conclusion: 风险感知占用表示可同时改善多模态候选生成与排序，缓解稀疏表示带来的证据不足和oracle依赖问题，并具备从感知输入端部署的可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal trajectory prediction improves behavioral coverage in end-to-end autonomous driving, but existing methods remain limited by sparse scene representations.
