Incomplete evidence leads to low-quality candidate generation and unreliable ranking among geometrically similar trajectories. On a register-based baseline, bad and poor candidates constitute 19.74% of the candidate set, while the oracle-best candidate ranks only 33.9th on average. We propose RRDrive, which introduces risk-aware occupancy as a dense, temporally aligned, and trajectory-queryable representation. Its global structure guides high-quality multimodal generation, while candidate-conditioned risk queries support fine-grained selection. We further construct RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM with automatic risk annotations. RRDrive achieves a selected-trajectory PDMS of 0.951, representing a 1.5% relative improvement over the baseline (0.937), and improves the average candidate PDMS by 7.7%. In challenging scenes, it improves candidate PDMS by 30.2% and increases the Spearman correlation among good candidates by 0.41, from 0.26 to 0.67. To move beyond this oracle setting, we further develop an external RiskOcc predictor, a perception module that estimates risk-aware occupancy directly from sensor inputs. The competitive performance validates the representation's feasibility.

</details>


### [102] [LogicTrack: Auditing Reasoning Trajectories of Large Language Models with Formal Logic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21492)
*Jingyu Hu,Shu Yang,Weiru Liu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LogicTrack 神经符号框架，通过将思维链每一步自动形式化为符号表示并用定理证明器验证，结合求解器回溯奖励（SBR）与回溯树搜索，并生成带回溯轨迹的 SFT 数据，使模型内化逐步审计能力，从而提升推理链可验证性与答案正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 优化多依赖结果反馈，忽视中间推理步骤的逻辑有效性，导致模型可能以逻辑有缺陷的链条得到正确答案，在高风险领域缺乏可信性。

Method: LogicTrack 将每个推理步骤自动形式化为符号表示，用自动定理证明器逐步验证；提出 Solver-Based Backtracking Reward（SBR）逐步评分逻辑可靠性，并在推理时引导回溯树搜索；进一步利用其轨迹构造含回溯痕迹的监督微调数据，使模型内化逐步审计能力。

Result: 在 8 个推理基准与 7 个 LLM 上的实验表明，LogicTrack 同时提升推理链可验证性和最终答案通过率。

Conclusion: LogicTrack 通过神经符号逐步审计与回溯搜索，增强了 CoT 质量与可信度，尤其有利于高风险领域的可靠推理。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has been shown to improve the performance of large language models (LLMs), yet existing optimization methods largely rely on outcome-based feedback, leaving the logical validity of intermediate reasoning steps largely unverified. To address the gap whereby LLMs arrive at correct final answers through logically flawed intermediate reasoning chains, we propose LogicTrack, a neuro-symbolic framework that audits reasoning trajectories by auto-formalizing each reasoning step into symbolic representations and verifying it with automated theorem provers. LogicTrack introduces Solver-Based Backtracking Reward (SBR), a step-wise scoring mechanism that quantifies logical soundness and guides backtracking tree search at inference time. We further extend LogicTrack to construct supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data with backtracking traces from its trajectories, enabling fine-tuned models to internalize step-wise auditing as an intrinsic capability. Extensive experiments across 8 reasoning benchmarks and 7 LLMs demonstrate that LogicTrack effectively improves both the verifiability of reasoning chains and final answer pass rate, thereby enhancing overall CoT quality and trustworthiness in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [103] [PolyBridgeBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Physics-Grounded Bridge Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21493)
*Zicheng Zhao,Dongyin Chen,Rui Xu,Yinghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PolyBridgeBench：一个可执行的多模态桥梁设计基准，要求 MLLM 根据视觉场景与工程约束生成节点—构件—材料拓扑，经确定性合法性检查后进入原生动态物理仿真；若执行失败，则基于失败 rollout 的时序视觉证据在固定交互预算下评估修复能力。实验覆盖 6 个代表性 MLLM、189 个关卡，揭示确定性合法性与动态功能成功之间存在显著差距、对材料预算高度敏感，且严格预算下失败后恢复能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型虽具备视觉理解与结构化生成能力，但能否生成真正可执行、可承载的工程设计仍不明确。已有基准多评估空间推理、结构有效性或物理约束构建，却无法判断 MLLM 能否综合完整承重结构，并在仿真执行暴露失败后进行修复。

Method: 构建可执行多模态桥梁设计基准 PolyBridgeBench。模型输入视觉场景与结构化工程约束，输出完整的节点—构件—材料拓扑；系统先用确定性合法性检查作为执行门槛，再在原生动态物理仿真中运行。若执行失败，基准返回失败 rollout 的时序视觉证据，并在固定交互预算下评估修复。评测分别测量确定性有效性、动态功能成功率与失败后恢复能力，以定位设计失败阶段。

Result: 在 189 个关卡上测试 6 个代表性 MLLM，结果显示确定性合法性与动态成功之间存在显著差距，模型对材料预算表现出明显敏感性，并且在主要严格预算设置下失败后恢复能力有限。

Conclusion: PolyBridgeBench 将多模态工程设计评估从静态结构判断推进到“生成—执行—失败修复”的可执行闭环，能够分阶段诊断 MLLM 的设计失败，并揭示当前模型在动态功能成功与受预算约束的修复方面仍存在明显不足。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models, or MLLMs, perform well at visual understanding and structured generation, yet these capabilities do not establish whether an engineering design will work when executed. Existing benchmarks assess spatial reasoning, structural validity, or physics-grounded construction, but they do not determine whether MLLMs can synthesize complete load-bearing structures and repair them after simulator execution exposes a failure. We introduce PolyBridgeBench, an executable benchmark for multimodal bridge design. A model receives a visual scene and structured engineering constraints and generates a complete node--member--material topology. Deterministic legality checks gate execution in a native dynamic physics simulation. Following an execution failure, the benchmark returns temporal visual evidence from the failed rollout and evaluates repair under a fixed interaction budget. Separate measurements of deterministic validity, dynamic functional success, and post-failure recovery identify the stage at which design fails. Experiments with six representative MLLMs across 189 levels expose a substantial gap between deterministic validity and dynamic success, pronounced sensitivity to material budgets, and limited post-failure recovery under the primary strict-budget setting.

</details>


### [104] [The Communication Bottleneck: A Round-Trip Study of Tree-Structured Expression Serialization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21509)
*Xavier Suau,Alex Ferrando de las Morenas,Luca Zappella,Samy Bengio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"往返协议"（round-trip protocol）来实证检验：当语言模型把树结构表达式序列化为自然语言时，究竟有多少树结构组合信息能通过这一"瓶颈"。通过生成器↔抽取器的两两配对实验，作者揭示了该信道的有损性、非对称性、失败根源，以及可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在思维链或自由文本中间交换中进行推理时，必须把结构化信息序列化为自然语言。然而这一"序列化瓶颈"会损失多少树结构的组合内容尚不清楚。作者希望量化评估：在把层次化（树结构）信息用自然语言传递时，结构化内容能保留下多少，并定位限制模型间通信的关键因素。

Method: 提出往返协议（round-trip protocol）：1）生成器把程序化生成的算术表达式转写为文字应用题；2）独立的抽取器仅凭该文字应用题还原表达式；3）用符号等价性作为精确的判定 oracle。对16个模型进行所有两两组合评估，得到通信矩阵，其边缘分布可分离"生成质量"与"抽取质量"。此外，用约3600条共享评估算符与树形的微调样本验证信道可训练性，并设置使用新算符与新词汇的"不相交领域"（disjoint-domain）实验以排除语义匹配假象。

Result: 三点主要发现：1）信道有损且非对称——交换生成/抽取角色可使准确率变化高达60.4个百分点，最佳配对（两端使用不同模型）达92.9%，优于同模型两端组合。2）至少73.6%的往返失败源于生成阶段，难度由树结构驱动（算符数量、深度、右分支结构），而非模型家族。3）信道可训练——约3600条共享算符与树形的微调样本使每个开源权重模型超过未训练的Gemini-3.1-Pro（语义匹配下的上界）；在含新算符与新词汇的不相交领域实验中，所有开源权重模型同样提升，说明增益并非语义匹配假象，但与前沿模型仍有差距。

Conclusion: 树结构表达式的序列化是模型通过自然语言传递层次化结构时的一个主要限制因素。往返协议提供了可分离生成与抽取质量、并可用符号等价精确判定的评估框架，表明该通信瓶颈可被微调显著缓解，但距离前沿水平仍有提升空间。

Abstract: When language models reason in chain-of-thought or exchange free-text intermediates, they serialize structured information into natural language. How much tree-structured compositional content survives this bottleneck? We propose a round-trip protocol that answers this question empirically for tree-structured expressions. A generator converts a procedurally generated arithmetic expression into a word problem, a separate extractor recovers the expression from the word problem alone, and symbolic equivalence provides an exact oracle. Evaluating all pairwise combinations of sixteen models yields a communication matrix whose marginals separate generation quality from extraction quality. Three main findings emerge. First, the channel is lossy and asymmetric: swapping which model generates and which extracts shifts accuracy by up to 60.4 points, and the best pair reaches 92.9% by combining different models on each end rather than the same model on both. Second, at least 73.6% of round-trip failures originate at generation, and difficulty is driven by tree structure (operator count, depth, right-branching) rather than model family. Third, the channel is trainable: ~3600 fine-tuning examples that share the evaluation's operators and tree shapes lift every open-weight model above untrained Gemini-3.1-Pro, an upper bound under matched semantics. A disjoint-domain regime with new operators and vocabulary also raises every open-weight model, confirming the gain is not an artifact of matched semantics, though a gap to the frontier remains. Together these results identify tree-structured expression serialization as a primary limiting factor when models communicate hierarchical structure through natural language.

</details>


### [105] [Learning-to-Optimize as the Missing Architectural Layer of AI-Native Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21519)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Pierpaolo Salvo,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将Learning-to-Optimize（L2O）作为AI原生通信网络中连接优化与AI智能的缺失架构层：把优化算法重新定义为离线知识生成器，为神经代理模型提供监督信息，并提出“优化—知识生成—代理学习—运行时推理”四阶段工作流；以NR-V2X中继选择为例，用MILP解训练GNN，实现实时近最优决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI原生网络架构虽在网络功能中嵌入智能，支持自主资源管理、自适应控制和零接触运维，但缺乏关于优化知识如何被系统化生成、迁移和利用的架构指导；现有L2O研究多聚焦算法加速，尚未上升为跨异构通信与计算系统的通用架构抽象。

Method: 将优化从单纯的在线决策引擎重新定义为离线知识生成器，为神经代理模型产生高质量监督信息；提出通用四阶段L2O工作流：优化、知识生成、代理学习、运行时推理；建立L2O作为架构抽象，使其适用于异构通信与计算系统。以NR-V2X中继选择问题为例，使用混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解器生成优化解，用于训练图神经网络（GNN）。

Result: 训练后的GNN能够实时复现近最优决策；神经代理模型继承优化算法的专业知识，同时支持适合动态网络环境的低延迟运行时推理。该案例表明L2O可作为跨系统的架构范式，而非仅用于算法加速。

Conclusion: L2O被定位为未来AI原生网络的关键架构使能者，为优化与AI原生智能之间提供缺失的架构层，并推动优化知识在异构通信与计算系统中的系统化生成、迁移与利用。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is becoming a fundamental design principle of future AI-native communication networks, enabling autonomous resource management, adaptive control, and zero-touch network operation. While current AI-native architectures increasingly embed intelligence across network functions, they provide little guidance on how optimisation knowledge should be systematically generated, transferred, and exploited by AI models. This paper argues that the Learning-to-Optimize (L2O) represents the missing architectural layer between optimisation and AI-native intelligence. Rather than viewing optimisation merely as an online decision engine, the proposed paradigm redefines optimisation algorithms as offline knowledge generators that produce high-quality supervisory information for neural surrogate models. The resulting models inherit optimisation expertise while enabling low-latency runtime inference suitable for dynamic network environments. A generic four-stage L2O workflow is introduced, comprising optimisation, knowledge generation, surrogate learning, and runtime inference. Unlike existing Learning-to-Optimize approaches, which primarily focus on algorithm acceleration, the proposed framework establishes L2O as an architectural abstraction applicable across heterogeneous communication and computing systems. The proposed paradigm is illustrated by an NR-V2X relay-selection problem, in which optimisation-generated solutions from a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solver are used to train a Graph Neural Network that can reproduce near-optimal decisions in real time. The presented perspective positions Learning-to-Optimize as a key architectural enabler for future AI-native networks.

</details>


### [106] [Dual-Interest Sequential Product Recommendation With Multi-Granular SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21548)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSRec 是一种双兴趣跨 SSM 序列推荐模型，通过长短期兴趣解耦、Mamba 与时间调制 SSM 编码及残差交叉融合，提升推荐性能并兼顾效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 显著提升了序列推荐效果，但成本效率受限；SSM 虽支持高效长程建模，然而多数现有方法将同一物品编码为单一静态上下文角色，忽略物品多义性。实际上，同一物品在不同用户上下文中承担不同语义角色，且现有方法难以捕捉不同时间粒度下的动态行为。

Method: 提出 DSRec：1）显式解耦长期与短期语义上下文中的物品角色；2）长期兴趣嵌入通过历史聚合捕捉稳定偏好，短期兴趣分支强调由点击时间间隔调制的局部会话意图；3）分别使用全序列 Mamba 进行长期建模，使用时间调制 SSM 根据时间间隔动态调整状态演化；4）采用残差交叉融合机制，在两个分支间交换上下文信息，同时保持语义独立性，实现跨粒度对齐。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上的实验表明，DSRec 优于其他最先进方法。

Conclusion: DSRec 通过显式建模物品多义性、解耦长短期兴趣并实现跨时间粒度融合，有效提升序列推荐性能，同时利用 SSM 保持高效的长程建模能力。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation aims to predict the next item a user will interact with based on their historical behavior. Advances in Transformers have significantly improved sequential recommendation but are still limited by cost efficiency. Although State Space Models (SSMs) have recently enabled efficient long-range modeling, most existing methods encode each item with a single static contextual role, overlooking the phenomenon of item polysemy. In fact, the same item often plays different semantic roles depending on user context, and existing methods are limited in capturing dynamic behavior across different temporal granularities. In this work, we propose DSRec, a novel dual-interest cross-SSM model that explicitly disentangles item roles across long-term and short-term semantic context. Sequential items are encoded into long-term interest embeddings that capture stable preferences via historical aggregation, and a short-term interest branch that emphasizes local session intent modulated by inter-click time intervals. These interest embeddings are processed through distinct SSM encoders: a full-sequence Mamba for long-term modeling, and a time-modulated SSM that dynamically adjusts state evolution based on temporal gaps. To enable effective cross-granularity alignment, we adopt a residual cross-fusion mechanism that exchanges contextual information between the two branches while preserving semantic independence. Experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that DSRec outperforms other state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Accuracy: Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss for Structured Fraudulent Job Posting Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21599)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Malaika Raza,Muhammad Shoaib Siddiqui,Muhammad Rafi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出质心引导对比损失（CGCL），将分类与密集聚类统一，通过质心驱动的 top-k 推拉机制重塑隐空间，在 EMSCAD 基准上实现虚假招聘广告检测的 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假招聘广告检测旨在识别含虚假内容、误导信息或恶意意图的招聘广告，以维护求职者与雇主的在线生态。现有方法难以同时兼顾高准确率与有意义的隐空间表示结构，无法刻画虚假帖子之间的细微差异。

Method: 提出质心引导对比损失（Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss, CGCL），一种将分类与密集公式化聚类相统一的损失函数。其通过质心驱动的 top-k 推拉机制持续重塑隐空间，借助分类与聚类的互补性，使模型既能学习准确的决策边界，又能保持较高的聚类紧致度。

Result: 在公开基准数据集 EMSCAD 上的大量实验表明，该方法取得了当前最优（SOTA）性能。代码已开源：https://github.com/ali-ahmed925/CGCL_code。

Conclusion: CGCL 通过统一分类与聚类，有效兼顾类别可分性与隐空间结构，验证了其在虚假招聘广告检测任务中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Fraudulent job posting detection aims to identify job advertisements that are corrupted either through fake content, misleading information, or negative intent, disrupting the online eco-system of job-seekers and employers. Existing studies in this domain lack effective methods to simultaneously achieve high accuracy and meaningful structure of latent-space representations that capture subtleties among fake posts. To this end, we propose Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss (CGCL), a loss function which unifies classification with densely formulated clustering to consistently reshape latent-space through a centroid-driven top-$k$ push-and-pull mechanism. The complementary nature of CGCL enables the model to enforce accurate decision boundaries and maintain high clustering compactness, effectively capturing both class separability and latent structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance of our method on EMSCAD, a public benchmark dataset. The code associated with this work is available at: https://github.com/ali-ahmed925/CGCL_code

</details>


### [108] [Reducing Barriers to Academic Support: Evaluating a Course-Specific RAG System for Addressing Help-Seeking Disparities in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21600)
*Andy Gray,Jake Hobbs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估了课程专属的RAG学术支持系统Beacon，旨在降低学生求助障碍，结果表明其能提供与模块内容一致、比通用生成式AI更可信的支持，并可作为独立学习与正式教学支持之间的中介。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术支持的可及性是学生成功的关键因素，但学生获取支持并不平等：部分学生因焦虑、害怕被评判、对期望不明确或缺乏理解信心而犹豫求助。在计算教育中，编程任务具有累积性和高认知负荷，这一问题可能更突出。通用生成式AI虽被广泛使用，但回答可能不准确、缺乏课程情境或与模块期望不一致，因此需要课程特定、可信且即时的学术支持系统。

Method: 研究提出并评估Beacon——一个课程专属的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，提供私密、即时、与模块对齐的学术支持。系统以经批准的教學材料为知识基础，设计目标为降低求助门槛并促进独立学习。采用设计型研究方法迭代开发，并使用混合方法评估，结合问卷与半结构化访谈，对象为某高等教育机构的学生和教职员。

Result: 学生认为Beacon的回答与模块内容高度一致，且比不受限的通用生成式AI工具更可信；他们重视系统使用伪代码和支架式解释，而非直接给出答案。参与者对未经核验的AI生成回答仍持谨慎信任态度，但将其视为咨询讲师或官方资源之前的有价值的第一支持点。

Conclusion: 精心设计的课程专属AI系统可通过占据独立学习与正式支持之间的中介空间，降低学术支持获取障碍。教育AI并非取代教育者；当它扩大获取指导的途径并保留讲师的教学角色时，其价值最大。

Abstract: Access to academic support is a key determinant of student success, yet students experience it unequally: some readily seek help from lecturers or tutors, while others hesitate due to anxiety, fear of judgement, uncertainty about expectations, or low confidence in their understanding. This may be especially evident in computing education, where programming tasks are cumulative and cognitively demanding. Although students increasingly turn to general-purpose generative AI tools, these can produce responses that are inaccurate, insufficiently contextualised, or misaligned with module expectations. This study presents and evaluates Beacon, a course-specific Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system providing private, immediate, module-aligned academic support. Grounding responses in approved teaching materials, Beacon was designed to lower barriers to help-seeking while encouraging independent learning. Using a design-based research approach, Beacon was developed iteratively and evaluated via mixed methods, combining questionnaires and semi-structured interviews with students and staff at a Higher Education institution. Students described Beacon's responses as closely aligned with module content and more trustworthy than unrestricted generative AI tools, valuing its use of pseudocode and scaffolded explanations over direct solutions. Although participants remained cautious about trusting AI-generated responses without verification, they viewed the system as a valuable first point of support before consulting lecturers or official resources. The findings suggest that carefully designed course-specific AI systems may reduce barriers to academic support by occupying an intermediary space between independent study and formal support. Rather than replacing educators, educational AI may be most valuable when it broadens access to guidance while preserving the pedagogical role of lecturers.

</details>


### [109] [Calibrating Teacher--Student Discrepancy for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21619)
*Qiangqiang He,Jin Li,MingCai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cal-OPD通过正负特权干预估计教师的自身偏差区域，仅保留超出该区域的教师-学生差异作为优化信号，在数学推理基准上以52-65%的信号量超越标准OPD及其变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过拟合更强教师与在线策略学生之间的token级差异来提升推理模型。然而该差异并非纯粹反映师生能力差距，还混杂了教师自身的偏差，导致学生在训练中不加区分地学习教师的错误倾向。在特权OPD中，特权信息会引发更大的教师侧似然偏移，使这一问题更加严重。

Method: 提出校准在线策略蒸馏（Cal-OPD）：通过正、负特权干预（positive and negative privileged interventions）估计教师的自身偏差区域（self-deviation region），并据此对原始的师生差异进行校准——仅保留位于该偏差区域之外的分量作为优化信号。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Cal-OPD仅保留约52%-65%的原始师生差异作为优化信号，却在多个模型规模下稳定优于标准OPD及其变体。

Conclusion: 教师-学生差异中的教师自身偏差是可识别且可剔除的噪声；通过特权干预校准并裁剪该偏差区域后，蒸馏信号虽被大幅压缩但质量更高，从而在推理任务上实现更优的蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves reasoning models by learning the token-level discrepancy between a stronger teacher and an on-policy student. However, this discrepancy does not purely reflect the capability gap between the teacher and the student: it also contains deviations arising from the teacher itself, which are consequently mixed into the observed teacher--student discrepancy and indiscriminately learned by standard OPD during training. This issue is further exacerbated by privileged OPD, where privileged information induces larger teacher-side likelihood shifts, thereby encouraging the student to learn more of the teacher's own deviation. We introduce \textbf{Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (Cal-OPD)}, which estimates the teacher's self-deviation region through positive and negative privileged interventions and calibrates the original teacher--student discrepancy by retaining only the component that lies beyond this region. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that, while retaining only about 52--65\% of the original teacher--student discrepancy as the optimization signal, Cal-OPD consistently outperforms standard OPD and its variants across model scales.

</details>


### [110] [One Prompt Does Not Fit All: Self-Meta-Evolve for Personalized Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21626)
*Hongliang Li,Lu Wang,Yong Xu,Hanyang Chen,Zhitao Hou,Xiaoting Qin,Song Ge,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Self-Meta-Evolve 分层框架，为企业信息抽取中的每个用户维护并持续优化专属提示，并发布 persona 驱动的可复现基准，性能显著优于现有提示优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业信息抽取场景中，同一文档需针对不同用户以不同方式重组，但现有提示优化方法通常只优化单一全局提示，与真实工作场所中的用户异质性不匹配。

Method: 将企业 IE 形式化为交互反馈下的按用户提示适配问题；提出 Self-Meta-Evolve 双循环框架：内循环基于 persona 条件反馈编辑结构化提示，外循环通过蒸馏成功编辑模式来进化 meta-prompt；同时构建包含 292 个模拟企业用户的基准及基于 O*NET 职业分类的可复现 persona 生成管线。

Result: 在该基准上达到 74.58% 成功率，较最强提示优化基线提升 13.56 个百分点，且仅两轮迭代即达到 52.54%；20 名真实从业者的双盲研究显示，该框架适配的提示在 71% 的成对比较中优于静态基线。

Conclusion: 按用户提示适配与元提示进化能够有效应对企业 IE 中的用户异质性，并通过人类评估验证其实用性；所发布基准与 persona 生成管线为可扩展训练和评估提供了支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [111] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 L0-MoE：一种基于 L0 正则化的轻量级 Mixture-of-Experts 方法，用于在几乎不损失性能的前提下加速稠密大语言模型，最高实现 2.5 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型性能强但推理慢、成本高；现有加速方法常导致明显性能退化；而传统 MoE 模型训练与部署需要大量计算资源。

Method: 利用 L0 正则化构建轻量级 MoE，以稀疏激活方式加速稠密 LLM；引入聚类混淆矩阵进行领域感知的数据集筛选；采用动态批处理提升训练效率。

Result: 实验表明，L0-MoE 相比稠密模型最高获得 2.5 倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的性能，并优于现有 LLM 加速基线方法。

Conclusion: L0-MoE 为稠密 LLM 提供了一种轻量、近乎无损的加速方案，在性能与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [112] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文面向大型推理模型（LRM）提出新范式：遗忘不应仅“压制”目标内容，而应学习一条自然的遗忘轨迹——先输出连贯且不泄密的思维链，再给出稳定的拒绝式回答；据此提出 GUARD（引导式答案-推理蒸馏）方法，并引入 NFRS 指标衡量替代质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型会在最终答案前生成中间思维链（CoT），受保护事实或不当推理可能已在中途泄露。现有遗忘目标只压制目标内容或重定向内部表征，未规定“遗忘后应如何继续生成”，因此易出现虚假替代、边界错乱或重复输出等问题。

Method: 1) 将模型生成的不安全披露转化为“安全退出”轨迹（safe-exit trajectory）；2) 通过引导 token 对冻结的 LRM 做行为对齐；3) 将引导后的行为蒸馏进模型参数，实现参数级遗忘。同时提出 Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score（NFRS），从结构稳定性、流畅性以及是否存在无依据替代内容三个维度评估遗忘输出的替代质量，弥补仅看泄漏率的度量不足。

Result: 在 R-TOFU 以及基于 STAR-1 构造的有害意图设置上，GUARD 在两个常用蒸馏 LRM 上显著降低了不安全披露与隐私泄露，同时保持推理效用。

Conclusion: 论文表明 LRM 遗忘应聚焦“自然遗忘轨迹”的学习与替代质量评估；GUARD 与 NFRS 为该方向提供了可行方法与度量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [113] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReACT-TTS 两阶段框架，利用听者一秒预反应面部序列规划对话响应的情绪与韵律，再接入 Grad-TTS 完成语音生成，实验证明时序视觉线索优于纯文本条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音不仅依赖对话上下文，还取决于听者紧邻前的行为表现；现有方法多忽略听者的即时反馈动态，缺乏对预反应听者面部线索的显式建模。

Method: ReACT-TTS 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段以一秒听者预反应面部序列为条件，规划下一话语的情绪与韵律（含 Temporal 条件化与视觉变体消融）；第二阶段将预测的响应风格接入 Grad-TTS 骨干，实现端到端语音合成。

Result: 在严格 dyadic MELD 协议下，Temporal 条件化在十个随机种子上取得更高的宏平均 F1 与 VAD 一致性，准确率基本不变；消融表明时序建模在视觉变体中最佳，显式早-晚差异并不必要，正确听者反应平均优于循环错配。20 名语音研究者参与的情境适当性研究中，76% 偏好 Temporal，9% 偏好 Text-only，15% 无偏好。

Conclusion: 预反应听者动态可作为对话响应规划的互补线索，支持将听者即时面部行为纳入对话语音合成框架；代码已开源。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [114] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文以 TaxiGPT 为案例，指出行为失败未必意味着 Transformer 缺乏世界模型；通过机制分析与因果干预，发现模型实际表征了路口/街道、位置与目标罗盘，失败源于叠加路口特征间的干扰。作者提出“可供性打包”缓解误差，并给出机制指标比较不同训练阶段的世界建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常以行为失败推断 Transformer 缺乏连贯内部世界模型，但该推断可能混淆“表征是否忠实”与“行为是否成功”。作者希望从机制层面重新审视世界模型问题。

Method: 在 Manhattan 随机游走数据上训练 TaxiGPT；采用机制分析与因果干预，识别模型内部的路口、街道、位置跟踪和目标罗盘表征；分析失败来源；提出可供性打包（affordance packing）与机制指标。

Result: 模型并非没有世界模型：它能表征路口与街道、跟踪自身位置、利用目标罗盘导航。行为失败主要来自叠加的路口特征相互干扰，破坏内部地图中的定位。可供性打包将具有相同合法移动的路口表征分组，可限制此类错误后果。机制指标显示，世界建模能力在不同训练阶段逐步涌现。

Conclusion: 研究应把问题从“模型是否拥有世界模型”转向“机制化地研究其世界建模”，即考察模型表征环境并以此指导行为的多种相互作用能力。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [115] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并量化了急诊多模态预测中的“ECG Mirage”现象：视觉-语言模型看似具备多模态能力，但并未有效利用患者特异性ECG信息。通过匹配ECG、结果不一致错配ECG和无图像输入的对比，发现四个VLM在MDS-ED上匹配ECG无一致预测优势；随后采用冻结VLM主干的受限视觉提示调优（监督学习+条件直接偏好优化）进行缓解，提升平衡准确率并扩大匹配-错配性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊决策依赖病史、生命体征、实验室检查和心电图等异质临床信息。视觉-语言模型可联合处理这些模态，但强预测性能并不必然意味着模型真正使用了正确患者的ECG信息。作者将这种“表观多模态能力但缺乏对患者特异性ECG有效依赖”的失败模式定义为ECG Mirage，并区分ECG忽视与ECG混淆两种形式，以评估多模态临床预测中的真实模态利用情况。

Method: 作者设计对照评估：固定临床文本和预测目标，比较匹配ECG、结果不一致的错配ECG以及无图像输入三种条件，在MDS-ED数据集上评估四个VLM对ICU入住和临床恶化预测的表现。随后，在保持VLM主干冻结的前提下，训练四个受限视觉提示：先进行监督学习，再进行条件直接偏好优化（conditional direct preference optimisation），以增强模型对匹配ECG的依赖。

Result: 在四个VLM上，匹配ECG对ICU入住或临床恶化预测均未带来一致优势，表明存在ECG Mirage。经过视觉提示调优后，模型在ICU入住预测上达到70.6%平衡准确率，在临床恶化预测上达到67.5%；匹配ECG与错配ECG之间的性能差距分别扩大到约16.5和5.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究识别了多模态临床预测中的ECG Mirage现象，即模型性能可能掩盖其未有效利用患者特异性ECG信息的问题；同时提出视觉提示调优作为一种高效缓解策略，在不微调VLM主干的情况下增强ECG信息的有效利用。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [116] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在严格的模型选择协议下，简单统计流水线能在单变量时间序列异常检测中达到多强。方法用滑动窗口提取少量统计量，以传导式鲁棒MAD模型打分，并在调参集上按域选择特征子集；在TSB-AD-U上达到0.529 per-series VUS-PR，超过公开榜单最佳神经（0.45）和统计（0.44）方法，并接近最强预训练基础模型（差距0.06）。消融表明候选池决定上限，搜索策略是二阶因素；进一步用多模态LLM按域生成候选池，与手工池互补，并集选择提升至0.588，达到榜单最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格选择协议下，简单统计方法在单变量时间序列异常检测中的性能上限，并识别性能差异的主要来源：是聚合方式、特征选择策略，还是候选统计量池本身。

Method: 滑动窗口提取一小池统计特征；对每个窗口使用传导式鲁棒MAD模型进行异常打分；在留出调参集上按域选择特征子集；通过消融比较三种选择策略、后见oracle、聚合网格和候选池变化；最后用多模态LLM结合域内示例窗口生成候选统计量池，并在手工池与生成池的并集上做选择。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上达到0.529 per-series VUS-PR，高于公开榜单最佳神经（0.45）和统计（0.44）条目，距最强预训练基础模型0.06。消融中：选择策略与oracle使分数变动0.031，聚合网格变动0.096，候选池变动0.226。LLM生成池在匹配选择下与手工池相当，且覆盖不同域；在十二组生成器-种子对中，并集选择均优于生成池，流水线提升至0.588，匹配榜单最佳。

Conclusion: 候选统计量池是性能上限的主要决定因素，选择与聚合的影响相对次要。简单统计流水线在合适候选池与选择协议下可超过复杂神经网络方法；LLM生成候选池提供互补覆盖，并集选择进一步提升性能，达到公开榜单最佳水平。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [117] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIST：以基因组特征为查询、组织学基础模型上下文为键/值的注意力融合策略，用于多模态生存预测，在四个外部队列中提升C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSI）与基因组数据可提供互补预后信息，但现有融合方法在外部队列分布偏移和计算复杂度方面仍难以兼顾，尤其缺乏在编码早期进行跨模态交互的紧凑设计。

Method: MIST将基因组特征表示为token，并令其查询由病理基础模型提取的紧凑组织学上下文token，从而在生存预测前实现基因组引导的组织学注意力融合；训练目标结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码、WSI dropout，以及配对的WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠、肾、肺和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部队列评估中，MIST在主要比较中较标准融合基线提升了外部C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤结局预测策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [118] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EnterpriseVal 用例级评估系统，弥合公共基准与部署决策之间的测量鸿沟，并在全球银行三个工作流试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型已能产出专家认可的专业交付物，但多数企业 GenAI 项目缺乏可测业务效果，大量 agentic 项目预计被取消。作者认为核心是测量问题：公共基准回答“模型能做什么”，部署决策却需回答“该工作流是否适配、可靠、安全且值得扩展——在本企业数据与管控条件下”。

Method: EnterpriseVal 包含五部分：(i) 用例形式化与冻结的社会技术配置（模型、提示、检索、工具、护栏、人类监督），以自主性等级和后果层级共同决定评估强度；(ii) 覆盖保真、效用、效率、可靠性、保障与监督的指标目录；(iii) 通过预测驱动推断，将盲审专家判断与校准的 LLM-as-judge 评分结合的评分协议；(iv) 以可执行算法将带置信界的指标向量映射为 REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE 的两层阈值门；(v) 以审查者捕获率为测量参数的价值—风险模型。

Result: 在全球银行三个工作流开展试点。信用备忘录起草中，最佳模型人工评分引用精度达 88%、幻觉率 1.6%，对应门槛为 70% 和 5%；流程转换中，分析师精修工作量从估计的每文档 27.4 小时降至 2.9 小时。作者区分了已确立结果、试点证据、所提系统与开放假设，并给出完整验证所需实验。

Conclusion: EnterpriseVal 将企业级 GenAI 评估从模型能力转向用例部署就绪度，通过形式化配置、多维指标、混合评分、阈值决策与价值—风险建模，为 SCALE/CONDITIONAL/REJECT 提供可审计依据；其全面有效性仍需后续实验验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [119] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoRecLab：一个基于 Python 的自主推荐系统实验平台，可由自然语言研究想法自动推导实验需求、构建并验证原型，再迭代扩展为完整实验；结合 RAG 文档检索、静态类型验证与执行引导的树搜索。演示中自动完成了显式到隐式反馈转换研究，在 6 种算法 × 3 个数据集共 9 次运行中 8 次成功，单次成本约 $1（GPT-5.4-mini）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统（RecSys）研究以实证评估为核心，但从实验设计到可执行代码的转化仍高度依赖人工，过程繁琐且易出错，缺乏自动化、可靠且低成本的实验实现手段。

Method: AutoRecLab 为基于 Python 的自主 RecSys 实验平台：输入自然语言研究想法后，先推导显式实验需求，构建并验证原型，再迭代扩展为所要求的完整实验。其工作流融合三项关键技术——面向文档检索的检索增强生成（RAG）、静态类型验证，以及受执行结果引导的树搜索（execution-steered tree search）。

Result: 演示中，AutoRecLab 自主实现了显式到隐式反馈转换（explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion）研究。基线对比覆盖 6 种算法与 3 个数据集，9 次运行中 8 次成功，使用 GPT-5.4-mini 时平均每次运行成本约 1 美元。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将 RAG、静态类型验证与执行引导树搜索相结合，可在较低成本下实现推荐系统实验的端到端自动化，为 RecSys 实证研究提供可行的自主实验范式。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [120] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAVEL——一种面向交互式行人重识别的检索感知在线强化学习框架，通过完整问答-检索闭环的排序反馈优化提问策略，在 Interactive-PEDES 上实现五轮交互中渐进增强的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式检索在部分证据下是一个序贯信息获取问题：智能体需决定哪个问题能为下一轮检索更新提供最有用证据。现有系统通过模仿候选问答对的离线排序来训练这一决策，但问题价值实际由所获回答及对检索的下游影响决定；作者指出候选区分度与主观有用性对该目标仅构成弱监督。

Method: 提出 RAVEL：从监督式问题生成初始化，直接观测当前 Top-4 候选，并以完整“提问-回答-检索”闭环的排序反馈来优化提问策略，采用在线强化学习实现检索感知的提问决策。

Result: 在 Interactive-PEDES 上，RAVEL 在五轮交互中呈现逐步增强的检索性能；进一步分析表明，RAVEL 将提问预算重新分配至局部化的开放式属性问题，这类问题能提供更有效的检索证据，并在初始较难的查询上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 以检索排序反馈为奖励的在线强化学习可有效替代对离线问题排序的模仿学习，使提问更贴合证据获取与检索更新的真实目标，尤其改善困难查询的交互检索效果。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [121] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem 是一个数据驱动的长期对话记忆框架，在写入阶段将记忆组织为自配置、低重叠的语义视图，从而把语义解耦从检索时前移到写入时，使标准 top-K 检索即可获得聚焦证据，在 LoCoMo 与 PersonaMem 基准上提升了长程问答与个性化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在长程交互中需要长期记忆来维持一致性与个性化。现有记忆系统多采用固定粒度或静态模式（fixed granularities or static schemas），当偏好、事件、约束、时序更新等异构信息被嵌入同一混合表示时，会产生语义干扰（semantic interference），导致 top-K 检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排序靠后。

Method: 提出 AutoViewMem 框架：1) 从交互轨迹中发现候选语义视图；2) 选取紧凑且互补的视图集合；3) 以这些视图指导写入时的结构化抽取，生成带溯源（provenance-grounded）的记忆。采用“表示优先（representation-first）”设计，将语义解耦从检索时转移到写入时，使标准 top-K 相似度检索无需显式路由或迭代检索即可获取聚焦证据；并引入离线整合（offline consolidation）提升记忆的紧凑性与一致性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 PersonaMem 两个基准上，分别以 Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-14B 为骨干模型进行实验，AutoViewMem 在长程问答与个性化任务上优于强记忆基线，同时保持简单推理流程。

Conclusion: AutoViewMem 表明显式、自配置的语义视图组织能有效缓解异构记忆的语义干扰，写入时的结构化解耦可替代复杂的检索时处理，为长期记忆系统提供了一种兼顾效果与推理简洁性的方案。该方法在检索效率与记忆紧凑性方面具备优势。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对运动应激与跨会话变化下的心电（ECG）生物识别展开系统评估，提出基于孪生ResNet与晚期多导联融合的方法，在来自心肺运动试验的大规模ECG数据集及公开基准上验证，取得会话内静息至运动峰值EER 1.7%、CYBHi数据集3.9%的当前最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG包含个体特异性模式，可用于可靠的身份识别，并具备保护敏感心脏数据、作为自监督学习预text任务的潜力。然而，现有研究多局限于单会话、静息状态数据，对时间变化与生理变化下的鲁棒性缺乏充分检验。

Method: 构建来自心肺运动试验（CPET）的大规模ECG数据集；采用Siamese ResNet，并设计晚期多导联融合策略；使用运动与时间感知的评估协议，并在公开基准数据集上进行测试。

Result: 在生理与时间变异联合条件下进行了首次广泛评估：会话内静息至运动峰值的EER为1.7%；在CYBHi数据集上达到3.9%，为当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持存在一种对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏特征签名，表明ECG生物识别在现实变化条件下具有可行性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [123] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PIR（Probe of Internal Recognition），借鉴取证学中的隐藏信息测试（CIT），通过在模型内部状态中读取其对候选答案的识别反应，从而区分模型“不愿回答”与“无法回答”。在五个家族八个模型上，PIR能以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复模型所识别的正确答案，且在各种隐蔽行为（提示欺骗、训练式敷衍、密码锁定、电路破坏）下保持0.85–0.93的识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能掌握知识却不报告：它可能在能力评估中故意敷衍（sandbagging），或给出与内部知识相悖的答案。仅凭输出行为无法区分模型是在隐藏答案还是根本不知道。现有检测方法通常依赖诚实的参照模型或有标注的真实语料，缺乏无参照的检测手段，因此需要一种能从模型内部直接判断其是否“认得”正确答案的方法。

Method: 作者类比取证学的隐藏信息测试（Concealed Information Test）：向嫌疑人呈现真实细节与合理干扰项，通过对其识别项产生更强反应来判断其是否知情。PIR将这一范式搬到模型内部：给定问题及其候选答案，从模型的内部状态（如隐层激活）中读取模型认为哪个候选是正确的。该方法无需诚实参照模型，也无需标注的真实语料，属于reference-free方法。作者在来自五个家族（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）的八个模型上评估，并测试了多种隐蔽形式：提示性欺骗、训练式敷衍、外部密码锁定检查点、电路破坏检查点。

Result: PIR在八个模型上以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复模型所识别的答案，显著高于未知项基线（0.28–0.40）和随机水平（0.25）。在各类隐蔽情形下识别率保持在0.85–0.93。当模型隐藏一个已知答案时，识别率仍然很高；当遗忘（unlearning）真正移除了知识时，识别率下降到模型从未见过该问题的水平。该信号具有因果性，且能提供黑盒行为线索之外的信息，并可从多项选择题推广到自由生成任务。

Conclusion: PIR能够区分“不愿回答”与“不能回答”的模型，为敷衍行为审计（sandbagging audits）和遗忘验证（unlearning verification）提供支持。其信号具有因果性、不依赖参照模型或标注语料，并可从选择题扩展到开放生成，是一种可用于模型可信度与知识审计的内部识别探针。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [124] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CodeMidas 提出一种以源代码为唯一任务输入、将已有代码库中已实现功能转化为可执行强化学习环境的智能体流水线；构建了覆盖 23 种语言、15 个技术领域、5,545 个任务的训练数据集，训练 MiMo-V2.5 后在多个编码基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练具备编码能力的智能体需要多样且带可靠验证器的任务。现有方法多依赖 issues、commits 等开发产物来提取任务，任务范围受限，难以规模化构建 RL 环境。为更好地扩展强化学习环境，需要一种仅依赖源代码即可生成任务与验证器的方法。

Method: CodeMidas 是一个智能体流水线，仅以源码作为任务特定输入，将代码库中已实现功能转化为可执行 RL 环境。其分配智能体计算到环境构建各阶段：1）智能体探索已实现功能，形成行为规范；2）基于原始代码执行构造测试；3）通过执行检查和重复解回滚（repeated solution rollouts）验证并过滤候选任务。

Result: 构建的数据集包含来自 3,185 个开源代码库的 5,545 个训练任务，覆盖 23 种编程语言和 15 个技术领域。使用 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5 后，在五个多样化基准上均提升：问题修复 DeepSWE +11.7%，整程序构建 ProgramBench +17%，终端任务 Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%。消融显示增加高质量训练任务数量可提升性能；轨迹分析显示 RL 训练后的智能体表现出更多代码库探索与更多样化自验证行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，源代码可作为构建 RL 环境的可扩展基础，能够提升编码智能体在多种软件任务上的表现。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [125] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向专业平面设计的持续自适应框架：冻结前沿模型通过230+工具操作设计软件，外部自然语言程序记忆积累并优化可复用设计流程；无需权重更新或人工标注，经五轮迭代将技能库从76增至139，显著提升执行成功率与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长时程智能体任务，结构化可编辑产物由大量相互依赖动作产生，但结果缺乏可靠程序化评估标准，导致难以通过传统监督或强化学习持续优化。

Method: 冻结前沿模型操作专业设计软件（230+工具）；外部程序记忆以自然语言技能形式积累与精炼设计流程。通过“拓宽”获取未覆盖子任务的新流程，“深化”依据自身成功/失败执行修订现有流程；匹配回放门控仅接受能修复失败且不损害已成功案例的变更。

Result: 在1,406个真实用户简报和1,869条自动评分轨迹上完成五轮迭代，无权重更新、无人工标签，技能库由76条文档衍生技能增至139条；Claude-Sonnet-4上GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%升至99.3%，生成质量提升11.99分；在四个专业设计基准上对无技能智能体胜率分别为61.8%和67.6%。在200个用户流量基准留出简报上，单独拓宽/深化胜率49.4%/48.6%，二者结合达58.5%（p=0.025），表明机制组合有效。

Conclusion: 程序记忆为在噪声大、不可验证反馈下实现智能体持续自适应提供了实用路径，且拓宽与深化机制具有互补增益。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [WM-VS: Progress-Aligned World Models for Closed-Loop Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20892)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yixuan Zhou,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WM-VS：面向闭环视觉伺服的目标中心、进度对齐世界模型框架，用DINOv2目标区域对应定义四维伺服坐标，并分两阶段对齐动作条件潜转移与训练反应式关节速度策略，实现仅RGB、无在线轨迹优化的闭环纠偏与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环视觉伺服中的预测往往只判断动作是否“合理”，却不能判断该动作是否真正减小任务误差，形成“预测—控制错配”（prediction-control mismatch）。因此需要一种能预测动作后果并朝误差收缩方向推进的世界模型与控制框架。

Method: WM-VS以目标区域为中心构建进度对齐世界模型：离线使用DINOv2目标区域对应关系，定义包含平移、尺度与面内旋转的带符号四维伺服坐标；Stage 1将动作条件潜空间转移与该伺服坐标对齐；Stage 2冻结世界模型，训练反应式关节速度策略，结合动作模仿、动作后果监督，以及偏向误差收缩的短时想象rollout。部署时仅用RGB，反应式执行，不做在线轨迹优化。

Result: 在真实7-DoF eye-to-hand系统上，WM-VS在30/30次试验中将角点RMSE降至不超过初值的10%，并在最终有效帧仍有25/30（83.33%）保持该标准；移除未来误差对齐后保持率降至26.67%。学习到的进度信号与未用于训练或控制的外部AprilTag角点误差一致，平均Spearman rho=0.8778。无需重训练，两个未见3D目标的平移误差分别降低86.48%和90.27%，旋转误差分别降低70.01%和65.70%。

Conclusion: 结果将“进度对齐的动作后果”与可重复的闭环校正及跨目标迁移联系起来，表明世界模型预测应对齐控制进度而非仅追求动作合理性；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Closed-loop visual servoing requires predictions that indicate whether an action reduces task error, not only whether the action is plausible. We call this gap the prediction-control mismatch and introduce WM-VS, a target-centric progress-aligned world-model framework for closed-loop visual servoing. Offline target-region DINOv2 correspondences define a signed four-dimensional servo coordinate for translation, scale, and in-plane rotation. Stage 1 aligns action-conditioned latent transitions with this coordinate; Stage 2 freezes the world model and trains a reactive joint-velocity policy with action imitation, consequence supervision, and short imagined rollouts that favor error contraction. Deployment is RGB-only and reactive, without online trajectory optimization. On a real 7-DoF eye-to-hand system, WM-VS reaches a corner RMSE no larger than 10 percent of its initial value in 30/30 trials and retains this criterion at the final valid frame in 25/30 (83.33 percent). Removing future-error alignment reduces retention to 26.67 percent. The learned progress signal agrees with an external AprilTag corner error not used for training or control (mean Spearman rho = 0.8778). Without retraining, two unseen 3D targets achieve translation-error reductions of 86.48 percent and 90.27 percent and rotation-error reductions of 70.01 percent and 65.70 percent. These results link progress-aligned action consequences to repeated closed-loop correction and transfer. Code and data will be released as open source.

</details>


### [127] [AeRove: A Compact Bimodal Aerial-Terrestrial Drone with Rapid Bistable Reconfiguration for Close-Range Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20965)
*Caleb Polillio,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双模态空中-地面机器人 AeRove，利用桨叶保护罩作为滚轮沿管道滚动，并以弹簧加载双稳态机构在200 ms内无持续驱动功耗地切换地面/飞行模式；将飞行保留用于越障、地面滚动用于连续行进，电流消耗较连续飞行降低14倍（0.7 A vs. 10 A），估计行程由144 m提升至2057 m。在51 cm直径钢管上完成直道/弯道导航、越障及模拟泄漏标记检测；CO2传感实验表明栖停检测响应显著大于悬停，飞行中底部桨叶进气配置比外伸探头检测更快更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无人机在近距离管道巡检中面临悬停功耗高、气流敏感以及螺旋桨尾流干扰气体传感等问题，难以兼顾续航与检测性能。

Method: 设计紧凑型双模态空中-地面机器人 AeRove：以螺旋桨保护罩作为滚轮沿管道滚动；采用弹簧加载双稳态机构，在200 ms内完成地面与飞行模式重构，且维持任一状态无需持续执行器功耗；收敛式保护罩几何提升实测推力效率。运动策略为地面滚动连续行进、飞行仅用于越障跳跃。开展51 cm直径钢管自主试验，验证直道与弯道导航、越障和模拟巡检标记泄漏检测；另进行独立CO2传感实验，比较栖停与飞行工况，并对比底部桨叶进气与外伸探头配置。

Result: 地面滚动相比连续飞行电流消耗降低14倍（0.7 A vs. 10 A），估计行程由144 m延长至2057 m；51 cm直径钢管自主试验实现直道/弯道导航、越障和模拟泄漏标记检测。CO2传感实验中，栖停检测的浓度响应显著大于悬停；飞行时底部桨叶进气配置借助桨叶诱导气流，较外伸探头具有更快且更强的气体检测响应。代码与设计以CC-BY许可发布。

Conclusion: AeRove 通过空中-地面双模态运动与无持续功耗的快速模式切换，有效降低巡检功耗并提升行程；栖停检测与桨叶诱导气流进气可改善气体传感响应，适用于近距离管道巡检。

Abstract: Close-proximity pipeline inspection is challenging for standard drones due to high hovering power consumption, airflow sensitivity, and propeller wash interference with gas sensing. To address this, we present AeRove, a compact bimodal aerial-terrestrial robot. AeRove uses its propeller guards as wheels to roll along pipes and employs a spring-loaded bistable mechanism to reconfigure between ground and flight modes in 200 ms without continuous actuator power to maintain either state. The converging propeller-guard geometry also improves measured thrust efficiency. By reserving flight for obstacle hopping and using ground rolling for continuous traversal, current draw is reduced 14x compared to continuous flight (0.7 A vs. 10 A), extending estimated travel distance from 144 m to 2057 m. Autonomous trials on a 51 cm diameter steel pipe demonstrated navigation on straight and curved sections, obstacle jumping, and leak detection of simulated inspection markers. Separate CO2 sensing experiments evaluated gas-detection performance under perched and aerial operating conditions. Perched inspection produced a substantially larger concentration response than hovering under the tested conditions. During flight, an underbody propeller-intake configuration produced a faster and stronger gas-detection response than an extended probe by leveraging propeller-induced airflow. Code and designs are released under the CC-BY license.

</details>


### [128] [Shake to Learn: Dynamic Interrogation of Hidden Object Physics for Robotic Manipulation with Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20970)
*Wen Sin Lor,Jun Wang,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“shake-to-learn”机械询问范式：以折纸启发软机械臂作为物理储备池计算机，抓取物体后施加固定抖动激励，利用耦合系统的衰减响应经轻量线性读出推断视觉不可见的物体物理属性（如质心COM），并完成COM方向、距离推断及再抓取引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中许多关键物理属性（如密封物体的质心位置、内部内容物）无法通过视觉等常规传感直接获取，只有在提升、摇晃或动态扰动后才可能显现，因此需要新的交互式感知与学习模态。

Method: 采用折纸启发的软机械臂构建物理储备池计算机；抓取物体后在基座施加固定抖动输入，通过相机跟踪或嵌入式传感器记录ringdown衰减响应；由于输入跨试验恒定，隐藏物体属性被编码进机器人—物体耦合动力学中；再用轻量线性读出解码这些动力学，并开发ringdown响应的动态摘要表示以提高预测精度。

Result: 该软臂储备池框架完成三项难度递增任务：推断物体隐藏质心的方向、推断质心到抓取点的距离、利用推断出的质心信息引导后续再抓取；动态摘要表示进一步提升了预测准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，shake-to-learn机械询问可将短暂物理交互转化为关于隐藏物体属性的可操作线索，为下游机器人操作提供有前景的感知与学习策略。

Abstract: Many physical properties relevant to robotic manipulation are hidden from vision. A sealed object, for example, may reveal little about its center of mass (COM) or internal contents until it is lifted, shaken, or otherwise dynamically perturbed. This study shows that such interactions can enable a new modality of robotic perception and learning, in which interaction-induced dynamic responses are used to infer object physics that is inaccessible to conventional sensing. We implement this idea using an origami-inspired soft robotic arm that functions as a physical reservoir computer. After grasping an object, the arm is excited by a fixed shaking input at its base, and the resulting ringdown response is recorded through either camera tracking or embedded sensors. Because the input is held constant across trials, hidden object properties, such as the COM position, are encoded through their effect on the dynamics of the coupled robot-object system. A lightweight linear readout can then decode these dynamics to recover interpretable information about the hidden object physics. Using this framework, the soft robotic arm reservoir completed three tasks of increasing difficulty: inferring the orientation of the object's hidden COM, inferring the COM distance from the grasp point, and using the inferred COM information to guide a subsequent regrasp. We further develop a dynamic summary representation of the ringdown response that improves prediction accuracy. Together, these results establish shake-to-learn mechanical interrogation as a promising strategy for robotic systems to convert brief physical interactions into actionable cues about hidden object properties for downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [129] [ForeTac-VLA: A Forecasting-Based Tactile-Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20980)
*Zhengyu Tao,Xin Li,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ForeTac-VLA，一种基于预测的触觉-视觉-语言融合模型，通过预测未来触觉状态来引导动作生成，在四类真实接触密集型操作任务上平均成功率达95%，显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中表现强大，但在接触密集型环境中依赖视觉感知存在局限：关键的物理交互状态往往无法从视觉中观测到。现有触觉增强VLA方法虽利用观测到的触觉反馈改善了物理接地，但大多是被动反应式的，未显式建模接触如何演化。

Method: 1）将近期触觉观测编码为时序表示，并通过双向交叉注意力与视觉-语言特征融合；2）引入基于Transformer的预测模块，预测多步未来触觉状态，使模型能对已观测与预期接触进行联合推理；3）将融合后的多模态表示与预测的未来触觉状态输入VLA主干以条件化动作生成；4）采用从真实值到预测（ground-truth-to-prediction）的课程学习策略，在早期预测不可靠时稳定训练。

Result: 在四类真实世界接触密集型操作任务上，ForeTac-VLA平均成功率达95%，较微调的VLA模型高出36.25个百分点，较最先进的触觉增强VLA基线高出逾22个百分点；在低照度与视觉杂乱条件下仍保持强性能。

Conclusion: 显式预测未来触觉状态并对观测与预期接触进行联合推理，可有效提升VLA模型在接触密集及视觉受限环境下的鲁棒性与操作成功率，验证了预测式触觉融合范式的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on visual perception limits robustness in contact-rich environments, where critical physical interaction states may not be visually observable. Existing tactile-enhanced VLA methods improve physical grounding using observed tactile feedback, but most remain largely reactive rather than explicitly modeling how contact may evolve. Therefore, we propose ForeTac-VLA, a forecasting-based tactile-vision-language fusion model that predicts future tactile states to guide action generation. Specifically, ForeTac-VLA encodes recent tactile observations into temporal representations and integrates them with vision-language features through bidirectional cross-attention. Further, a transformer-based forecasting module predicts multi-step future tactile states, enabling the model to reason jointly over observed and anticipated contact. Finally, the fused multimodal representations and predicted future tactile states are fed into the VLA backbone to condition action generation. To stabilize training, a ground-truth-to-prediction curriculum is employed when early forecasts are unreliable. Across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ForeTac-VLA achieves an average success rate of 95%, outperforming the fine-tuned VLA model by 36.25 percentage points and state-of-the-art tactile-enhanced VLA baselines by over 22 percentage points. ForeTac-VLA also maintains strong performance under low-illumination and visually cluttered conditions. Video demonstrations can be found on https://foretac-vla.github.io/

</details>


### [130] [PIVOT: Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability for Field Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20983)
*Aoran Jiao,Wenda Zhao,Hshmat Sahak,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIVOT：一种物理知情的视觉语言越野可通行性导航系统，用VLM语义推理增强传统几何规划，并通过两级架构兼顾性能与效率。在约6.4 km混合地形闭环试验中，将自主性从59.6%提升至97.0%，人工干预从11次降至3次，MDBI从69.2 m提升至412.9 m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野移动机器人需要在非结构化、几何复杂环境中安全可靠导航。传统基于几何的地形评估计算快，但在非结构化环境中往往过于保守；因此需要引入语义理解，同时将VLM预测与真实物理测量对齐，并保持计算效率。

Method: 提出PIVOT系统，将基于VLM的语义推理用于增强传统几何规划。为物理接地，量化VLM预测的通行能量代价、机器人振动和轮滑与真实测量之间的相关性，并据此加权构建统一可通行性分数。导航采用两级架构：以几何规划为常态模式，仅当几何模式无法找到路径时才触发语义重规划。在混合地形路线上进行5次重复闭环试验，总里程约6.4 km。

Result: 与仅几何导航相比，系统整体自主性由59.6%提升至97.0%，人工干预由11次降至3次，平均干预间隔距离（MDBI）由69.2 m提升至412.9 m。

Conclusion: 物理接地的VLM地形评估能够显著扩展自主导航能力，突破仅依赖几何信息的局限，同时通过保留几何规划作为常态模式维持高效规划。

Abstract: Terrain assessment is a critical capability for off-road mobile robots, enabling safe and reliable navigation through unstructured and geometrically complex environments. Conventional geometry-based terrain assessment is fast to compute but often overly conservative in unstructured environments. We present PIVOT: a Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability navigation system that augments conventional geometry-based planning with vision-language-model (VLM)-based semantic reasoning for field robots. To physically ground this assessment, we quantify how strongly the VLM's predicted traversal energy cost, robot vibration, and wheel slip correlate with real-world measurements and introduce a unified traversability score that weights each modality by its prediction-measurement correlation. For efficiency, we design a two-level navigation architecture that retains geometry-based planning as the nominal mode and invokes semantic replanning only when that mode fails to find a path. Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [131] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转式FMCW雷达的逐方位多普勒速度能否提升车辆检测与跟踪性能，并提出自动标注流程与多普勒速度先验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转式FMCW雷达因对恶劣天气鲁棒且具备360°视场，在自动驾驶感知中日益流行；近期扫描雷达还能输出逐方位多普勒速度。但现有带动态目标真值标签的数据集缺乏多普勒旋转雷达数据，因此作者希望验证多普勒速度对旋转雷达车辆检测与跟踪的实际增益。

Method: 检测方面，先估计自车运动，以消除雷达图像中的多普勒距离畸变，再输入网络；跟踪方面，提出一种逐车辆速度估计方法，并将其作为跟踪器运动模型的先验。为获得训练数据，构建自动标注流水线：用集成微调后的现成激光雷达检测器标注Boreas Road Trip数据集全部643 km，再将检测结果迁移到雷达，使用超过250 km车辆密集序列作为真值训练数据，并训练与评估两个先进检测器。

Result: 多普勒去畸变可将检测平均精度（mAP）最多提升2.37个百分点；多普勒速度先验相比零速度初始化基线，将多目标跟踪精度（MOTA）提升13.68个百分点，并达到使用真值速度先验所得MOTA的99.7%。

Conclusion: 多普勒速度测量能够有效提升旋转式雷达的车辆检测与跟踪性能；自动标注流水线可在无人工动态标签的情况下构建大规模训练数据；基于多普勒的速度先验接近使用真值速度先验的跟踪效果。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [132] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证了面向无人机周界防御的机载视觉感知与控制框架 SCOUT，在真实飞行中实现实时目标检测、关联、滤波与闭环拦截控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的无人机快速增多，周界防御亟需抗干扰、不依赖外部基础设施的反无人机系统。现有方案常依赖地面站或外部传感与计算，难以满足机载实时闭环防御需求。

Method: SCOUT 是集成于 ROS 的机载感知与控制框架，直接在防御无人机上完成视觉检测、目标关联、航迹滤波和控制指令生成。感知管线采用 TensorRT 加速的无人机检测、ByteTrack 关联以及轻量级航迹保持状态机；状态机抑制目标突变跳变，并在检测短暂退化时维持短时目标连续性，从而减少误检或目标切换导致的控制不稳定。

Result: 通过集成硬件部署执行平面“守门”拦截策略进行评测。防御无人机跟踪来袭目标并调整运动，以在受保护边界附近保持阻挡构型。真实飞行结果表明，SCOUT 在实时机载检测速率下，92.2% 的帧保持有效目标检测。

Conclusion: 结果证明了面向无人机周界防御的视觉跟踪与闭环控制的可行性，并提供了端到端周界防御演示视频。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. https://figshare.com/s/497befc033091fc0b84f

</details>


### [133] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究机器人在路径上遇到临时障碍时的序贯导航决策问题，将其建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），提出基于POMCP的信念空间规划器SPARROW，可在线学习障碍清除的生存模型，并在仿真与实物机器人上相比OSCAR降低12–26%和20.5%的平均到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人规划路径上可能出现临时障碍，机器人需在等待障碍清除、改道绕行或主动获取更多障碍信息之间做出序贯决策。现有方法（如基于生存分析的OSCAR）难以在部分可观测条件下同时权衡动作代价、信息获取价值与未来阻塞风险，因此需要更优的信念空间规划方法。

Method: 将临时障碍下的图导航形式化为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POSMDP）；提出SPARROW，一种基于部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划（POMCP）的信念空间规划器，搜索通行、观测和有限时长等待动作，并维护关于潜在障碍类别与清除时间的粒子信念；在线学习类别条件的生存模型，同时利用清除观测与机器人在清除前改道产生的右删失样本；通过生成模型模拟动作展开过程中的障碍到达与清除，以考虑替代路径上的阻塞；引入学习价值（value-of-learning）准则，权衡采集带标签生存数据的即时成本与未来导航遗憾的期望降低。

Result: 在两个仿真图和多种障碍类别设置下，SPARROW相比同问题的近期生存分析方法OSCAR将平均到达时间（time-to-goal）降低12–26%；在物理移动机器人上，SPARROW相比OSCAR降低20.5%的平均到达时间，并能随环境条件变化选择性地观测、等待和改道。

Conclusion: SPARROW通过在信念空间中进行POMCP规划、在线学习右删失生存模型并引入学习价值准则，有效解决了临时障碍下的序贯导航问题，在仿真和真实机器人上均优于OSCAR，验证了主动信息获取与等待/改道联合决策的有效性。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文验证了在低于1万美元的开源AUV平台上，仅使用消费级立体相机、MEMS IMU和深度传感器，即可在完全无约束的6-DOF水下环境中实现可用的VI-SLAM，并通过传感器融合优化降低对DVL、USBL等昂贵工业级传感器的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AUV通常依赖DVL、USBL等昂贵传感器套件，成本高昂，限制了先进海洋机器人从专业实验室向更广泛科研与爱好者群体推广。因此，需要验证低成本平台能否实现稳健、高质量的水下导航与定位。

Method: 基于价格低于10,000美元的开源AUV，搭建低成本VI-SLAM实验平台；评估立体相机、MEMS IMU和深度传感器在完全无约束6-DOF水下环境中的表现；分析现成SLAM软件包的有效性，并提出传感器融合优化方法，以缓解无缆水下作业面临的视觉与物理挑战。

Result: 结果表明，使用消费级低成本传感器即可获得可用的SLAM解决方案，为 demanding、实时海洋任务中的性能预期提供了基准，同时无需承担工业级硬件的财务门槛。

Conclusion: 低成本VI-SLAM具有普及潜力，可使高质量水下导航面向更广泛用户，推动海洋机器人从专业实验室走向大众化研究和业余应用。摘要未给出具体定量指标，如定位精度、漂移率或计算资源消耗。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [135] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Feedback 提出一种双时间尺度架构，将低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈相结合：保留扩散去噪的最后一步作为轻量级反馈接口，在执行每个动作前用最新观测进行实时校正，从而在保持扩散规划器表达力的同时实现实时动作修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的动作生成器擅长建模时间连贯的动作块，但推理后通常以开环方式执行整个动作块，导致在物体移动、接触变化或场景演化时缺乏响应能力。目标是在不重跑完整视觉-语言扩散模型的前提下，兼顾扩散规划的表达力与实时反馈响应能力。

Method: 提出双时间尺度架构：低频侧使用扩散规划器生成动作块，高频侧引入视觉反馈。核心设计是不在推理时完全去噪整个动作块，而是将最终去噪步骤保留为轻量级反馈接口，使每个动作在执行前都能利用最新观测进行校正。

Result: 在静态 LIBERO 任务上与 GR00T 持平；在动态仿真任务上将平均成功率从 27.5% 提升至 85.0%；在真实机器人任务上将平均成功率从 51% 提升至 73%。

Conclusion: VLA-Feedback 通过保留扩散去噪的最后一步作为反馈接口，实现了无需重跑完整扩散模型的动作级实时校正，在静态任务中保持性能的同时显著提升了动态场景与真实机器人任务的成功率，验证了低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈结合的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback.
