To address this problem, we introduce Predictive Sensorimotor Representation (PSR) learning, a framework that learns a hierarchy of predictive representations from multimodal sensorimotor signals and integrates them into the action stream of a visuomotor policy. Specifically, during a pretraining stage, a multimodal Transformer is trained to learn a hierarchy of predictive representations by jointly forecasting future interaction dynamics. The learned hierarchy subsequently augments the action stream, enabling the resulting policy to exploit contact-relevant cues at multiple depths. We further instantiate PSR within a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model, resulting in PSR-VLA, and evaluate it on six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that PSR-VLA achieves 91.7% overall success, improving over $π_{0.5}$, ForceVLA-$π_{0.5}$, and ForceVLA2-$π_{0.5}$ by 30.0, 22.5, and 19.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed PSR for force-aware, contact-rich manipulation. Videos of the tasks and stability tests are available at https://psr-vla.pages.dev/.

</details>


### [204] [CRISP: Contact-Rich Robotic Simulation Platform with Extensive Geometries and Contact Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21761)
*Somang Lee,Sunkyung Park,Jinhee Yun,Seoki An,Dongjun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRISP 是一个面向紧公差机器人操作等复杂多接触场景的高保真物理仿真平台，支持多样几何表示、基于优化的精确碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解器，并在与前沿平台的对比以及复杂操作任务中验证了其物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真要达到高物理保真度，需要兼具表达力强的几何与接触建模，以及通过鲁棒碰撞检测与接触求解器实现的精确数值求解。然而现有仿真器往往受限于几何表示支持不足、接触交互建模过于简化，或其数值方法的精度与鲁棒性本身存在固有约束，难以胜任接触密集且尖锐的交互场景。

Method: 作者提出 CRISP（Contact-RIch Simulation Platform）：一方面支持多种几何表示，并采用基于优化的精确碰撞检测；另一方面将接触建模与鲁棒的增广拉格朗日（augmented Lagrangian）接触求解器相结合。该集成方案可在复杂几何间高效、一致地检测接触信息，并在不引入有问题松弛的前提下准确求解多接触约束。

Result: 作者将 CRISP 的物理保真度与当前最先进的仿真平台进行了对比验证，并通过复杂机器人操作场景展示了其能力；同时该平台已开源发布（https://github.com/INRoL/crisp）。

Conclusion: CRISP 通过多样几何表示、优化式碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解的整合，实现了对接触密集与尖锐交互的高效、准确仿真，为复杂多接触机器人操作提供了高保真仿真工具，并已公开可用。

Abstract: We present CRISP (Contact-RIch Simulation Platform), a high-fidelity physics engine tailored for complex multi-contact simulations such as tight-tolerance robotic manipulation. Achieving high physical fidelity in robotic simulation requires both expressive modeling of geometry and contact interactions, as well as accurate numerical resolution via robust collision detection and contact solvers. However, existing simulators often either rely on limited support for geometric representations and simplified modeling of contact interactions, or employ numerical resolution methods whose accuracy or robustness is inherently constrained. Accordingly, we develop a new simulator that supports diverse geometric representations with accurate optimization-based collision detection, and combines contact modeling with robust augmented Lagrangian-based contact solvers. This integration enables efficient and consistent detection of contact information across complex geometries while accurately resolving multi-contact constraints without problematic relaxations, which is essential for simulating contact-intensive and sharp interactions. We validate the physical fidelity of our simulator against state-of-the-art platforms and further demonstrate its capabilities through complex robotic manipulation scenarios. CRISP is publicly available at https://github.com/INRoL/crisp.

</details>


### [205] [Scaling Vision-Language Reward Learning for Robot Manipulation in Parallel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21767)
*Lobna Joualy,Eric Demeester,Nikolaos Tsiogkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID通过GPU并行rollout、自适应策略更新、单请求偏好标注、自动奖励稳定和代表性图像采样，显著加速并降低VLM偏好奖励学习的训练成本与API调用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）可替代人工标注者进行基于偏好的奖励学习，但顺序API请求与单一环境数据采集导致训练速度慢、成本高。

Method: 提出RAPID（Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity），将GPU并行rollout与数据感知策略更新结合，并集成单请求偏好标注、自动奖励稳定化与代表性图像采样。在IsaacLab的五个Franka Panda操作任务上评估各组件。

Result: 并行rollout与自适应更新使匹配两阶段提示下的平均运行时间从9.18小时降至3.13小时；启用全部RAPID组件后，训练用时1.15小时，每轮API调用从19,840降至896，最终成功率从86.3%提升至98.7%，实现8.0倍端到端加速和95.5%的API使用减少。使用Gemma 3 12B与GPT-4.1 mini的离线评估表明，单请求提示降低了两种模型的标注延迟与成本。

Conclusion: RAPID能有效提升VLM偏好奖励学习的训练效率、降低API依赖并提高任务成功率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can replace human annotators in preference-based reward learning, but sequential API requests and single-environment data collection make training slow and costly. We present RAPID (Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity), a system that couples GPU-parallel rollout with data-aware policy updates, single-request preference labeling, automatic reward stabilization, and representative image sampling. We evaluate these components on five Franka Panda manipulation tasks in IsaacLab. Parallel rollout and adaptive updates provide the first substantial reduction in training time: under matched two-stage prompting, mean runtime falls from 9.18 to 3.13 hours. With all RAPID components enabled, training completes in 1.15 hours using 896 rather than 19,840 API calls per run, and aggregate final success rises from 86.3\% to 98.7\%. This represents an 8.0$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 95.5\% reduction in API usage. An offline evaluation with Gemma~3 12B and GPT-4.1 mini demonstrates that single-request prompting reduces labeling latency and cost across both models. Code is available at: https://github.com/rapid-vlm/rapid-vlm-rl.

</details>


### [206] [TRACE: Coverage Path Planning for Unknown Environments Using Hierarchical Coverage Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21777)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种基于分层覆盖树的在线覆盖路径规划算法，通过增量维护全局巡回并局部细化，实现未知环境的实时覆盖，在覆盖率、路径长度、重叠率与转向次数上优于六种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知环境下的实时覆盖任务，在线覆盖路径规划（CPP）面临环境逐步揭示、剩余未覆盖空间被新发现的障碍物与已覆盖单元分割成多个不连通区域等挑战，导致全局重规划计算负担大、覆盖进程不一致。本文旨在提出一种能够高效应对空间动态分裂、降低重规划开销并保持覆盖连续性的在线 CPP 算法。

Method: TRACE 建立在分层覆盖树之上，用于全局表征未覆盖空间的连通性演化。当新障碍物与已覆盖单元使剩余未覆盖空间碎片化为不连通区域时，算法递归扩展相应树节点以显式表示这些区域并组织后续覆盖规划。基于更新后的树，维护增量式全局巡回以引导覆盖过程，仅对受影响部分进行局部细化，同时保留未变区域的访问顺序，从而降低全局重规划的计算负担。在全局巡回引导下，局部规划器生成往复式覆盖路径，并切换至全局巡回感知规划以高效完成目标区域。

Result: 理论分析建立了 TRACE 的计算复杂度与完全覆盖性质，并推导出增量全局巡回细化的近似界。通过大规模高保真仿真与移动机器人实机实验进行评估，与六种现有 CPP 方法对比，在覆盖时间、路径长度、重叠率和转向次数方面均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: TRACE 通过分层覆盖树与增量全局巡回的结合，有效解决了未知环境中空间碎片化带来的在线覆盖难题，在保证完全覆盖与可分析复杂度的同时显著提升覆盖效率，验证了其在实际机器人平台上的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel online coverage path planning (CPP) algorithm, called TRACE, for real-time coverage of unknown environments. TRACE is built upon a hierarchical coverage tree that provides a global representation of the evolving connectivity of the uncovered space. As the environment is incrementally revealed and covered, newly discovered obstacles and covered cells may fragment the remaining uncovered space into disconnected regions. TRACE recursively expands the corresponding tree nodes to explicitly represent these regions and organize them for subsequent coverage planning. Based on the updated tree, an incremental global tour is maintained to guide the coverage process. TRACE locally refines only the affected portions while preserving the visiting order of unchanged regions, thereby reducing the computational burden of global replanning and maintaining a consistent coverage progression. Guided by the global tour, a local planner generates back-and-forth coverage paths and switches to global-tour-aware planning to efficiently complete the target regions. Theoretical analysis establishes the computational complexity and complete coverage property of TRACE, and derives an approximation bound for the incremental global tour refinement. The performance of TRACE is evaluated through extensive high-fidelity simulations and real-robot experiments using a mobile robot. Comparative evaluations against six existing CPP methods demonstrate significant improvements in coverage time, path length, overlap ratio, and number of turns.

</details>


### [207] [Compact but Moving: Intervention-Relevant Geometry in Recurrent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21787)
*Yuming Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究"紧凑干预"进入高维循环世界模型后的命运。先前工作发现一次性反事实速度干预对应一个checkpoint专属的秩4入口子空间，但该修正会在自主rollout中迅速离开该固定子空间。作者发现，将入口方向沿事实性循环Jacobian链传播得到的低秩像，仍能捕获大部分非线性修正；用切空间预测的修正重启可保留可观的反事实未来函数。该"传播/功能"模式在结构化GRU及参数匹配的LSTM上复现。作者进一步刻画了有限时域"未来响应算子"，发现patch会将其主导未来敏感方向移向匹配的原生反事实组织。结论：紧凑干预结构可作为随状态变化的局部几何嵌入高维循环动力学中持续存在，但不意味着存在固定或动态封闭的低维状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的世界模型即使循环状态是高维的，也可能拥有紧凑的干预结构；但干预一旦进入模型后如何演化并不清楚。作者希望在一个可控的循环世界模型中，厘清"紧凑修正"进入高维循环状态后的传播与功能命运，判断其是否对应固定或封闭的低维子空间。

Method: 在可控循环世界模型中，利用先前识别的秩4入口接口进行一次性反事实速度干预；通过将入口方向沿事实性循环Jacobian链进行切空间传播，构造低秩像来预测修正；在结构化GRU与参数匹配LSTM上验证；并定义有限时域未来响应算子，在注册方向面板上对扰动效果排序；另设全幅度实验考察事实端点切残差与响应重构。

Result: 修正会迅速离开固定入口子空间，但沿Jacobian链传播的低秩像仍捕获大部分非线性修正，切空间预测修正重启可保留可观的反事实未来函数；该模式在多个独立训练的结构化GRU及参数匹配LSTM上复现。未来响应算子能准确排序有限扰动效果；patching使主导未来敏感方向向匹配的原生反事实组织靠拢；全幅度实验在3个checkpoint中的2个发现显著事实端点切残差并支持响应重构。

Conclusion: 紧凑的干预结构可以在高维循环动力学中作为"移动的、随状态变化的局部几何"持续存在，而不必意味着存在一个固定或动态封闭的低维状态。这为理解世界模型中紧凑修正的持久性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Learned world models may have compact interventions even when their recurrent state is high-dimensional, but it is unclear what happens to such a correction after it enters the model. We study this question in a controlled recurrent world model where prior work identified a checkpoint-specific rank-4 interface for one-shot counterfactual velocity interventions. The correction rapidly leaves this fixed entry subspace during autonomous rollout. Nevertheless, a low-rank image obtained by transporting the entry directions through the factual recurrent Jacobian chain continues to capture most of the nonlinear correction. Restarts using the tangent-predicted correction preserve substantial counterfactual future function. This transport/function pattern recurs across independently trained structured-GRU models and a parameter-matched LSTM initialized with a privileged compact correction. We further characterize a finite-horizon future-response operator over the full recurrent carrier. Patching shifts its leading future-sensitive directions toward the matched native-counterfactual organization, and the local operator accurately ranks finite perturbation effects over the registered direction panels at the patched and native-counterfactual basepoints. A separate full-amplitude assay finds substantial factual-endpoint tangent residuals and supports response reconfiguration in two of three checkpoints. Together, these results show that compact intervention structure can persist as a moving, state-dependent local geometry embedded in high-dimensional recurrent dynamics, without implying a fixed or dynamically closed low-dimensional state.

</details>


### [208] [From Pretraining to Proficiency: Real-World Subtask RL for Long-Horizon Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21788)
*Sichang Su,Benjamin Yang,Zhiyun Deng,Boyuan Liang,Yip Fun Yeung,Zelin Wang,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PARTS：一种面向真实世界长时程机器人任务的子任务强化学习微调框架，冻结预训练策略提供标称动作，由智能体生成的选择器与成功验证器激活残差修正并提供局部奖励，使训练集中在少数瓶颈子任务上，从而以少量人类干预和稀疏奖励显著提升全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人基础策略在长时程任务中常能完成大部分步骤，却反复在少数关键子任务上失败。若用监督微调补齐，需要采集完整任务示范，迫使操作者重复策略已掌握的行为，代价高；而现有强化学习微调方法在仅使用稀疏奖励时难以处理长时程任务。

Method: 提出 PARTS（Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks）真实世界子任务 RL 框架：冻结的预训练策略在执行全程提供标称动作；智能体生成的选择器（selectors）与成功验证器（success verifiers）在瓶颈处激活残差修正，并给出局部结果奖励。该局部奖励使算法在完整任务成功稀少时仍能从成功子任务中学习。训练结合在线 RL 与成功重加权再训练（success-reweighted retraining），每次再训练得到的残差策略重新部署以继续收集经验。人类仅在设置阶段识别瓶颈并在需要时进行物理重置。

Result: 在双臂 YAM 与单臂 Franka 任务上，PARTS 将完整任务成功率分别从 32% 提升到 61%、从 50% 提升到 95%，平均每个任务仅需数十分钟真实世界 RL rollout。与现有真实世界 RL 微调方法相比，在相同机器人 rollout 预算下，全任务成功率提升超过 25%，且所需人类参与更少。

Conclusion: PARTS 表明：将真实世界 RL 微调聚焦于预训练策略的瓶颈子任务，并利用标称策略与残差策略分工、局部成功奖励及再训练机制，可在有限机器人交互预算和较少人工干预下有效提升长时程任务的最终成功率。

Abstract: A pretrained robot foundation policy may execute most of a long-horizon task yet repeatedly fail at a few critical subtasks. Collecting additional full-task demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) requires operators to repeat behaviors the policy already performs well. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches struggle to solve long-horizon tasks using only sparse rewards. We present PARTS (Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks), a real-world subtask RL framework that concentrates practice at these bottlenecks while allowing training rollouts to proceed with minimal human intervention. The frozen pretrained policy supplies nominal actions throughout execution, while agent-generated selectors and success verifiers activate residual corrections and provide local outcome rewards. These rewards support learning from successful subtasks even when complete-task successes are scarce. Training combines online RL with success-reweighted retraining, and each retrained residual policy is redeployed to collect further experience. Humans identify bottlenecks during setup and perform physical resets when needed. On bimanual YAM and single-arm Franka tasks, PARTS improves complete-task success from 32% to 61% and from 50% to 95%, respectively, using tens of minutes of real-world RL rollouts per task on average. Compared with existing real-world RL fine-tuning methods, PARTS raises full-task success by more than 25% under the same robot-rollout budget while requiring less human involvement.

</details>


### [209] [AcousticDiffusion: Semantically Conditioned Audio-Guided Diffusion Policy for Search-and-Rescue Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21792)
*Iana Zhura,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Hajira Amjad,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcousticDiffusion：一种语义条件下、声学引导的扩散策略，用于救援机器人朝人类呼救者导航。基于冻结的预训练音频识别器与麦克风阵列方位测量，构建机器人为中心的贝叶斯鸟瞰信念场，融合自我运动补偿，条件化扩散模型生成航点轨迹；仿真验证端点方位误差11.20°、91.78%轨迹在30°内，并在ZSL-1四足机器人上在线部署，平均方位误差64.9°、最终源距离较经典规划器缩短37.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉退化或被遮挡的救援场景中，机器人朝人类呼救者导航是关键能力，但声学定位不精确且存在方位—距离不确定性，需要将不确定的声学观测转化为可靠的接近行为。

Method: 冻结的预训练音频识别器处理10.24s窗口，通过语音门控与困境感知优先化将识别输出转为源级导航角色；麦克风阵列到达方向测量递归整合为机器人为中心的贝叶斯鸟瞰信念场；自我运动补偿对齐连续观测以逐步约束源位置并保留方位引起的距离不确定性；以语义信念、近期声学观测、音频特征及机器人状态条件化扩散模型生成航点轨迹。

Result: 仿真验证集（真实录音）平均端点方位误差11.20°，91.78%轨迹在30°内对齐；干扰源抑制89.20%–98.99%；91.07%窗口优先HELP标记呼救者。ZSL-1四足机器人无额外重训练在线部署，平均方位误差64.9°（A*为98.2°、RRT为90.4°），平均规划耗时6.07ms；最终源距离由ODAS引导的经典规划器3.96m降至2.48m，提升37.4%。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效将不确定的声学观测转化为对呼救者更近距离的接近行为，在声学定位不完备的条件下仍优于经典规划器。

Abstract: Navigating toward human callers is an important capability for rescue robots operating where visual contact is degraded or occluded. We present AcousticDiffusion, a semantically conditioned, audio-guided diffusion policy for human-directed navigation. A frozen pretrained audio recognizer processes 10.24 s windows, with speech gating and distress-aware prioritization converting recognition outputs into source-level navigation roles. Microphone-array direction-of-arrival measurements are recursively integrated into a robot-centric Bayesian bird's-eye-view belief field. Ego-motion compensation aligns successive observations, progressively constraining source position while preserving bearing-induced range uncertainty. The semantic belief, recent acoustic observations, audio features, and robot state condition a diffusion model that generates waypoint trajectories. On a synthetic-navigation validation set using recorded audio, AcousticDiffusion achieves a mean end-point bearing error of 11.20 degrees, with 91.78% of trajectories aligned within 30 degrees of the caller. Distractor rejection ranges from 89.20% to 98.99%, and the policy favors a HELP-designated caller over a competing speaker in 91.07% of windows. Deployed online on a ZSL-1 quadruped without additional retraining, it achieves a mean bearing error of 64.9 degrees, compared with 98.2 degrees for A* and 90.4 degrees for RRT, with a mean planner compute time of 6.07 ms. Despite imperfect acoustic localization, the reported mean final source distance is reduced from 3.96 m for the classical planners using ODAS-derived (Open embedded Audition System) guidance to 2.48 m, a 37.4% improvement. These results demonstrate the framework's ability to translate uncertain acoustic observations into closer approaches to human callers.

</details>


### [210] [Contact-Rich Motion Planning via GPU-Parallel Mode Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21803)
*Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoMET（Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization），一种面向接触丰富运动规划（CRMP）的“显式接触模式广域评估 + GPU 并行轨迹优化”方法。其核心是用 GPU 高吞吐轨迹评估器配合贪心式接触模式扩展，替代或补充搜索启发式与优化重构。平面推动实验中，CoMET 在解质量与规划时间上可比甚至优于优化、采样、树搜索基线，并在几乎所有算例上匹配完全枚举参考；双臂非抓取操作中，GPU 友好的局部模式扩展随模式空间增大取得更高规划成功率。结论是广域显式模式评估是 CRMP 的简单有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富运动规划对机器人操作与运动至关重要，但因接触决策具有组合性而计算困难。现有方法通常通过搜索启发式或优化重构来避免对接触模式序列进行广域评估。作者重新审视广域评估，借现代 GPU 硬件的高并行吞吐，探索显式评估大量接触模式是否可行且有效。

Method: 提出 CoMET：将 GPU 并行轨迹评估与贪心接触模式扩展结合。方法不依赖复杂搜索启发式或优化重构，而是显式枚举/扩展接触模式序列，并对候选轨迹进行高吞吐并行评价，从而在模式空间中逐步贪心扩展。

Result: 在平面推动基准上，CoMET 在解质量与规划时间上与基于优化、采样、树搜索的基线具有竞争力；在几乎所有实例上匹配完全枚举参考，且评估次数更少、规划时间更短。消融实验表明性能提升很大程度来自高吞吐轨迹评估器。在双臂非抓取操作中，随着模式空间增大，GPU 友好的局部模式扩展比所测试的自适应树搜索获得更高规划成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，广域显式接触模式评估为接触丰富运动规划提供了一种简单而有效的替代路径；现代 GPU 并行计算使“广泛评估”重新具备实用价值，尤其适合组合接触决策问题。

Abstract: Contact-rich motion planning (CRMP) is essential for robotic manipulation and locomotion, yet remains computationally challenging due to combinatorial contact decisions. Existing methods typically avoid broad evaluation of contact-mode sequences through search heuristics or optimization reformulations. We revisit broad evaluation in light of modern GPU hardware and introduce Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization (CoMET), which combines GPU-parallel trajectory evaluation with greedy contact-mode expansion. On planar pushing benchmarks, CoMET is competitive with optimization-based, sampling, and tree-search baselines in solution quality and planning time, matching the full-enumeration reference on nearly all instances with fewer evaluations and shorter planning times. Ablations suggest that much of the performance gain comes from the high-throughput trajectory evaluator. In bimanual nonprehensile manipulation, GPU-friendly local mode expansion achieves higher planning success than the tested adaptive tree search as the mode space grows. These results demonstrate that broad explicit mode evaluation provides a simple yet effective alternative for CRMP.

</details>


### [211] [A Sim-to-Real Integration Pipeline for Training and Deployment of Chunk-Based VLA Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21817)
*Mathilde Kappel,Clémence Grislain,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Louis Annabi,Faïz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux,Mahdi Khoramshahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套开源 sim-to-real 实验协议，用仿真专家轨迹在真实 Franka FR3 上开环回放采集视觉/本体感知数据，并复用同一部署栈进行闭环策略评测，从而低成本、可扩展地构建 VLA 训练与评估数据，同时量化 sim-to-real 差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通常依赖大规模真实机器人演示数据进行训练与评测，但真实数据采集多依赖人工遥操作，成本高、耗时长、难以规模化，成为该范式发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 在仿真中生成专家轨迹，将其在真实 Franka FR3 平台上开环回放，记录对应的真实视觉与本体感知观测，并转换为 VLA 训练兼容格式；随后复用同一部署栈，以闭环方式在相同硬件配置上评测训练后的策略。

Result: 协议将数据采集与策略评测统一在同一硬件配置下，且每条真实记录都与产生它的仿真轨迹配对，可直接测量 sim-to-real 差距；同时开源发布所采集数据集（Hugging Face）与流水线源码。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可复现、开源的 sim-to-real 数据采集与评估方案，缓解真实演示数据采集瓶颈，并为 VLA 模型训练、闭环评测及 sim-to-real 差距分析提供基础设施。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a prominent paradigm for mapping multimodal inputs, including semantic instructions, visual observations of the scene, and proprioceptive observations, to robot actions. Most state-of-the-art models predict actions in the end-effector pose space as sequences of action chunks. Training and evaluating these models requires large-scale collections of real-world demonstrations, pairing robot actions with the corresponding visual and proprioceptive observations. Collecting such data on real hardware typically relies on human teleoperation, making the process costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. We present an open-source sim-to-real experimental protocol that addresses this bottleneck: expert trajectories generated in simulation are replayed open-loop on a real Franka FR3 setup, where the corresponding real visual and proprioceptive observations are recorded and converted into a format compatible with VLA training. The same deployment stack is then reused, in closed-loop, to evaluate a trained policy on that setup, so that data collection and evaluation share an identical hardware configuration. Because each real recording is paired with the simulated trajectory that produced it, the protocol also yields a direct measurement of the sim-to-real gap. We release the collected datasets on Hugging Face together with the pipeline source code https://gitlab.isir.upmc.fr/kappel/sim2real_public_chunk_control.

</details>


### [212] [LunaDrive: A Delay-Compensated High-Voltage GaN FET-Based Motor Driver for Dynamic Robots with Flat BLDC Motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21818)
*Sota Yuzaki,Temma Suzuki,Hiromi Tada,Masanori Konishi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究基于GaN FET开发了面向高功率扁平BLDC电机的紧凑型驱动器，实现超额定电压运行：96 V下加装散热片可连续输出30 A，峰值电流80 A、最高电频率3110 Hz，并用24S（100 V）LiPo电池完成高速负载起升实验，验证了其在动态机器人中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态机器人的发展依赖高功率扁平BLDC电机的性能提升，但商用伺服电机额定电压多为48 V及以下，限制了最高转速，而动态机器人需要快速能量输出，故希望通过超额定电压驱动提升瞬时最大转速。同时，电机驱动器用半导体器件存在耐压与电流容量的权衡，传统Si MOSFET驱动器难以兼顾高压与大电流；GaN FET虽具潜力，但可安装于扁平BLDC电机背面的紧凑型驱动器仍很有限。

Method: 采用GaN FET开发适用于高功率扁平BLDC电机的电机驱动器，并研究在超出额定工作范围的高速区内延迟补偿的效果；通过实验测试其电压、电流、电频率性能，并进行由24S（100 V）LiPo电池驱动的高速负载起升实验。

Result: 在96 V运行条件下，加装散热片时实现了30 A连续电流；确认峰值电流达80 A、最大电频率为3110 Hz；并以24S（100 V）LiPo电池完成高速负载起升实验，展示了其对动态机器人运行的适用性。

Conclusion: 基于GaN FET的驱动器可在紧凑结构下实现高压、大电流运行，突破48 V额定电压限制，提升扁平BLDC电机的瞬时最高转速，并具备应用于动态机器人高动态运动的可行性。

Abstract: The performance improvement of high-power flat BLDC motors has accelerated the development of dynamic robots. However, many commercially available servo motors assume operating voltages of 48 V or lower, which limits the maximum rotational speed. Dynamic robots require rapid energy generation, so this voltage constraint restricts motion performance. Therefore, driving motors beyond the rated voltage is desirable to increase the instantaneous maximum speed. On the other hand, semiconductor devices used in motor drivers have a trade-off between voltage rating and current capacity. Conventional drivers using Si MOSFETs have difficulty achieving both high-voltage and high-current operation. Although GaN FETs are promising, compact drivers that can be mounted on the rear side of flat BLDC motors remain limited. In this study, a motor driver for high-power flat BLDC motors using GaN FETs is developed. The effect of delay compensation in the high-speed region beyond the rated operating range is also investigated. In the experiments, under 96 V operation, a continuous current of 30 A was achieved with a heat sink attached. A peak current of 80 A and a maximum electrical frequency of 3110 Hz were confirmed. A high-speed load lifting experiment driven by a LiPo battery 24S (100 V) was also conducted, demonstrating applicability to dynamic robot operation.

</details>


### [213] [PopNavShift: Stress-Testing Social Navigation under Behavioral Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21838)
*Kaizhen Tan,Diyu Zheng,Tim Guangyu Wu,ChengHe Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PopNavShift 匹配仿真框架，用于在行人群体分布偏移下压力测试社会导航策略；结果显示控制器排名对行人负担指标远比机器人通行时间敏感，且该敏感性在空间受限场景中更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会导航算法通常在固定行人行为分布下评估，但行人对机器人的反应在个体与社会情境间差异显著，固定分布可能掩盖策略在真实群体变化下的脆弱性。

Method: PopNavShift 通过提示 Gemini 3.7 Flash 处理来自 MatrAIx Persona 1M 的 600 条合成 persona 记录，构建群体条件化的行人运动画像，并将响应确定性映射为有界运动参数；随后在 8 种群体条件下，对反应式避让、提前让行与互惠避碰三类导航策略进行 7,488 次匹配机器人运行对比；另对同一 202 个 persona 做仅改变时间压力的匹配干预，并在第二种行人动力学模型下复验。

Result: 仅改变时间压力的匹配干预中，基于机器人通行时间的控制器排名反转 8.6%，而基于平均行人延误的反转达 22.4%，基于最差十分位延误的反转达 23.9%。跨群体条件看，策略排名对行人负担的敏感性高于机器人通行时间，并在空间受限环境中增强；该定性模式在第二种行人动力学模型下保持一致。

Conclusion: 社会导航策略应在多样化行为群体中评估，并同时报告机器人性能与行人负担两类指标，以避免固定分布评估带来的误导性结论。

Abstract: Social-navigation algorithms are often evaluated under a fixed pedestrian-behavior distribution, despite substantial variation in pedestrian responses to robots across individuals and social contexts. We introduce PopNavShift, a matched simulation framework for stress-testing social-navigation strategies under pedestrian population shifts. PopNavShift constructs population-conditioned pedestrian motion profiles by prompting Gemini 3.7 Flash with 600 synthetic persona records from MatrAIx Persona 1M and deterministically mapping the responses into bounded motion parameters. It then compares three representative navigation strategies, reactive avoidance, early yielding, and reciprocal collision avoidance, across eight population conditions and 7,488 matched robot runs. In a matched intervention on the same 202 personas, changing only time pressure reverses 8.6% of controller rankings based on robot travel time, but 22.4% based on mean pedestrian delay and 23.9% based on worst-decile delay. Across population conditions, this sensitivity is greater for pedestrian burden than for robot travel time and increases in spatially constrained settings; the same qualitative pattern persists under a second pedestrian dynamics model. These findings support evaluating navigation strategies across behavioral populations using both robot performance and pedestrian burden.

</details>


### [214] [GALA: Geometry-Aware Latent Action Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Model Pretraining across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21948)
*Yichen Liu,Puzhen Yuan,Xiang Zhu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GALA 框架：以 3D 末端执行器几何运动增强图像潜动作建模，并引入统一末端执行器运动表示 UEMR，实现跨具身细粒度动作的 VLA 预训练，在 RoboCasa-GR1 上达 68.3% 成功率、真机达 75.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多具身数据集学习大规模 VLA 模型的核心难点在于不同末端执行器的动作空间异构。潜动作模型（LAM）虽可从多样视频中学习具身无关的动作表示，但现有基于图像的 LAM 难以刻画细粒度末端执行器关节运动，尤其是人手与灵巧手机器人的手指级几何变化。

Method: 提出 GALA（Geometry-Aware Latent-Action）框架，在图像潜动作基础上引入 3D 末端执行器几何运动。针对直接引入点云会导致细粒度表示共享语义不足、阻碍跨具身预训练的问题，设计统一末端执行器运动表示（UEMR），在保留细粒度运动信息的同时提升潜动作的跨具身泛化性。GALA 由此融合两类监督信号：捕捉场景级动态的视觉潜动作，与捕捉共享细粒度末端执行器关节运动的几何潜动作，用于多具身数据（含无动作的自我中心人类视频）的 VLA 预训练。

Result: 在细粒度运动探测、跨具身检索与下游 VLA 评测中验证有效性，可跨具身建模可泛化的细粒度运动：RoboCasa-GR1 成功率 68.3%，真实世界成功率 75.5%。

Conclusion: 以 UEMR 为桥梁将几何信息注入潜动作建模，可有效弥补图像式 LAM 在末端执行器细粒度articulation上的不足，为多具身数据（含无动作人类视频）的 VLA 预训练提供更有效的监督，代码、附录与演示已开源。

Abstract: Learning large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models from multi-embodiment datasets remains challenging due to heterogeneous action spaces across end effectors. Although latent action models (LAMs) can learn embodiment-agnostic action representations from diverse video data, existing image-based LAMs often fail to capture fine-grained end-effector articulation, particularly finger-level geometric changes in human and dexterous robot hands. To address this limitation, we propose GALA, a Geometry-Aware Latent-Action modeling framework that augments image-based latent actions with 3D end-effector geometric motion. However, naively incorporating point clouds yields fine-grained action representations with limited shared semantics, hindering cross-embodiment pretraining. To address this issue, we introduce the Unified End-effector Motion Representation (UEMR), which preserves fine-grained motion information while improving the cross-embodiment generalizability of latent actions. Building upon UEMR, GALA combines visual latent actions that capture scene-level dynamics with geometric latent actions that capture shared fine-grained end-effector articulation, providing effective supervision for VLA pretraining from multi-embodiment data, including action-free ego-centric human videos. Experiments on fine-grained motion probing, cross-embodiment retrieval, and downstream VLA evaluation demonstrate GALA's effectiveness in modeling generalizable fine-grained motions across embodiments, achieving 68.3% RoboCasa-GR1 success rate and 75.5% real-world success rate. Code, appendix, and demos are available at https://puzhenyuan.github.io/GALA-website/.

</details>


### [215] [When Should a Failing Robot Ask? Initiating Corrective Human-Robot Dialogue from Audited Sensor Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21942)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建可控故障基准，测量机器人各传感器对故障原因的诊断能力，并评估六个开源视觉语言模型在“自行行动/查询自身传感器/询问人类”三动作纠正对话中的决策行为。结果显示模型诊断常受提示表面形式支配、置信度无信息量；提供力数据文本后部分模型才超过基线，而是否提问应与实测准确率和成本挂钩，而非模型自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务失败后需决定：依据自身诊断行动、查询其他机载传感器，还是打断人类求助。做出好决策需要知道传感器能揭示多少故障原因，以及机器人自身诊断的可靠性。现有模型是否具备这种基于证据与成本的决策能力尚不清楚。

Method: 作者构建模拟基准，故障真实原因由注入已知，并显式检查数据泄漏，从而测量不同传感器对故障原因的可诊断性。随后测试六个开源视觉语言模型，在多种提示变体下评估其诊断与求助行为，包括改变拒绝选项位置、有无示例、是否将力数据以十行文本提供。最后将选择形式化为三动作决策问题：自行行动、查询自身传感器、询问人类，并以实测准确率推导最优策略。

Result: 部分故障可从相机图像诊断，另一些只能从力数据诊断：力数据达0.99，而图像方法无一超过0.55。六个视觉语言模型的行为随提示表面变化而非证据变化：将拒绝选项从末位移到首位，使六个模型-家族对中的三个拒绝率从78–100%骤降至0–6%。基于帧的准确率在所有提示变体下均不高于多数类基线，且陈述置信度与正确性无关。将力数据以十行文本提供给模型后，六个模型中有四个首次出现高于基线的诊断，表明失败很大程度源于传感器数据缺失而非能力缺失。模型未遵循由实测准确率推导出的最优策略，其提问率对四倍提问成本变化不敏感。一次向人类提问可将模型从基线提升至约答案者自身可靠性（提问时0.70–0.81）。

Conclusion: 是否提问应依据实测准确率与明示成本来决定，而不应依赖模型自身置信度。纠正对话中的求助决策需要证据驱动的诊断能力、成本敏感的策略以及对传感器信息价值的显式建模。

Abstract: A robot that fails at a task faces the first decision in corrective dialogue: act on its own diagnosis, consult another onboard sensor, or interrupt a person. Choosing well requires knowing how much the robot's sensors reveal about the cause and how reliable the robot's own diagnosis is. We build a simulated benchmark in which every failure's true cause is known, because we injected it, and measure what each sensor reveals, with explicit checks against data leakage. Some failures are diagnosable from camera images; others only from the robot's force data (0.99 from force data, no image method above 0.55). We then test six open vision-language models. Their behavior tracks the surface of the prompt, not the evidence: moving the refusal option from last to first in the answer list collapses refusal rates from 78-100% to 0-6% in three of the six swept model-and-family pairs. Accuracy from frames stays at or below a majority-class baseline under every prompt variant, with or without worked examples, and stated confidence carries no information about correctness. Handing the same models the force data as ten lines of text produces the first above-baseline diagnoses, in four of the six models: much of the failure reflects missing sensor data, not missing ability. We pose the choice as a three-action decision problem, act, consult your own sensors, or ask a human, whose optimal policy follows from measured accuracy. The models do not follow it, and their ask rates ignore a fourfold change in question cost. One question to a human still lifts them from that baseline to roughly the answerer's own reliability (0.70-0.81 when they ask). The decision to ask should be tied to measured accuracy and stated costs, not to the model's confidence.

</details>


### [216] [VIRGA: Virtual-Agent-Intermediated Riemannian Geometry for Active-Sensing Air-Ground Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21883)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VIRGA：一种神经几何协调框架，用于 UGV 云台 LiDAR 持续观测 UAV 的空地协同任务，通过双 LiDAR 观测构建有界、源特定的黎曼场，并借助虚拟智能体与互惠弹性反馈耦合，实现动态避障、异构运动协调与观测保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地自主系统在 UAV 必须始终处于 UGV 云台 LiDAR 可观测范围内时难度显著增加，需在同一闭环中同时处理动态障碍物规避、异构运动协调、有限感知和任务主导权变化。

Method: VIRGA 将双 LiDAR 观测转化为有界、源特定的黎曼场，并通过虚拟智能体以互惠弹性反馈进行耦合；平台感知执行映射将共享协调参考转换为可行的 UAV、UGV 和云台指令，同时施加主动观测安全约束。消融实验验证了在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介和互惠反馈的贡献。

Result: 与三类基线对比：适配 Ray-RMP 响应最快但耦合空地任务中安全间隙不足；密集解析黎曼场改善几何避障，但评估成本高，难以稳定维持视场；适配 ColAG 延迟最低，但仍存在安全与可观测性违规。VIRGA 安全完成所有成对仓库条件，并在无需重训练的长距离洞穴压力测试中于不规则、受限几何环境中保持协调。

Conclusion: VIRGA 能在观测约束下有效完成空地协同，兼具安全性、低延迟与视场维持能力，并展现出对不规则受限环境的泛化鲁棒性；其性能来源于在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介与互惠反馈的协同作用。

Abstract: Air-ground autonomy becomes harder when the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) must remain observable by a gimbal light detection and ranging (LiDAR) mounted on the unmanned ground vehicle (UGV). The platforms must avoid dynamic obstacles while coordinating heterogeneous motion, limited sensing, and changing task initiative within one closed loop. This paper presents VIRGA, a neural geometric coordination framework that turns dual-LiDAR observations into bounded source-specific Riemannian fields and couples them through a virtual agent with reciprocal elastic feedback. Platform-aware execution maps convert the shared coordination reference into feasible UAV, UGV, and gimbal commands while enforcing active-observation safeguards. Evaluation against three complementary baselines reveals distinct limitations. An adapted Ray-RMP controller provides the fastest Riemannian response but produces insufficient clearance in the coupled air-ground task. A dense analytical Riemannian field improves geometric avoidance, yet its high evaluation cost prevents stable field-of-view maintenance. An adapted ColAG controller achieves the lowest latency but still incurs safety and observability violations. VIRGA completes all paired warehouse conditions safely, while a long-range cave stress test without retraining demonstrates sustained coordination in irregular and confined geometry. Ablations confirm contributions from online geometric evaluation, virtual-agent mediation, and reciprocal feedback.

</details>


### [217] [CommitFlow: Semantic Commitment Verification and Local Correction for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21908)
*Zixiang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Yang Yang,Chaoyu Wang,Haoran Xiao,Hui Zhang,Chuang Cheng,Jianjun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CommitFlow，一种在冻结基座策略的前提下，将"承诺监控"与"局部纠偏"相结合的闭环执行框架，用于缓解长时程视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中的语义承诺与物理状态不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 VLA 策略发展迅速，长时程执行中仍可能在前一阶段所需的物理效果尚未真正建立时，就推进到下一任务阶段，形成"语义承诺（阶段必须建立或维持的物理条件）"与"实际物理状态"之间的不匹配。由于仅凭动作指令无法确认该条件是否满足，局部偏差会逐步传播并最终导致任务失败。

Method: 提出 CommitFlow 闭环执行框架，保持基座策略冻结，融合三个组件：(1) 语义承诺监控器（SCM）：将阶段需求与当前状态证据进行比对，当所需条件未满足或被破坏时，抑制依赖于该条件的后续动作；(2) BoundaryFlow：以当前状态与基座动作为条件，生成局部纠偏动作；(3) 关系与增益校准（RGC）：在满足相关约束的前提下，选取最小的纠偏强度。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的十个常见基准任务上，CommitFlow 取得 75.9% 的平均成功率，相较基座策略 pi0.5 提升 22.7%。跨策略实验亦显示出一致的性能增益。

Conclusion: CommitFlow 通过在执行过程中引入承诺监控与最小强度局部纠偏，有效提升了长时程机器人执行的成功率与可靠性，为实现可靠的长时程机器人执行提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced rapidly, long-horizon execution may still progress to the next task stage before the required physical effect has been established. We call this a mismatch between semantic commitments, physical conditions that a stage must establish or maintain, and the actual physical state. Because an action command alone cannot confirm such a condition, local deviations can propagate and cause task failure. To address this problem, we present CommitFlow, a closed-loop execution framework that combines commitment monitoring with local correction while keeping the base policy frozen. CommitFlow integrates three components. A Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM) compares stage requirements against current state evidence and holds back dependent actions when a required condition is unmet or violated. BoundaryFlow then generates a local correction conditioned on the current state and base action, and Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC) selects the smallest correction strength that satisfies the relevant constraints. Across the ten common RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark tasks, CommitFlow achieves a mean success rate of 75.9 percent, improving on the base policy pi0.5 by 22.7 percent. Cross-policy experiments show consistent gains, pointing toward reliable long-horizon robot execution.

</details>


### [218] [MAAP: Multi-Agent Active Perception for Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21929)
*Bruno N. Y. Chen,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Xiufeng Song,Zhemeng Zhang,Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MAAP与RAIL：将多机械臂本身（腕部相机）作为主动感知的移动视角，并用角色感知模仿学习统一建模不同角色的动作；在多任务仿真与双臂平台上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体/多机械臂操作中，每个机械臂都带腕部相机并在执行动作时移动，天然产生多个任务驱动视角；但这些观测通常未被充分利用，主动感知在操作中仍常被当作需要额外专用感知智能体的问题。

Method: MAAP（Multi-Agent Active Perception）：让每个机械臂“双用途”，既执行操作动作，又通过其腕部相机为团队提供移动视角。RAIL（Role-Aware Imitation Learning）：控制器在预测每个机械臂动作块（action chunk）的同时预测其当前角色，并以角色为条件生成动作，从而在单一网络中表示角色相关动作。

Result: 四个仿真任务中，扩大感知范围将平均成功率从固定相机的56.5%提升到单个主动腕部视角的62.5%，再到全部腕部视角的70.0%；MAAP+RAIL达到79.2%。RAIL的额外增益集中在三臂Microwave任务，在相同多腕部视角输入下成功率从47%升至82%。在双臂平台上，MAAP+RAIL在20次放置试验中成功14次，而固定视角ACT为0/20。

Conclusion: 协作式操作本身即可作为一种主动感知机制；多机械臂的腕部视角不应被忽视，显式角色建模能进一步释放其收益。

Abstract: Multi-agent manipulation naturally produces multiple task-driven viewpoints: every arm carries a wrist camera and moves through the scene while acting. Yet these observations are typically underutilized, and active perception in manipulation is still often treated as requiring a dedicated sensing agent. We introduce MAAP (Multi-Agent Active Perception), in which every arm is dual-purpose: it executes manipulation actions and, through the wrist camera it carries, simultaneously serves as a moving viewpoint for the team. We pair this with RAIL (Role-Aware Imitation Learning), a controller that predicts each arm's current role alongside its action chunk and conditions action generation on it, representing role-dependent actions within one network. Across four simulated tasks, widening the perception regime lifts average success from 56.5% with a fixed camera to 62.5% with one active wrist view and 70.0% with all of them, while MAAP+RAIL reaches 79.2%. RAIL's additional gain is concentrated on the three-arm Microwave task, where success rises from 47% to 82% on identical multi-wrist inputs. On a dual-arm platform, MAAP+RAIL succeeds in 14 of 20 placement trials compared with 0 of 20 for fixed-view ACT. Collaborative manipulation can thus serve as an active perception mechanism in its own right.

</details>


### [219] [CARF: Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21982)
*Shuqi Zhao,Bang Du,Cheng-En Wu,Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CARF 框架，利用失败轨迹中的“渐进片段”与“关键失败片段”之间的非对称监督关系，通过对比式吸引-排斥流匹配目标，从含失败的不完美机器人演示数据中更全面地学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示采集常包含失败或不完美轨迹。现有方法多仅挖掘失败轨迹中仍朝任务完成推进的片段，而忽视直接导致任务失败的关键失败行为，导致对不完美数据的利用不充分。

Method: CARF 的核心是：1）设计基于进度的的重要性评分器，仅使用成功专家演示及其扰动结果训练，用于估计每步对任务完成的贡献并识别失败轨迹中的信息区域；2）利用这些评分构建统一的流匹配目标，对渐进行为施加吸引、对关键失败行为施加排斥，同时排除语义模糊片段，以避免不可靠监督。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界中进行了大量实验，在多种失败场景下相较竞争基线取得一致提升；消融实验进一步验证了所提出的评分机制与吸引-排斥机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹中的渐进片段与关键失败片段应被非对称处理：前者应被模仿，后者应被显式回避。CARF 通过进度评分与吸引-排斥流匹配统一目标，实现了对不完美机器人数据的更全面、可靠利用。

Abstract: Robot demonstration collection often produces imperfect or failed trajectories in addition to successful demonstrations. Existing methods typically exploit failed trajectories by identifying segments that still make progress toward task completion, but largely overlook \textit{failure-critical behaviors} that directly lead to task failure. Here we argue that these two types of segments provide fundamentally asymmetric supervision: progressive segments should be imitated, whereas failure-critical segments should be explicitly avoided. Based on this observation, we propose CARF, a Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework for learning from imperfect robot data. CARF introduces a progress-based importance scorer, trained solely on successful expert demonstrations and its perturbation results, to estimate step-wise contributions toward task completion and identify informative regions in failed trajectories. These scores guide a unified flow-matching objective that attracts the policy toward progressive behaviors and repels it from failure-critical ones, while excluding ambiguous segments. This enables more comprehensive utilization of imperfect data and avoids unreliable supervision from ambiguous failure segments. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines across diverse failure scenarios, with ablations further validating the effectiveness of the proposed scoring and attraction-repulsion mechanisms. Our website is https://zhao-sq.github.io/carf/#.

</details>


### [220] [SkelWAM: A Skeleton-Guided World-Action Model for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21983)
*Pengjun Niu,Yujia Xie,Rui Peng,Hang Zhao,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkelWAM 提出以"骨架"几何表示（机械臂中线几何、TCP 位姿、夹爪指令）构成共享 25 维状态，通过视频-动作混合 Transformer 预测规范骨架动作块，再由各本体专属的约束解码器转为关节或连续体控制，实现无需关节一一对应、无需目标任务示范的跨本体操作迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器人本体的操作经验复用对规模化机器人学习、减少重复的任务专用数据采集至关重要。但本体变化会同时改变视觉外观、动作维度与语义，以及能实现同一工具位姿的全身构型，因此难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出 SkelWAM（骨架引导的世界-动作模型），用单一显式几何表示耦合感知与控制：以机械臂中线几何、工具中心点（TCP）位姿、平行夹爪指令组成共享 25 维状态，统一规范第三人称与腕部观测以及未来全身动作目标。模型为视频-动作混合 Transformer，配合预测性视觉监督训练，输出规范骨架动作块；再由本体专属的约束解码器映射为关节控制或连续体机器人控制。该方法不要求本体间关节一一对应，也不使用目标任务的示范或目标策略更新。

Result: 构建 LIBERO-Cross10 源域专用跨本体迁移基准，涵盖 10 个任务、10 个目标本体、4 类形态分组。在该基准 1000 个回合中，Franka 训练的 SkelWAM 成功率达 43.3%，超出最佳评估基线 36.2 个百分点。此外，将 JAKA mini2 训练的策略部署到 Feagine A03 连续体机器人上完成三项桌面操作任务，验证了真实世界跨本体操作的潜力。

Conclusion: 以显式几何骨架状态作为共享中间表示，可在无目标示范、无关节对应、无目标策略更新的条件下实现单源跨本体操作迁移，并在仿真基准与真实连续体机器人上均展现出有效性，为跨本体机器人学习提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Reusing manipulation experience across robot embodiments is important for scaling robot learning and reducing repeated task-specific data collection. However, changes in embodiment alter visual appearance, action dimensionality and semantics, and the whole-body configurations that can realize the same tool pose. We present SkelWAM, a skeleton-guided world-action model that couples perception and control through one explicit geometric representation for single-source cross-embodiment manipulation. Arm centerline geometry, tool-center-point (TCP) pose, and parallel-jaw commands form a shared 25-D state. The same definition underlies canonical third-person and wrist observations and future whole-body action targets. Trained with predictive visual supervision, a video-action mixture of transformers predicts canonical skeleton action chunks, which embodiment-specific constrained decoders convert into joint or continuum-robot controls. This formulation requires no one-to-one joint correspondence and uses no target-task demonstrations or target policy updates. We introduce LIBERO-Cross10, a source-only cross-embodiment transfer benchmark covering ten tasks and ten target embodiments across four morphological groups. On this benchmark, Franka-trained SkelWAM achieves 43.3% success over 1,000 episodes, exceeding the best-performing evaluated baseline by 36.2 percentage points. We further deploy a JAKA mini2-trained policy on the Feagine A03 continuum robot for three tabletop manipulation tasks, illustrating the approach's potential for real-world cross-embodiment manipulation. Project page: http://www.liukepku.com/skelwam/index.html

</details>


### [221] [Gripper-Aware Automatic Dense Packing of Irregular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22062)
*Tianhao Qin,Connor McCann,Berk Calli,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向真实机械臂的闭环不规则物体密集装箱流水线，将感知、考虑夹爪的放置优化与力引导执行集成，并在 Franka Panda 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库作业需要自动密集装箱，但机器人操作中仍是基础难题。已有不规则物体装箱工作多局限于理想接触的仿真，把物体视为孤立刚体；夹爪通常只在事后作为离散可行性检查，甚至被忽略，执行中的感知与接触漂移也未处理。

Method: 构建闭环流水线：1）优化器将物体与夹爪表示为分层球树组成的单一复合体；2）在 GPU 上以 CMA-ES 搜索五自由度放置，竖直坐标依据当前高度图解析确定；3）执行阶段采用力监控垂直下降，首次接触即停止；4）释放后通过整合推压消除夹爪感知规划遗留的侧向间隙；5）每次放置后重新感知容器以避免漂移累积。

Result: 在 Franka Emika Panda 上验证，装箱对象包括 3D 打印的平坦、弯曲与凹形物体及 YCB 物体子集。消融实验分离了夹爪感知优化、整合推压与网格几何对端到端成功率、达到密度和计算成本的贡献，并与高度图最小化基线方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将夹爪纳入放置优化、结合力引导执行与释放后整合，并在每次放置后重感知，可提升真实机器人不规则物体密集装箱的可行性与密度；消融与基线对比为各模块贡献提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Automatic dense packing is widely desired in warehouse operations but remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Existing work on irregular-object packing largely targets simulation with idealized contact, treating the object as an isolated rigid body. The gripper often enters as a discrete, post-hoc feasibility check, if considered at all, and the perception and contact drift accumulated during execution are not addressed. We present a closed-loop pipeline that integrates perception, gripper-aware placement optimization, and force-guided execution on a real manipulator. The optimizer represents the object together with the gripper as a single composite body of hierarchical sphere trees. It searches over five degrees of freedom on a GPU within a CMA-ES framework, with the vertical coordinate grounded analytically against the current heightmap. During execution, a force-monitored vertical descent stops on first contact. A post-release consolidation push then closes the residual lateral clearance that gripper-aware planning leaves behind. The container is re-perceived between placements so that drift does not accumulate. We validate the system on a Franka Emika Panda robot packing a 3D-printed set of flat, curved, and concave objects, and a YCB object subset. An ablation study isolates the contribution of gripper-aware optimization, the consolidation push, and mesh-derived geometry to end-to-end success, achieved density, and computational cost. We further benchmark against the heightmap-minimization method as a baseline representative of prior irregular-object packing work.

</details>


### [222] [Duty Factor Predicts Robust Constrained Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Gait Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22073)
*James Zhu,David Ologan,George Ortiz,Thomas Chun Fai Lee,Selvin Garcia Gonzalez,Ardalan Tajbakhsh,Pinhas Ben-Tzvi,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究指出步态类型（如行走、小跑）不足以唯一刻画四足运动，提出并验证“占空比（duty factor）”是预测局部误差收敛和运动鲁棒性的更强、更简单指标，可跨步态类型与控制架构使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人越来越多地部署在需要抵抗扰动、适应受限地形的环境中。传统上常用步态类型来描述四足运动，但同一步态类型内占空比、速度、站姿宽度等参数仍可变化，因此步态类型并不能唯一决定运动特性，需要寻找更有效的低维描述与选择依据。

Method: 论文采用三种不同的四足运动控制方法研究步态参数关系：第一，使用全身轨迹优化结合LQR反馈；第二，使用学习型运动控制器研究占空比选择；第三，使用质心模型预测控制（centroidal MPC）框架验证趋势，并在物理四足机器人上进行窄地形实验。

Result: 结果表明：占空比比名义步态类型更能预测局部误差收敛；在学习型控制器中，占空比可作为低维参数用于调节窄地形环境下的运动鲁棒性；相关趋势在质心模型预测控制框架下仍然存在，并通过物理四足机器人的窄地形实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 占空比为理解和选择鲁棒四足运动提供了一个简单而有效的基础，能够跨不同步态类型和控制架构统一解释运动鲁棒性，并为受限地形下的控制器设计提供参数化依据。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots are increasingly deployed in environments where locomotion must remain robust to disturbances and constrained terrain. Gait type, such as walking or trotting, is commonly used to characterize quadrupedal locomotion. However, gait type does not uniquely define locomotion, as parameters such as duty factor, speed, and stance width can vary within a single gait type. In this work, we investigate the relationship between these gait parameters using three distinct quadrupedal locomotion control approaches. First, using whole body trajectory optimization with LQR feedback, we show that duty factor is a stronger predictor of local error convergence than nominal gait type. Second, we investigate duty factor selection with a learned locomotion controller, suggesting how duty factor may serve as a low-dimensional parameter for adapting locomotion robustness in narrow-terrain environments. Finally, we show that these trends persist under a centroidal model predictive control framework and validate them through narrow-terrain experiments on a physical quadruped. These results show that duty factor provides a simple and effective basis for understanding and selecting robust quadrupedal locomotion across gait types and control architectures.

</details>


### [223] [LIMBO: Learning and Internalizing Model-Free Barrier Objectives for Agile and Safe Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22075)
*Jake Gonzales,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Aaron D. Ames,Lillian J. Ratliff,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMBO 提出一种状态-动作控制屏障函数（Q-CBF）合成与安全结构蒸馏框架，用于高维全身控制。它围绕冻结的基础控制器在残差动作空间学习安全证书，并以风险引导采样探索可恢复性边界，再将其作为教师信号训练任务策略，从而在部署时无需在线安全滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身控制中的碰撞避免与平衡协调面临高维、非线性动力学，安全证书难以设计且难以跨行为复用；同时，部署阶段通常依赖在线安全滤波器，增加复杂性与保守性。作者希望把学习到的安全结构内化到任务策略中，实现可扩展、可迁移的全身安全控制。

Method: LIMBO 从黑盒转移动态与基于状态的失败规范中学习状态-动作控制屏障函数（Q-CBF），学习对象是围绕冻结基础控制器的残差动作，从而使 Q-CBF 合成在完整控制维度上可行，并将证书置于任务策略的控制空间中。合成阶段，学习到的安全值驱动风险引导采样，聚焦于估计的可恢复性边界附近；任务学习阶段，该安全值作为教师提供动作级安全反馈，训练鲁棒任务策略。

Result: 在 29 自由度人形机器人上验证了躲避球规避与低障碍物下运动两项任务。风险引导边界采样被证明能以有理论依据的方式探索可恢复性边缘；在相同安全规范与其他条件不变时，改变采样集中度可产生从下蹲到新颖的后仰 limbo 动作等不同策略。学习到的策略无需在线安全滤波即可迁移到硬件。

Conclusion: LIMBO 将学习型 Q-CBF 扩展到全身控制，并表明风险引导边界采样可系统探索可恢复性边界；通过学习安全合成并将安全结构蒸馏进任务策略，可实现敏捷全身控制，并在真实硬件上无需在线安全滤波器部署。

Abstract: Safe whole-body control requires coordinating collision avoidance and balance under high-dimensional, nonlinear dynamics--making safety certificates difficult to design and reuse across behaviors. We present LIMBO, a framework for synthesizing a state-action control barrier function and distilling its safety structure into a task policy. LIMBO learns the safety certificate from black-box transitions and a state-based failure specification over residual actions around a frozen base controller, making Q-CBF synthesis tractable in the full control dimension while placing the certificate in the task policy's control space. During synthesis, the learned safety value drives risk-guided sampling near the estimated boundary of recoverability; during task learning, it serves as a teacher that provides action-level safety feedback, yielding a robust task policy and alleviating the need for an online safety filter at deployment. We demonstrate LIMBO on a 29-degree-of-freedom humanoid performing dodgeball avoidance and locomotion beneath low obstacles. Beyond scaling learned Q-CBFs to whole-body control, we show that risk-guided boundary sampling provides a theoretically grounded way to explore the edge of recoverability. Under the same safety specification, ceteris paribus, varying the sampling concentration produces strategies ranging from crouching to a novel backward-leaning limbo maneuver. In both settings, the learned policies transfer to hardware without online safety filtering, showing that learned safety synthesis scales to agile whole-body control.

</details>


### [224] [SeeQ: Training Generalist Value Functions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22085)
*Saksham Singh,Zheyuan Hu,Max Sobol Mark,Jeffrey Yu,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeeQ 提出面向当前活跃子任务的 Q 函数学习框架，利用离线机器人数据中的子任务标注缩短价值预测时域，并通过自回归自然语言子任务预测消除测试时人工标注与模块化子任务预测依赖，在真实双臂操作任务上显著提升 best-of-N 策略引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂长时程任务中仍表现脆弱，尤其是包含多阶段、需要重复尝试与 deliberation 的任务。基于任务级稀疏奖励学习 Q 函数虽可对候选动作排序或指导策略改进，但面临信用分配时域长、Bellman 备份困难、数据覆盖要求高等问题。

Method: SeeQ（Subtask-elicited Q-functions）改为学习当前活跃子任务的 Q 值，以缩短价值预测时域，使其可用时序差分（TD）目标有效学习。训练阶段利用离线机器人数据中已有的子任务级标注提供任务分解，从而可从广泛且可能次优的机器人数据集中学习。测试阶段无需人工标注或模块化子任务预测系统：Q 函数架构先以自然语言自回归预测当前活跃子任务，再估计其价值。实现上采用视觉-语言骨干网络，先在多样开源机器人操作数据上预训练，再在下游任务上微调。

Result: 在两个双臂机器人平台、四个真实世界操作任务上，SeeQ 价值函数显著提升了 best-of-N 策略引导性能。

Conclusion: SeeQ 通过子任务级 Q 函数与自回归自然语言子任务预测，缓解了长时程任务中稀疏奖励下的信用分配与价值学习难题，并减少了对测试时人工子任务标注和额外子任务预测模块的依赖，为利用大规模离线机器人数据改进通用策略提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle on complex, long-horizon tasks that comprise multiple stages or require repeated attempts and deliberation on the same underlying stage before success. Q-value functions can improve these policies by ranking candidate actions or guiding policy improvement, but learning from sparse task-level rewards entails long credit-assignment horizons, difficult Bellman backups, and broad data-coverage requirements. We introduce SeeQ (Subtask-elicited Q-functions), which instead learns Q-values for the currently active subtask. This shortens the value-prediction horizon and enables effective learning with temporal-difference (TD) objectives. During training, subtask-level annotations present in offline robot data provide the decomposition and enable learning from broad, potentially suboptimal robot datasets. To eliminate the need for human annotations or modular subtask prediction systems at test time, our Q-function architecture is trained to autoregressively predict the active subtask in natural language before estimating its value. We instantiate SeeQ using a base vision-language backbone, pretrain it on diverse open-source robot manipulation data, and finetune it on downstream tasks. Across four real-world manipulation tasks on two bimanual robot platforms, the SeeQ value function substantially improves best-of-N policy steering.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Physically Based Rendering in the Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21054)
*Vuk Radovanovic,Vishesh Gupta,Adrien Gruson,Binh-Son Hua*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该工作提出在生成模型的潜在特征空间中进行基于物理的渲染（PBR），通过修改渲染方程并配合可微渲染器，为扩散模型提供物理可控的内容生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像扩散模型生成能力强但难以控制，而传统计算机图形学管线（如基于物理的渲染）具有明确的物理可控性。作者观察到光传输现象与扩散模型潜在空间数值分布之间存在联系，因此希望将物理渲染引入潜在空间以增强生成可控性。

Method: 在生成模型所学习的VAE特征空间中进行光传输模拟，使PBR可在潜在空间中运行；对渲染方程进行修改，并结合可微渲染器优化场景参数，使其只需极少微调即可准确渲染到预训练潜在空间。方法仅使用单张渲染图像进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够泛化到场景几何变化、光照变化以及相机视角变化等情形，并输出可用于物理引导内容生成的潜在图。

Conclusion: 通过将基于物理的渲染与生成模型的潜在空间相桥接，可在保留扩散模型生成能力的同时引入物理可控性，为物理引导的生成式建模提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: Image diffusion models have shown impressive image generation capabilities but are often hard to control, in contrast to classical computer graphics pipelines such as physically based rendering. However, we observe that there is a bridge between light transport phenomena and the distribution of latent space values produced by such models. Thus, we introduce physically based rendering in the feature space learned by the variational autoencoders in generative models, enabling light transport simulation in the latent space. This allows us to leverage physically based rendering techniques to output latent maps for physically guided content generation. We propose modifications to the rendering equation, which, when paired with a differentiable renderer, can yield an optimal set of scene parameters that require only minimal refinement to accurately render into the pretrained latent space. We train our method on a single rendered image, and then demonstrate the generalization of the method to scene geometry changes, lighting changes, and camera view changes.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维分布学习中的维度灾难问题，提出"稀疏先验"与"稀疏维度"概念，证明在 k-稀疏先验下贝叶斯风险下界为 Ω(√(k/n))，并在温和假设下给出 TV 距离上的匹配上界，表明合适的先验可克服对样本量 n 的维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式 AI 方法的理论保证显示，从 n 个样本学习支撑于 d 维的分布，其误差以 O(n^{-1/Θ(d)}) 衰减。该速率虽被证明是极小极大最优的，但随维度急剧恶化，显得过于悲观。作者假设：仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画真实应用中分布的结构，因此需要引入更能反映实际分布稀疏性的假设，以突破维度灾难。

Method: 作者引入"稀疏先验"这一类分布先验，并定义"稀疏维度"（Sparse Dimension）作为衡量先验在全体分布空间上稀疏程度的度量。在此基础上分析 k-稀疏先验下的分布学习问题：一方面推导贝叶斯风险下界；另一方面针对 TV 距离，在温和附加假设下构造与之匹配的上界；同时建立贝叶斯框架下"分布学习"与"学习采样"之间的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下，k-稀疏先验的分布学习达到贝叶斯风险下界 Ω(√(k/n))；对 TV 距离，在温和附加假设下给出与下界匹配（在 n、k 上渐进意义下至多相差对数项）的上界。此外，证明贝叶斯设定下分布学习与学习采样在统计上等价，故结论同样适用于学习采样任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在合适的分布先验下学习，可克服关于样本量 n 的维度灾难，得到与 √(k/n) 一致的收敛速率。尽管 k 仍可能依赖于维度 d 或某种内在维度，但这一框架通过稀疏维度刻画了真实分布的有效复杂度，为高维分布学习提供了更乐观且可实现的统计保证。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [227] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出首个端到端商业智能（BI）基准 BI-Bench，并设计工具增强的 BI-Agent 与领域后训练框架，使 LLM 在复杂 BI 工作流上的准确率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BI 工作流要求用户手动完成识别相关表、数据转换、构建连接关系，最后才能回答业务问题，步骤繁琐耗时。尽管 LLM 在数据处理上能力较强，但其端到端回答 BI 问题的能力尚缺乏系统评估，因此需要构建基准并探索提升方法。

Method: 1) 从公开来源收集真实 BI 项目（Power BI、Tableau 等），从真实用户仪表盘中人工抽取（问题，标准答案）对，构建 BI-Bench 基准；2) 设计工具增强的 BI-Agent，将 BI 工作流分解为结构化数据上的子任务（搜索、连接、转换等），并编排各 BI 阶段的专用数据管理方法；3) 提出后训练框架，从真实 BI 项目合成训练轨迹，用监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）对 BI-Agent 进行后训练。

Result: 前沿 LLM 在 BI-Bench 上表现不佳，准确率不足 50%。BI-Agent 在原生 LLM 上最高带来约 40 个百分点的准确率提升，经后训练的 BI-Agent 再获得最高约 30 个百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在复杂 BI 工作流中结合工具增强推理与领域特定后训练至关重要，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [228] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA 是一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构：由 query 表示预测动态上下文阈值，训练时以乘性抑制代替删除子阈值 logits，从而在长上下文解码中实现硬件加速且不牺牲稠密模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，大规模 KV cache 造成严重的内存带宽瓶颈。稀疏注意力虽可通过选择性加载缓解，但刚性启发式会丢弃必要上下文，导致模型质量下降。因此需要一种既能获得硬件加速解码速度、又能保持稠密模型质量的方案。

Method: 提出 Elastic Threshold Attention（ETA）：1）从 query 表示直接预测动态、上下文相关的阈值，使困难检索或推理步骤获得稠密式上下文，常规 token 被剪枝；2）训练时对子阈值 logits 进行乘性抑制至零而非删除，避免表示坍缩，并形成平滑均匀注意力下限与分布式概率储备，使初始 token 上的局部注意力汇消失；3）推理时可硬剪枝无信息 KV 块，并吸收粗粒度 GPU 块选择误纳入的 token；4）实现 Triton 自定义解码 kernel，利用缓存的几何-概率边界以 O(1) 时间筛选 KV 块；5）提出离线校准算法，冻结每头常量阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B 预训练 ETA 模型在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文 needle retrieval 上可媲美稠密注意力，训练稀疏度约 85%，活跃解码密度约 38%。Triton 解码 kernel 在最长 512K token 序列上相对 FlashAttention-2 取得最高 2.5× 墙钟解码加速。离线校准可额外减少 27% 注意力计算。

Conclusion: ETA 表明，可训练的弹性动态阈值稀疏注意力能够在长上下文解码中同时实现显著硬件加速与稠密级模型质量，并通过定制 kernel 与离线校准支持领域特定部署。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [229] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Bio-MF：一种无潜变量、单步 MeanFlow 的 EEG 条件 fNIRS 生成框架，可在 7.0 ms 内生成一 trial fNIRS，并在下游混合 MI 解码中稳定提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG-fNIRS 混合运动想象 BCI 可优于纯 EEG 系统，但配对采集常不可用或不方便；现有 EEG-to-fNIRS 跨模态生成方法生成慢、常需预训练，难以满足实时 MI-BCI 需求。一步生成模型虽低延迟，但去除迭代精修会降低保真度并引入非生理伪影。

Method: Bio-MF 采用无潜变量的一步 MeanFlow 框架，直接在信号空间进行 x-prediction，并将其转换为 MeanFlow 速度监督，推理仅需一次网络评估。为在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构，方法整合空间-时间交互 4D 编码、跨模态无分类器引导以及噪声水平门控 FFT 正则化。

Result: 在 Dataset 1 上，EEG + 合成 fNIRS 相对纯 EEG 的 ACC 分别提升 3.37（HbR）和 4.15（HbO）个百分点；在 Dataset 2 未见过的 64 通道 EEG 蒙太奇下，增益分别为 2.98 和 2.50 个百分点。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上，生成一个 fNIRS trial 仅需 7.0 ms，相对 1000 步 SCDM 延迟实现 857 倍加速。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 实现了快速 EEG-to-fNIRS 合成，同时保持任务相关的生成质量，可用于下游混合 MI 解码；代码已开源。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [230] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以类星体光变曲线建模为背景，综述随机微分方程（SDE）与神经网络参数化结合的历史，并提出连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD）及无需求解HJB方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体亮度存在随机变化，其光变曲线携带超大质量黑洞信息；但从地基巡天时序数据中建模这些变化是统计难题，需要借助SDE与神经网络参数化。同时，优化中的探索与收敛平衡、连续时间策略梯度的可行性也是动机之一。

Method: 回顾SDE结合神经网络参数化处理类星体光变曲线建模的历史；提出Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent，使离散迭代过程依赖过去状态；在二维景观上进行仿真并调节超参数；提出连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构，用于探索性策略且不需求解HJB偏微分方程，并推导其最优性条件。

Result: 二维景观仿真表明，相较Vanilla SGD，所提方法通过调整超参数表现出更宽的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；强化学习框架的最优性条件可恢复此前工作中的Gibbs策略。

Conclusion: 该文将SDE建模、延迟记忆优化与连续时间强化学习相联系，说明无需解HJB PDE即可得到Gibbs最优策略，并为类星体光变等随机时序建模提供了方法框架。摘要未给出定量实验指标，主要贡献偏理论与方法综述。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [231] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作以RydbergGPT为对象，研究基于Rydberg原子阵列量子投影测量数据的神经标度律。结果表明：仅在临界点附近，损失随训练集规模的幂律描述（含损失下限修正）较好，且其两点统计与自然语言最接近；远离临界点时幂律描述变差、两点函数衰减更快。作者据此认为多尺度依赖性有助于稳定的神经标度，标度行为应视为“模型—数据”配对的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是检验神经标度律是否具有普遍性，还是依赖于具体数据分布；并探索量子多体临界性、测量数据统计结构与自然语言统计结构之间的关系，从而理解稳定神经标度出现的条件。

Method: 训练自回归Transformer模型RydbergGPT，数据为相互作用Rydberg原子阵列的量子比特投影测量结果，并通过改变激光失谐参数覆盖临界点附近与远离临界点区域。对损失随训练数据集大小的关系拟合幂律并加入损失下限修正；同时使用熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息“两点”函数，比较Rydberg测量数据与自然语言语料的统计结构。

Result: 在临界点附近，Transformer损失随训练集大小的变化可由带损失下限修正的幂律较好描述；远离临界性时，幂律描述质量显著下降。两点函数比较显示，临界附近的统计结构与自然语言最接近，而远离临界点的量子比特构型对应的两点函数衰减更快。

Conclusion: 结果支持如下假设：多尺度依赖性有助于形成稳定的神经标度行为；神经标度律不应仅视为模型属性，而应视为模型与数据分布共同决定的“模型—数据”配对属性。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [232] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在受控实验中揭示 LLM 评审递归训练会导致“科学判断坍缩”（scientific-judgment collapse），并提出 TrustReviewer 通过训练期数据筛选与测试期激活引导缓解评分压缩和语义多样性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 越来越多地作为自动评审或人类评审的助手参与科学评价，模型生成的评审会进入公开数据和未来训练语料，后续评审模型可能学习前代模型的判断，形成递归反馈循环。作者旨在实证这一风险，并提供可行干预。

Method: 以 Llama 3.1 8B 为基座，先在 ICLR 2018--2023 官方评审上微调评审模型，再用 ICLR 2024 数据、按系统变化比例混合官方评审与模型生成评审，训练四个后继模型。提出 TrustReviewer：训练期在精选语料上单阶段训练核心评审模型，以减少低质量和语义退化监督；测试期采用配对激活引导（paired activation steering），在不再训练、不增加专家标注的情况下纠正残余的坍缩判断倾向。

Result: 引入合成评审会压缩评分分布，并降低同一论文层面和语料库层面的语义多样性，作者称之为 scientific-judgment collapse。TrustReviewer 的两阶段干预可缓解该失败模式，并有助于改善推荐对齐。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了递归评审训练的一个具体风险，并为 AI 辅助科学评估中保持判断多样性和改善推荐对齐提供了实用干预方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [233] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端、基于模型的强化学习算法，用于在未知环境中针对给定线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范（如安全性或可达性）高效合成策略：将LTL任务的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）与环境贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步，并设计贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）实现近似贝叶斯最优策略合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中满足LTL规范的策略合成具有挑战性；传统非贝叶斯、无模型方法在探索-利用权衡和样本效率方面存在不足，且训练过程中容易产生任务违规。因此需要利用贝叶斯RL增强探索，并支持谨慎RL以减少违规。

Method: 1）用LDBA表示LTL任务；2）用BAMDP表示未知环境；3）将LDBA与BAMDP同步，构建同步BAMDP结构；4）提出新的BAMCP算法，在该同步结构中实现近似贝叶斯最优策略合成；5）与传统无模型方法及经典BAMCP进行对比和消融。

Result: 在有限时域和无限时域任务实验中，该方法在性质满足和样本效率方面均优于传统无模型方法；消融研究验证了新BAMCP算法相比经典BAMCP对LTL任务满足的价值；并成功应用于谨慎RL，减少了策略训练期间的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 该端到端模型基贝叶斯RL框架能够有效处理未知环境下的LTL策略合成问题，兼顾性质满足与样本效率，并可扩展至谨慎RL以降低训练违规。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [234] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将非平稳在线学习中的动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化的简单框架，通过构造辅助随机序列并借助现成的切换遗憾算法，统一导出强凸、指数凹与一般凸损失下的最优动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态遗憾是衡量在线学习器相对于时变比较器序列表现的重要指标。已有研究虽取得进展，但针对强凸和指数凹损失获得最优界通常需要复杂分析，因此作者希望提供一个更简单、更通用的分析框架。

Method: 核心是将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化。对任意比较器序列构造一个辅助随机序列，使其每轮无偏、方差可控且切换次数可管理；再结合合适的代理损失，将动态遗憾分解为对随机序列的期望切换遗憾及其可控方差。由此可直接调用具有切换遗憾保证的现成算法来推导动态遗憾界。

Result: 对于强凸和指数凹损失，得到 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ 的动态遗憾界；对于一般凸损失，同一归约可恢复 $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ 的动态遗憾界。其中 $T$ 为时间跨度，$P_T$ 为比较器序列的路径长度。这些结果均与三类损失下的 minimax 最优结果相匹配。

Conclusion: 该框架通过动态遗憾到切换遗憾的归约，简化了非平稳在线学习的分析，并统一达到强凸、指数凹和一般凸损失的最优动态遗憾界，体现了所提方法的通用性与简洁性。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [235] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将竞争性地质解释转化为替代空间先验、并依据其与水力水头观测的一致性进行排序的工作流；该方法结合文本到图像基础模型、变分自编码器、监督反演网络与稳态流动模拟，在合成基准和WIPP Culebra Dolomite案例中验证了其区分地质解释合理性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高后果地下决策常面临严重数据稀缺，专家可能对同一地下系统给出相互竞争的解释，而项目早期缺乏实用手段判断哪种解释最符合实际；这种不确定性往往要等到多口井钻探后才能消解，成本可达数百万美元。现有地质解释评估方法要么依赖主观判断，要么依赖早期调查中难以获得的密集数据。

Method: 将竞争性地质解释转化为不同的空间先验，并用水头观测对其进行排序。对每个解释，先用文本到图像基础模型生成包含1600幅地质图像的集合，再用单独训练的变分自编码器提供该解释特有的潜表示；监督反演网络将水头观测映射到该潜空间，冻结解码器生成图像并映射为对数导水率场；随后通过稳态流动模拟得到预测水头，将预测与观测的失配转换为高斯形式的兼容性评分。

Result: 在基于Johansen Formation的合成基准上，使用三种与参考表示一致性递减的解释进行925个测试案例评估：平均水头RMSE从Precise & Accurate解释的0.197升至Accurate解释的0.227，再到Mismatched解释的0.280。在Waste Isolation Pilot Plant的Culebra Dolomite Member两个已发表概念模型上，修订模型获得0.991的兼容性权重，而原始模型仅为0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流能够在数据稀缺的早期阶段，将竞争性地质解释定量排序，并利用水头观测识别更合理的空间先验；合成基准与实际案例均表明其具有区分力和应用潜力，可减少因解释不确定性导致的钻探风险与成本。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [236] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ASGARD：一种两阶段“教师-学生”框架，使基于强化学习的无人机控制策略在运行时对动作空间攻击具有韧性；教师阶段利用特权信息训练策略与监控器，学生阶段仅用机载物理状态历史进行监督学习部署，实验表明可完成任务并泛化到未见及隐蔽攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 控制器已用于无人机导航与控制，但其易受动作空间攻击：攻击者在策略生成动作命令之后、执行器执行之前篡改动作命令。现有防御多针对策略输入攻击；面向动作空间攻击的方法通常在训练阶段重训策略，无法在运行时应对被篡改动作，因此需要一种运行时鲁棒的控制方案。
