Method: ASGARD 采用两阶段教师-学生流水线。教师阶段：编码器将无人机物理状态与“动作攻击相关”的特权信息融合，生成攻击感知的潜在表示，用于训练 RL 控制策略，并训练一个监控器向执行器输出校正后的动作命令。学生阶段：编码器与监控器通过监督学习从教师对应模块蒸馏，使其仅依赖无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。

Result: 在针对无人机不同动作命令的多类攻击场景中评估：ASGARD 对动作空间攻击具有韧性，即使遭受攻击也能完成既定任务；同时可泛化到未见攻击，并在隐蔽攻击下保持韧性。

Conclusion: ASGARD 通过教师-学生架构将动作攻击感知能力迁移到仅依赖物理状态历史的机载模块，为 RL 无人机控制提供了运行时动作空间攻击防御，并展现出对未知与隐蔽攻击的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [237] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对可穿戴生理信号情绪识别中的时序深度学习架构进行系统性比较，在WESAD与EmoWear两个多模态数据集上评估Bi-LSTM、TCN与Transformer，并分析传感配置、集成、消融与采样频率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多仅评估单一模型、传感配置或数据集，难以判断模型、传感器与数据特性对情绪识别性能的相对影响，缺乏可指导实际部署的系统性比较证据。

Method: 采用参与者独立的留一被试交叉验证（LOSO-CV），在手腕、胸部及多模态传感配置下比较双向LSTM、TCN与Transformer；进一步考察软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率与基于梯度的显著性分析。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态上取得最高准确率99.02%±0.51%；LSTM在EmoWear多模态上表现最佳，唤醒度91.80%±1.06%、效价89.96%±0.36%。多模态传感在两个数据集上均优于仅手腕或仅胸部配置；4 Hz在显著降低训练成本的同时可达到与更高采样频率相当的性能。

Conclusion: 架构的相对优劣取决于数据集特性，不存在普遍最优模型；实际应用应优先采用多模态传感，并将采样频率设为约4 Hz作为性能与计算成本的折中点，为可穿戴生理情绪识别的模型、模态与频率选择提供指导。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [238] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MOSAIC-SR：用预训练 Transformer 生成多个初始表达式草图，并以其初始化多区域搜索，结合尺度感知常数优化与局部符号修复，从而在符号回归中兼顾灵活结构搜索与高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归旨在从观测数据中恢复闭式方程，以获得可解释模型。现有方法难以兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效推理：搜索类方法可调整表达式结构，但依赖昂贵的组合优化且常从随机初始化开始；预训练神经模型虽能近乎即时生成公式，但预测结果常含符号错误。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 使用预训练 Transformer 提出多个初始 sketch（表达式草图），将这些草图作为多个有希望区域的搜索起点，避免在庞大表达式空间中进行随机初始化。每个搜索过程通过尺度感知的常数优化与局部符号修复，联合恢复表达式结构与常数。

Result: 在 SRSD-Feynman 数据集（含与不含 dummy variables）以及六个额外基准上评估。MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集上取得最高的符号解率（symbolic solution rate），预测精度位列前二；在存在无关 dummy 输入时该优势仍然保持。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习到的先验可将搜索聚焦于有希望的方程结构，而数值优化与符号修复对表达式恢复具有重要作用。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [239] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出：MCR² 在分布偏移下可能完全失效；仅达到编码最优并不能保证 OOD 稳定预测，且直接引入 IRM/REx 的不变性原理也无法消除该失败，需额外假设或学习原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR² 作为信息论框架，可学习类子流形的结构化、判别性表示，并启发了可解释白盒架构；但作者观察到其在分布偏移下会完全失败，因此需要研究其 OOD 泛化极限。

Method: 通过理论构造与反例分析：构造完全依赖不稳定环境特征的表示，使其达到全局编码最优但在相关性反转后预测失败；构造训练中所有可能测试输入均可出现、编码质量任意接近最优但预测误差接近 100% 的情形；并检验将 IRM/REx 不变性原理直接结合到 MCR² 后是否消除失败。

Result: 得到两个限制：第一，MCR² 目标本身可允许完全预测失败，即使存在完全稳定的特征；编码最优与预测稳定不一致。第二，直接引入 IRM/REx 的不变性原理不能消除失败；失败表示在训练环境间可共享同一最优编码算子，说明共享编码最优不保证稳定预测。

Conclusion: 要使 MCR² 获得可靠的 OOD 保证，需要额外的假设或学习原则，以建立跨环境稳定的预测关系。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [240] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体在测试时的通信（test-time communication）对复杂任务求解能力的影响，发现在算力充足且有明确反馈时，通信团队显著优于独立并行尝试，并能在研究型任务上超越此前最佳方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统很少体现"协作推动科学进步"这一特性；通信智能体是否真正有益仍是悬而未决的问题，且以往结果参差不齐。作者希望厘清测试时通信在多智能体系统中的实际增益及其适用条件。

Method: 在ARC-AGI-3基准上研究多智能体测试时通信的规模效应：智能体无预设角色，通过共享目录（shared directory）通信，构成 k 个通信智能体组成的团队 team@k，并与独立并行尝试 best@k 进行对比；同时将方法迁移到多联骨牌填充（polyomino packing）与 MNIST 分类器压缩等研究型任务。

Result: team@k 的成功率可匹配 4k 个独立智能体，且优势随 k 增大而增长；存在单智能体无法解决、但团队能可靠解决的任务。在多联骨牌填充上超越 best@k 并超过此前最佳已知分数；在 MNIST 分类器压缩上，四智能体团队产出 1,957 字节、99.4% 测试精度的提交，小于最佳人类方案与最佳单智能体结果。但该增益并非无条件：算力有限或缺乏明确进度度量时，独立智能体可能更优。

Conclusion: 在算力充足且反馈明确的前提下，多智能体通信能持续带来更强结果；团队规模越大，通信带来的复合增益越明显，并可解决个体难以独立完成的难题。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [241] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Stiefel-AdamW，通过将线性分解块（W=BA）中的一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上，把 GL(r) 规范对称性降为紧致正交对称性，从而消除因子爆炸、提升训练稳定性，且几乎不增加开销，可作为 AdamW 的近似即插即用替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习广泛存在线性分解块 W=BA（如 LoRA 适配器、低秩压缩层、自注意力的 query-key 乘积），其共同问题是分解非唯一，导致训练不稳定并限制可用学习率。然而这类模块通常仍用忽略底层几何结构的标准欧氏方法优化。作者希望在不牺牲 AdamW 逐元素对角预条件这一实践优势的前提下，解决因子非唯一带来的不稳定问题。

Method: 提出 Stiefel-AdamW：将分解块中的一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上，另一因子保持欧氏，从而把完整的 GL(R^r) 规范对称性松弛为紧致正交对称性，排除因子爆炸，同时保留 AdamW 的逐坐标对角预条件能力。矩估计在环境欧氏空间中完成，几何仅通过切空间投影与流形回缩（retraction）介入，实现上相对 AdamW 的开销极小。

Result: 在 GPT2、ViT、Mistral 7B 的 LoRA 风格微调以及 GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的完整预训练中验证 Stiefel-AdamW，相较强基线均取得一致提升，且相较 AdamW 基本无额外成本。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW 兼具黎曼方法的稳定性优势与标准收敛保证，可作为 AdamW 的近似即插即用替代，适用于一切出现线性分解块的场景，并以极小的实现开销带来一致性能提升。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [242] [A Lightweight Plug-in Gate for Transformer-Based Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21044)
*Hongkai Zhuang,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"编码器前的协变量准入（pre-encoder covariate admission）"形式化为一种输入侧接口，用轻量级的表示级门控对表示单元赋予 sigmoid 分数，并作为插件模块接入 TimeXer、iTransformer、PatchTST 进行零额外调参评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量丰富的时间序列预测需要决定外部变量如何进入目标预测路径。现有基于 Transformer 的预测器通常直接构建协变量表示并送入编码器，缺乏显式的"准入"阶段，即对外部变量是否、以及在多大程度上被纳入编码处理的显式控制。

Method: 将编码器前的协变量准入视为在编码处理前即时调节协变量表示的输入侧接口，并用轻量级表示级预编码门控实现：为表示单元分配 sigmoid 分数；同时研究一种使用正则化变体，对平均准入值施加惩罚。该接口作为插件模块接入 TimeXer、Inverted Transformer（iTransformer）与 Patch Time Series Transformer（PatchTST），采用零额外调参协议，各门控模型继承对应基线配置。

Result: 在 ETTm1、ETTm2（Electricity Transformer Temperature 分钟级）、Traffic、Energy 及 ILI（流感样疾病）数据集上开展实验，涵盖成对预测比较、门控位置消融、初始化消融、受控协变量准入分析，以及基于方差膨胀因子（VIF）的置换特征重要性（PFI）诊断案例研究。在测试设置中，门控与对应基线相比具有竞争力；使用惩罚项降低了平均准入分数，同时将预测误差保持在接近未加惩罚的 TimeXer 水平。

Conclusion: 编码器前的协变量准入可作为调节协变量表示的有效输入侧接口，轻量级表示级门控在多个基线上具备竞争力；使用正则化可在几乎不损失精度的前提下降低平均准入水平，表明该接口为控制外部变量进入预测路径提供了可行的机制。

Abstract: Covariate-rich time-series forecasting requires deciding how external variables enter the target forecasting path. Existing Transformer-based forecasters usually build a covariate representation and pass it to the encoder without an explicit admission stage. This paper studies pre-encoder covariate admission as an input-side interface that regulates that representation immediately before encoder processing. We implement the interface with a lightweight representation-level pre-encoder gate that assigns sigmoid scores to representation units, and we also study a usage-regularized variant that penalizes average admission. The interface is evaluated as a plug-in module for TimeXer, Inverted Transformer (iTransformer), and Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) under a zero-extra-tuning protocol, where each gated model inherits the corresponding baseline configuration. Experiments on the Electricity Transformer Temperature minute-level (ETTm1 and ETTm2) datasets, Traffic, Energy, and influenza-like illness (ILI) include paired forecasting comparisons, gate-placement ablation, initialization ablation, controlled covariate-admission analysis, and a variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed permutation feature importance (PFI) diagnostic case study. In the tested settings, the gate is competitive with the corresponding baselines, and the usage penalty reduces average admission scores while keeping forecasting errors close to the unpenalized TimeXer setting.

</details>


### [243] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文刻画了在客户端参与分布未知时 FedAvg 实际优化的目标，并提出嵌入 CVaR 的风险规避联邦平均算法 FedeRage，在非 IID 与随机参与场景下提升精度、公平性与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可在不共享原始数据的前提下协同训练模型，但在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据和随机客户端参与下性能下降。基于经典联邦平均（FedAvg）的改进方法通常假设服务器已知客户端参与概率，而实际部署中该假设往往不成立。因此，需要研究参与完全未知、可能高度偏斜且每轮参与规模可变时的优化问题，并设计不依赖参与分布先验的鲁棒聚合方法。

Method: 作者首先讨论并刻画“分布不可知”的 FedAvg 在参与情况完全未知时实际求解的优化问题：均匀聚合被证明可在凸且可能非光滑损失下，以标准 O(1/√T) 速率最小化一个由参与诱导边际加权的随机目标。基于该刻画，提出 Federated Risk-Averse Averaging（FedeRage），将条件风险价值（CVaR）嵌入本地目标，置于分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架中。FedeRage 隐式提高高损失且低参与频率客户端的权重，每客户端仅增加一个标量参数，并获得 O(κ/√T) 速率，其中 κ 是风险规避“代价”的上界。与依赖最优传输、需要可用性分布作为输入的聚合对齐方案不同，FedeRage 对参与分布保持不可知。

Result: 在三个异构基准上的实验表明，FedeRage 在准确率、公平性和收敛速度方面相较当前最先进方法取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上阐明了参与分布未知时 FedAvg 的隐含优化目标，并给出一种仅增加单标量开销、对参与分布不可知的风险规避聚合方法 FedeRage；其在 non-IID 与随机参与条件下兼具收敛保证和实证优势，为实际联邦部署提供了更鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance degrades under non-IID data and stochastic client participation. Remedies built on classical Federated Averaging (FedAvg) typically presuppose that client participation probabilities are known to the server, which is rarely the case in deployed systems. We first discuss and then characterize the optimization problem that \emph{distributionally agnostic} FedAvg actually solves when participation is entirely unknown, possibly highly skewed, and of variable size across rounds: uniform aggregation is shown to minimize a well-defined stochastic objective, weighted by the participation-induced marginal, at a standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for convex and possibly nonsmooth losses. Building on this characterization, we propose \emph{Federated Risk-Averse Averaging} (\textsc{FedeRage}), a risk-averse extension of FedAvg that embeds the \emph{Conditional Value-at-Risk} (CVaR) into the local objective within a natural distributionally robust optimization (DRO) framework. \textsc{FedeRage} implicitly upweights high-loss and infrequently participating clients while adding only a \emph{single scalar per-client}, and admits an $\mathcal{O}(κ/\sqrt{T})$ rate in which the factor $κ$ is the upper bound on the ``price" of risk aversion. In contrast with aggregation-alignment schemes based on optimal transport, which require the availability distribution as an input, \textsc{FedeRage} remains agnostic to it. Several experiments on three heterogeneous benchmarks indicate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, fairness, and convergence speed.

</details>


### [244] [Toward individual-level calibration in affect recognition with perceptual adjustment queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21073)
*Xuanzhou Chen,Sankaraleengam Alagapan,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于知觉调整查询（PAQ）的个体化知觉校准框架，用于估计面部情感谱上的最小可觉差（JND），从而构建难度等价的情感知觉任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为任务通常假设相同刺激对所有被试具有同等知觉难度，但个体知觉敏感性差异会系统性地破坏这一假设，因此需要按个体知觉难度进行归一化。

Method: 以情感知觉任务为测试平台，通过认知负担较轻的PAQ直接估计每名被试在面部情感谱上的JND；利用PAQ推断的JND重新表达刺激距离，在知觉空间中构建难度等价任务。采用二选一强制选择（2AFC）任务，以二元元认知难度判断和反应时方差分解两种互补行为指标进行验证，并与未校准基线及群体水平Weibull校准比较。

Result: PAQ校准在个体水平上显著均衡了主观任务难度，优于未校准基线和群体水平Weibull校准；同时降低了平均反应时及反应时的被试间方差。

Conclusion: PAQ可作为面部情感识别中个体化知觉校准的一种有原则且实用的工具。

Abstract: Behavioral tasks measuring facial affect perception assume that identical stimuli impose equivalent perceptual difficulty across participants. However, this assumption is systematically violated by individual differences in perceptual sensitivity. Using an affective perception task as our testbed, we propose a framework to normalize for perceptual difficulty that directly estimates each participant's Just Noticeable Difference (JND) along the facial affect spectrum via cognitively lightweight perceptual adjustment queries (PAQs). We use these PAQ-inferred JNDs to re-express stimulus distances, constructing difficulty-equated tasks in perceptual space. We validate the framework in a Two-Alternative Forced-Choice (2AFC) task using two complementary behavioral measures: binary metacognitive difficulty judgments and response time variance decomposition. We find that PAQ calibration significantly equalizes perceived task difficulty at an individual level when compared to both the non-calibrated baseline and population-level Weibull calibration, while also reducing mean response time and between-subject variance in response time. These results establish PAQ as a principled and practical instrument for individualized perceptual calibration in facial affect recognition.

</details>


### [245] [REFINEPPO: Learning Continuous Control Policies by Iterative Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21108)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Sagar Kamarthi,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出迭代动作细化（IAR）：用共享细化网络对初始动作提议施加一系列学习的残差修正，逐步构造控制动作，并与 PPO 结合为 REFINEPPO；在 14 个基准控制任务上与标准 PPO 相当或更优，且部分任务收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在连续控制中表现优异，但其策略通常被定义为从观测状态到动作（或动作分布）的直接映射，要求单个前馈网络一次性给出最优控制决策。这种单次前向的范式使策略在形成初始预测后，几乎没有机会重新考虑或逐步改进该动作。

Method: 提出迭代动作细化（IAR）：不再只学习直接预测动作，而是通过一系列学习的残差修正来构造动作。从初始提议出发，共享的细化网络反复以观测状态与当前动作提议为条件，每一步都可修正前序步骤所构造的动作，最终细化后的提议决定智能体执行的动作。将该迭代动作构造机制与近端策略优化（PPO）集成，得到 REFINEPPO。实验在 14 个基准控制任务上评估，并辅以细化深度、更新调度等受控消融，以及旨在理解迭代细化有效原因的分析。

Result: 在 14 个基准环境中，REFINEPPO 的性能与标准 PPO 相当或更优，并在若干任务上表现出更快的收敛速度。细化深度与更新调度的消融实验进一步刻画了该机制的行为特征，相关分析为迭代细化为何有效提供了依据。

Conclusion: 迭代式动作构建可作为直接动作预测的有效替代方案：通过残差式的多步修正，策略获得了对动作进行反复调整与渐进改进的能力，从而在不牺牲（甚至提升）性能的前提下加快学习。该机制与 PPO 的集成方式具有通用性，可为连续控制策略设计提供新的思路。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has achieved strong performance across a wide range of continuous-control problems. These continuous-control policies, however, are often defined as direct mappings from an observed state to an action or action distribution, requiring a single feed-forward network to construct an optimal control decision in one pass. While effective, this formulation leaves little opportunity for the policy to reconsider or progressively improve an action once an initial prediction has been formed. In this work, we explore an alternative approach: rather than learning only to directly predict an action, can a policy learn to iteratively improve one, and can this iterative process provide advantages during policy learning? We introduce Iterative Action Refinement (IAR), an iterative action-construction method that constructs control actions through a sequence of learned residual corrections. Starting from an initial proposal, a shared refinement network repeatedly conditions on the observed state and the current action proposal, allowing each refinement step to revise the action constructed by preceding steps. The final refined proposal is then used to determine the action executed by the agent. We integrate this iterative action-construction mechanism with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), yielding REFINEPPO. We evaluate REFINEPPO across 14 benchmark control tasks, complemented by controlled ablations of refinement depth and update schedules and analyses aimed at understanding why iterative refinement is effective. Across these environments, REFINEPPO matches or exceeds the performance of standard PPO while demonstrating faster convergence on several tasks.

</details>


### [246] [Talk to Me, Jarvis: An Open-Source Edge-Deployable Voice Assistant Framework for Autonomous Racecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21109)
*Daniel Henel,Frederik Werner,Alexander Langmann,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Jarvis：面向自动驾驶高层行为指令的离线语音助手，将语音识别/合成与基于 Mistral 7B 领域微调的文本到命令分类器集成于轻量本地框架；在实验中取得 97.63% 意图识别准确率和 1.39 s 平均延迟，优于更大的在线托管模型，并开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽提升了语音助手的意图理解与上下文相关输入分类能力，但在线托管模型存在网络依赖和推理延迟波动问题，难以满足自动驾驶等时间关键型应用对低延迟与可靠性的要求。

Method: 开发 Jarvis 离线语音助手，面向自动驾驶车辆的高层行为命令；架构将语音识别、语音合成与自然语言命令分类整合为轻量级本地框架。核心组件为文本到命令分类器，通过对 Mistral 7B 进行领域特定微调实现低延迟推理。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方案优于更大的在线托管模型，意图识别准确率达到 97.63%，平均处理延迟为 1.39 s，适合需要快速响应的操作场景；同时提供开源实现以支持后续研究与微调。

Conclusion: 通过本地化、领域微调的轻量 LLM 语音助手，可在无网络依赖条件下实现高准确率与低延迟的车辆行为指令理解，为自动驾驶场景中的实时语音交互提供可行方案，并以开源形式促进后续研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.

</details>


### [247] [Signal-Centric Remote Sensing via Alternative Preprocessing and Acoustic Processing for ML-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21123)
*Logan Luna,Sirio Jansen-Sánchez,Ilteris Demirkiran,Leo Ghelarducci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以CSV格式替代传统图像化表示来处理声纳/遥感数据，实验显示处理时间减少91.18%，并改善机器学习目标检测精度及SNR、PSNR等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流声纳数据处理依赖图像化表示，需要在自主系统上对图像数据进行预处理，计算开销大、实时性受限；作者希望寻找更高效的数据处理替代方案。

Method: 不采用图像表示，而是使用逗号分隔值（CSV）格式处理遥感/声纳数据；在实验中比较该替代方法对处理时间、机器学习目标检测以及SNR、PSNR等评价指标的影响。

Result: 处理时间减少91.18%；机器学习目标检测准确率提升；SNR、PSNR及其他评价指标均有所提高。

Conclusion: CSV式数据表示可作为声纳/遥感应用中的有效替代处理路径，在效率、检测精度和信号质量指标上优于图像化表示；但其泛化性、数据维度与空间信息保留等问题仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The dominant method of processing sonar data is using image-based representations, requiring the preprocessing of image data on autonomous systems. We propose an alternative data processing method for remote sensing applications via the use of data in Comma-Seperated Value format. Experimentation on our alternative approach shows a reduction of processing time by 91.18%, an improvement in accurate object detection by Machine Learning, and an increase in SNR (Signal-to-noise ratio), PSNR (Peak signal-to-noise ratio), and other evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [248] [Layerwise Decoupling for Stable Structured Sparsification of Fully Connected Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21126)
*Charles Kulick,Armenak Petrosyan,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦、逐层的结构化稀疏化方法，用于剪枝预训练神经网络的全连接层；证明其约束目标在最优处等价于特定的内外权重联合惩罚，并显示其比联合基线更稳健、灾难性过剪枝更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对所有层联合施加惩罚的耦合式稀疏化方法可能存在正则强度敏感、层间优化耦合以及灾难性过剪枝等问题，因此需要一种更稳健、可控的结构化稀疏化方案。

Method: 方法为解耦逐层处理：从预训练网络中抽取浅层两子网络，归一化内层权重，并对外层权重矩阵施加结构化组惩罚；按层顺序处理以剪除神经元并降低每层宽度。作者进一步证明，对任意正齐次激活函数，约束解耦目标在最优性与特定内外权重联合惩罚等价，因此可获得干净的投影与近端形式。

Result: 数值实验表明，该解耦重构比耦合方法更稳健：相比所测试的联合基线，具有更宽的正则化强度可用范围，灾难性过剪枝率更低，同时保持可比精度。作者在受控分类与稀疏恢复研究中验证了这些性质，并在高维PINN压力测试和OPT-1.3B前馈层中考察其适用范围。

Conclusion: 解耦逐层结构化稀疏化是一种更稳健的预训练网络剪枝思路，能够在保持精度的同时降低过剪枝风险，并具备从受控任务到大规模模型前馈层的适用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled, layerwise method for structurally sparsifying the fully connected layers of pretrained neural networks. Rather than penalizing all layers jointly, our approach extracts shallow two-layer subnetworks, normalizes the inner weights, and applies a structured group penalty to the outer weight matrix of each block, processing layers sequentially to prune neurons and reduce the width of each layer. We prove that the constrained decoupled objective is equivalent at optimality to a specific joint penalty on the inner and outer weights, for any positively homogeneous activation, and thus admits a clean projected and proximal formulation. Our central finding is that this decoupled reformulation is more robust than coupled methods. In numerical experiments it provides a wider usable range of the regularization strength and a lower rate of catastrophic over-pruning than the tested joint baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy. We establish these properties in controlled classification and sparse-recovery studies, and examine their scope in a high-dimensional PINN stress test and in the feed-forward layers of OPT-1.3B.

</details>


### [249] [EnSol: an environment-aware graph neural network for molecular solubility prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21151)
*Thao Nguyen,Saman Shafaei,Zhengyi Zhang,Huimin Zhao,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnSol是一个环境感知的概率框架，用分子图分别表示溶质与溶剂，经交叉注意力建模相互作用，并通过特征级温度调制和混合密度网络输出完整溶解度分布；在SolProp、Leeds及实验验证中均优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子溶解度影响反应可行性、制剂性能、分离效率和溶剂选择，但实验测量成本高且采样稀疏。现有计算模型常依赖固定溶剂假设、确定性建模或简化的溶质-溶剂相互作用，难以同时刻画复杂分子相互作用、连续温度效应和实验不确定性。

Method: EnSol将溶质和溶剂表示为分子图并分别学习表征，再通过交叉注意力融合以捕捉溶质-溶剂相互作用；温度通过特征级调制直接注入溶剂环境；采用混合密度网络预测完整溶解度分布，以同时建模温度依赖行为和实验不确定性。

Result: 在独立的SolProp和Leeds基准数据集上，EnSol分别取得0.876和0.601的Spearman相关系数，均优于当前最先进的溶解度预测模型；在化学多样性溶质-溶剂对上的实验验证中，模型保持较强预测性能并支持可靠溶剂排序，Spearman相关系数为0.715。

Conclusion: EnSol能够在多种化学体系中实现可靠的溶解度预测和溶剂选择，并能对预测不确定性进行建模。

Abstract: Molecular solubility directly affects key aspects of molecular development such as reaction feasibility, formulation performance, separation efficiency, and solvent selection. However, experimental measurement across solutes, solvents, and temperatures remains costly and sparsely sampled. Existing computational models often rely on fixed-solvent assumptions, deterministic formulations, or simplified representations of solute-solvent interactions, limiting their ability to capture complex molecular interactions, continuous temperature effects, and experimental uncertainty. Here, we introduce EnSol, an environment-aware probabilistic framework for molecular solubility prediction. EnSol represents the solute and solvent as molecular graphs and learns separate representations for each before bringing them together through cross-attention to capture solute-solvent interactions. Temperature is incorporated directly into the solvent environment through feature-wise modulation, and a mixture density network predicts full solubility distributions to capture both temperature-dependent behavior and experimental uncertainty. On the independent SolProp and Leeds benchmark datasets, EnSol achieved Spearman correlations of 0.876 and 0.601, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art solubility prediction models across both benchmarks. Beyond computational benchmarking, experimental validation across chemically diverse solute-solvent pairs showed that EnSol maintained strong predictive performance and supported reliable solvent ranking, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.715. These results show that EnSol can support reliable solubility prediction and solvent selection across diverse chemical systems while accounting for predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [250] [HMB-GAN: Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN for Vector Shape Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21158)
*Elian Hugh Thiele-Evans,Binh Duong Pham,Hani Omar M Alharbi,Liibaan Aaden,Syed Umer Hasnain Zaidi,Prem Prakash Jayaraman,Muhammad Saeed,Boris Eisenbart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HMB-GAN，一种混合量子-经典、端到端可微的多段 Bézier 生成对抗框架，用于合成 CAD 可用的闭合矢量几何；实验显示量子生成器收敛更快、参数更少、点云指标略优，但受限于模拟器开销与硬件约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法多局限于栅格化或单 Bézier 域，难以直接生成具有结构化几何连续性的 CAD 矢量形状；同时需探索量子增强生成器在结构化几何建模中的可行性与局限。

Method: 提出 HMB-GAN（Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN）：以拼接多段 Bézier 表示构造闭合形状，并通过构造方式强制几何连续性；框架端到端可微。在同一架构下对比量子增强生成器与经典生成器。

Result: 量子生成器收敛更快、模型参数量减少，并在点云分布指标上略有提升；但模拟器开销过大，基于经典模拟的评估受硬件约束限制。

Conclusion: 结果证明通过混合量子架构建模结构化几何具有可行性，同时凸显当前量子硬件与模拟资源的现实局限。

Abstract: We explore the use of hybrid quantum-classical generative adversarial networks for synthesising CAD-ready vector geometries. Unlike prior work that operates in rasterised or single-Bézier domains, we introduce HMB-GAN (Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN), an end-to-end differentiable generative framework that constructs closed shapes through stitched multi-segment Bézier representations with geometric continuity enforced by construction. We compare a quantum-enhanced generator with a classical generator within this architecture and evaluate them across point cloud distribution metrics and geometric shape statistics. Results show that despite faster convergence, a reduction in model parameter count, and slightly improved performance on point cloud metrics, the quantum generator suffers from excessive simulator overhead and thus classically-simulated evaluation suffers from hardware constraints. These results demonstrate the feasibility of modelling structured geometries through hybrid quantum architectures whilst highlighting contemporary hardware limitations.

</details>


### [251] [M2G-LLM: Enhancing Clinical Prediction via Multimodal Graph Reasoning and LLM Context Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21164)
*Inyoung Choi,Sukwon Yun,Jiayi Xin,Jie Peng,Tianlong Chen,Qi Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 M2G-LLM（Multimodal MedGraph-LLM），通过 GNN 增强 LLM 的多模态整合与对齐能力，将异构医疗数据构建为多模态上下文向量并注入 LLM 中间层，在 MIMIC-IV 与 MIMIC-CXR 上提升临床预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策需要整合临床笔记、实验室结果和医学影像等多种数据模态。尽管 LLM 在处理非结构化临床文本方面表现突出，但其纳入非文本模态的能力有限，制约了在医疗场景中的广泛应用，因此需要一种能融合多源异构数据并支持联合推理的框架。

Method: 提出 M2G-LLM 框架：利用 GNN 建模患者多次就诊之间的时序关系，并在临床相似患者之间传播信息；对齐异构数据源，构建增强的多模态上下文向量；将这些向量注入 LLM 的中间层，使模型能够对文本与非文本模态进行联合推理。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 和 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上进行评估，M2G-LLM 在临床预测任务上相较强基线模型取得提升，验证了多模态图增强 LLM 的有效性。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 的语言理解能力与 GNN 的关系推理能力相结合，为全面、多模态的医疗分析提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Integrating diverse data modalities --- such as clinical notes, laboratory results, and medical imaging --- is essential for advancing clinical decision-making. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable performance in processing unstructured clinical text, their limited capacity to incorporate non-text modalities hinders their broader utility in healthcare applications. Here, we introduce M2G-LLM (Multimodal MedGraph-LLM), a novel framework that enhances LLMs with multimodal integration and alignment via Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Our approach models temporal relationships between patient visits, propagates information across clinically similar patients, and aligns heterogeneous data sources to construct enriched multimodal context vectors. These vectors are injected into the intermediate layers of the LLM, enabling joint reasoning over textual and non-textual modalities. We evaluate M2G-LLM on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets, demonstrating improvements in clinical prediction tasks over strong baseline models. Our results highlight the promise of combining the language understanding of LLMs with the relational reasoning capabilities of GNNs for comprehensive, multimodal healthcare analysis.

</details>


### [252] [TierKV: Long-Context On-Device LLMs via Predictive Multi-Tier KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21172)
*Zhihao Shu,Md Musfiqur Rahman Sanim,Jie Hu,Kun Yuan,Minghai Qin,Gagan Agrawal,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TierKV 是面向移动端 LLM 推理的框架，基于 Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization (PMCO)：在解码前利用 prefill 隐藏状态预测未来 KV 缓存需求，并在设备内存与精度预算下将 token 联合分配到 exact、low-rank 与 flash-offloaded 三层；运行时通过闭式求解器选择层级边界与逐层秩。实验在 3 种移动 SoC、8 个文本/视觉/音频模型上，prefill 吞吐最高提升 17.6 倍，RAM 常驻 KV 缓存降低 12.5–34%，在相同内存预算下支持更长上下文，且精度损失较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 正迁移到移动设备并处理文本、图像、视频、音频等多样化负载，长上下文需求使 KV 缓存成为主要内存瓶颈：其随序列长度线性增长，且每个解码步都被访问。已有低秩压缩、token 驱逐、flash 卸载等方法虽能降低 KV 缓存占用，但会带来重建开销、不可逆的 token 信息损失或 I/O 停顿，从而抵消节省内存的收益。

Method: 提出 TierKV 移动端 LLM 推理框架，核心为 Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization (PMCO)。PMCO 在解码开始前，从 prefill 隐藏状态预测未来缓存需求，并在设备内存与精度预算约束下，将 token 联合分配到 exact、low-rank 和 flash-offloaded 三类缓存层级。该建模保留完整上下文访问，消除反应式驱逐的循环依赖，并支持闭式求解器在运行时选择层级边界与逐层秩。

Result: 在三种移动 SoC 与八个文本、视觉、音频模型上，TierKV 相比现有移动 LLM 框架将 prefill 吞吐最高提升 17.6 倍；将 RAM 常驻 KV 缓存降低 12.5–34%，从而在相同内存预算下支持显著更长的上下文；同时仅带来较小的精度退化。

Conclusion: TierKV 通过预测式多层级 KV 缓存优化，在移动端长上下文 LLM 推理中同时改善吞吐、内存占用与可支持上下文长度，并在精度上保持较低损失，为移动设备高效 LLM 推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are moving onto mobile devices for increasingly diverse workloads over text, images, video, and audio. These applications often require long contexts, making the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck because it grows linearly with sequence length and is accessed at every decoding step. Prior work reduces KV-cache footprint through low-rank compression, token eviction, or flash offloading, but the resulting reconstruction overhead, irreversible token loss, or I/O stalls can offset the benefit of saving memory. We present TierKV, a mobile LLM inference framework built on Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization (PMCO). Before decoding starts, PMCO predicts future cache demand from prefill hidden states and jointly assigns tokens to exact, low-rank, and flash-offloaded tiers under the device memory and accuracy budgets. This formulation retains access to the full context, removes the circular dependency of reactive eviction, and admits a closed-form solver that selects tier boundaries and per-layer ranks at runtime. Across eight text, vision, and audio models on three mobile SoCs, TierKV improves prefill throughput by up to 17.6x over existing mobile LLM frameworks, reduces RAM-resident KV cache by 12.5-34%, thereby enabling substantially longer contexts under the same memory budget, while incurring only minor accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [253] [SWE-Proof: Can Language Models Resolve Real-World Issues with Machine-Checked Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21190)
*George Ma,Benjamin Mikek,Haoyu Li,Ferhat Erata,Yuhao Zhang,Zeren Shui,Behrooz Omidvar Tehrani,Jun Huan,Murali Krishna Ramanathan,Somayeh Sojoudi,Hao Zhou,Anoop Deoras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Benchproofer 流水线，将带正确补丁的编码任务转化为形式化验证任务，构建 SWE-Proof（500 个真实 issue，正确性经形式化验证而非测试），并揭示测试通过补丁中大量存在反例、规范编写（尤其忠实性）是核心难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic 代码生成基准依赖留出测试集，测试本身不完整且易受记忆污染；形式化验证虽能规避这两点，但已有工作仅覆盖规范作为输入给出的独立任务，无法处理涉及大型仓库、意图以模糊自然语言表述的真实 issue。

Method: Benchproofer：给定编码任务及其已知正确补丁，为新代码编写规范，并用公理概括被调用既有函数；仅当机械门与对抗门一致通过时才接纳实例。应用于 SWE-bench Verified 得到 SWE-Proof，并可扩展至 SWE-bench Pro。

Result: 在两个前沿模型上，形式化验证捕获了测试遗漏的问题：约四分之一到一半的测试通过补丁存在反例；结构化自然语言规范无法修复，而正确形式化规范将 Opus 4.8 的解决率从 85% 提升至 95%。自写规范的模型相比无辅助基线没有收益，仅 62% 规范通过审计；主要失败模式是忠实性不足，即规范只约束部分必需行为而放任其余。

Conclusion: 规范质量与最终结果高度相关：未解决实例中 89% 规范失败，已解决实例中为 47%。因此，忠实规范合成仍是一个具体的开放问题。

Abstract: Ensuring the correctness of LLM-generated code is a core challenge for modern software engineering. Benchmarks for agentic code generation check correctness with held-out test suites, which are inherently incomplete and increasingly susceptible to memorization. Formal verification avoids both problems, but existing work covers only standalone tasks whose specifications are given as input, not real issues, which touch large repositories and state intent in vague natural language. We present Benchproofer, a pipeline that turns a coding task with a known correct patch into a formally verified one: it writes a specification for the new code, summarizes the existing functions that code calls with axioms, and admits an instance only after mechanical and adversarial gates agree. Applying it to SWE-bench Verified yields SWE-Proof, 500 real issues whose correctness is formally verified rather than tested, and it extends to SWE-bench Pro. Across two frontier models, verification catches what tests miss: a quarter to a half of test-passing patches admit counterexamples, which a structured natural-language specification does not fix, while a correct formal one lifts resolution from 85% to 95% for Opus 4.8. Writing that specification is the hard part: models that must write their own gain nothing over an unaided baseline, and only 62% of their specifications pass our audit. The usual failure is faithfulness, a specification that constrains part of the required behavior and leaves the rest free. Specification quality still tracks the outcome, failing on 89% of unresolved instances against 47% of resolved ones, making faithful specification synthesis a concrete open problem.

</details>


### [254] [Reliability-Centered Evaluation of Sparse Longitudinal CT Lesion-Size Forecasting with Conformal Interval Calibration and Gompertz-Inspired Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21197)
*Lingfei Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于DeepLesion与DLT构建五访视同病灶轨迹基准（205条轨迹/129例患者），在固定T3→T4预测视界下逐步加入更早观察，系统评估点预测、不确定性、共形校准、亚组与Gompertz正则；发现m=2时RMSE最低，额外历史收益有限，精度、不确定性与轨迹一致性并非同步改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏纵向CT随访中仅少量先验观察时，病灶大小预测困难；现有研究缺乏标准化的同病灶轨迹基准，且对预测精度、不确定性可靠性、区间校准和轨迹一致性缺少联合评估。

Method: 从DeepLesion与Deep Lesion Tracker（DLT）构建五访视同病灶轨迹基准，含129例患者的205条轨迹。比较探索性“稀疏到最终”分析与主分析中固定访视索引视界设计：预测T3→T4的常见对数变化，并逐步加入更早观察。评估指标涵盖点预测精度、不确定性可靠性、事后共形区间校准、亚组表现和Gompertz启发轨迹正则化；比较Cohort-Level Feature GP、MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble、残差尺度区间等；使用十个训练种子，并采用患者分组开发集交叉验证选择正则化系数。

Result: 十个训练种子的平均留出RMSE在m=1、2、3、4时分别为0.4726、0.4305、0.4499、0.4513，m=2最低；增加历史观察未改善RMSE。m=4时，原始Cohort-Level Feature GP覆盖率接近95%名义水平，而MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble和残差尺度区间偏保守。患者级共形校准通常达到接近名义或保守覆盖率，但区间更宽。患者分组交叉验证选择Gompertz项λ*=0。全局人群参考常与病灶级变化方向相反，预测难度随解剖亚组变化。

Conclusion: 在控制预测视界后，额外历史观察带来的预测收益有限；预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性不一定同步提升，稀疏纵向影像研究应联合评估这些维度。

Abstract: Sparse longitudinal CT follow-up limits lesion-size forecasting when only a few prior observations are available. We constructed a five-visit DLT-derived same-lesion trajectory benchmark from DeepLesion and Deep Lesion Tracker (DLT), yielding 205 trajectories from 129 patients. We compared an exploratory conventional sparse-to-final analysis with a primary fixed visit-index horizon design predicting the common log change from T3 to T4 while progressively adding earlier observations, evaluating predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, post-hoc conformal interval calibration, subgroup performance, and Gompertz-inspired trajectory regularization. The evaluated methods showed partially overlapping point-prediction accuracy but distinct uncertainty behavior. Mean held-out RMSE across ten training seeds was 0.4726, 0.4305, 0.4499, and 0.4513 for m = 1, 2, 3, 4, indicating the lowest mean RMSE at m = 2; additional history did not improve RMSE. At m = 4, raw Cohort-Level Feature GP coverage was near the 95% nominal level, whereas MC Dropout, Deep Ensemble, and residual-scale intervals were conservative. Patient-level conformal calibration generally produced near-nominal or conservative coverage at the cost of wider intervals. Patient-grouped development cross-validation selected lambda* = 0 for the Gompertz-inspired term. A global population reference frequently opposed lesion-level change directions, and prediction difficulty varied across anatomical subgroups. Overall, additional historical observations provided limited predictive benefit once the prediction horizon was controlled, while predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, and trajectory consistency did not necessarily improve together, and should be evaluated jointly in sparse longitudinal imaging.

</details>


### [255] [MIRCID: Inferred Hub-miRNAs Drive Cross-Task Improvements in Drug Mechanistic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21280)
*Xin Cao,Yigang Chen,Jiatong Xu,Ziyue Zhang,Xiang Cheng,Shenyu Wang,Yangyi Zhang,Xiaoxuan Cai,Shidong Cui,Zihao Zhu,Xiang Ji,Hsi-Yuan Huang,Yang-Chi-Dung Lin,Hsien-Da Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MIRCID 框架，利用 HubmiRNet 从 L1000 landmark 基因推断泛癌 hub miRNA，并系统比较基因表达、推断 TF 活性与 miRNA 表达在通路分类和基于相似性的 MoA 检索中的效用；结果表明推断 HubmiRs 可作为具生物学信息的转录组重编码用于扰动药物建模，但实测扰动 miRNA 响应的恢复仍待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物机制-of-action（MoA）建模通常依赖扰动转录组，但匹配的 miRNA 测量往往缺失。推断调控特征提供了一种可扩展的数据复用方式，因此需要评估推断的 TF 活性和 miRNA 表达相对于原始基因表达在药物建模任务中的价值。

Method: 提出 MIRCID 框架，在通路分类和基于相似性的 MoA 检索中比较基因表达、推断转录因子（TF）活性与 miRNA 表达。HubmiRNet 从 977 个 L1000 landmark 基因推断 414 个泛癌 hub miRNA（HubmiRs），并构建 1,298 输出的变体。研究还引入通用嵌入对照，并进行互补性分析与示意性 rescue 案例分析。

Result: HubmiRNet 推断 HubmiRs 的 Pearson 相关系数达 87.72%；其 1,298 输出变体在 full-miRNA 任务上优于 SiCmiR（71.21% 对 67.30%）。在所评估的比较中，miRNA 增强比 TF 活性带来更一致的增益；通用嵌入对照的效用依模型而定。互补性分析识别出一种独特的、可部分线性恢复且保留基因衍生结构的表示。示意性 rescue 案例将分类提升与弱转录特征样本中生物学上合理的 miRNA 模式联系起来。

Conclusion: 研究支持将推断 HubmiRs 作为扰动药物建模中具生物学信息的转录组重编码方式，同时指出实测扰动 miRNA 响应的恢复仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Drug mechanism-of-action (MoA) modeling commonly relies on perturbational transcriptomes, but matched microRNA (miRNA) measurements are often unavailable. Inferred regulatory features offer a scalable way to reuse these data. Here, we present MIRCID, a framework comparing gene expression with inferred transcription factor (TF) activity and miRNA expression across pathway classification and similarity-based MoA retrieval. HubmiRNet infers 414 pan-cancer hub miRNAs (HubmiRs) from 977 L1000 landmark genes, achieving a Pearson correlation coefficient of 87.72\%; its 1,298-output variant also outperformed SiCmiR on the full-miRNA task (71.21\% versus 67.30\%). In the evaluated comparisons, miRNA augmentation provided more consistent gains than TF activity. Generic embedding controls showed model-dependent utility, while complementarity analyses identified a distinct, partially linearly recoverable representation that retained gene-derived structure. Illustrative rescue cases linked improved classification to biologically plausible miRNA patterns in samples with weak transcriptional signatures. These findings support inferred HubmiRs as a biologically informed recoding of transcriptomic data for perturbational drug modeling, while leaving recovery of measured perturbational miRNA responses to further validation.

</details>


### [256] [Multi-Subject Pretraining Enables Short-Calibration Personalization for Closed-Corpus Surface EMG Speech Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21288)
*Chenqian Le,Beatrice Fumagalli,Yasamin Esmaeili,Xupeng Chen,Tianyu He,Nikasadat Emami,Adeen Flinker,Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对基于表面肌电（sEMG）的无声语音接口，在每名被试数据量不足0.5小时的有限数据条件下，验证了"单被试检查点初始化+多被试预训练+目标被试微调"的个性化流程，实现21.7% CER / 31.9% WER，并证明仅需3分钟校准即可达到与全量校准无显著差异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sEMG无声语音接口受限于跨用户变异性和校准负担。作者希望在数据量有限（每人约21.3分钟）的设定下，探索能否通过检查点初始化、多被试预训练和短时目标被试校准来降低校准成本并提升性能。

Method: 采用27名被试（Aloud与Mimed语音），封闭50句语料库。使用留一被试（leave-one-subject-out）评估：从公开的单被试检查点初始化，在非留出被试上预训练，再在目标被试上微调。对比了无目标被试校准、直接检查点微调、随机初始化多被试预训练等基线，并测试了目标被试校准时长（3分钟 vs 约13分钟全量）与引入被试特定适配器（adapter）的效果，以及排除评估句对训练数据的影响。

Result: 完整流程达21.7% CER / 31.9% WER；无目标被试校准为49.3% CER，直接检查点微调为68.0% CER。随机初始化多被试预训练仅44.9% CER，且27折中有5折在固定调度下未收敛。宏平均CER随预训练被试数从1人（74.4%）增至26人（21.7%）。3分钟校准达20.5% CER / 31.7% WER，与约13分钟全量池（21.7% CER / 31.9% WER）无统计显著差异。被试特定适配器未带来可检测增益。将5个评估句从所有sEMG训练数据中排除后，CER/WER升至78.6% / 99.9%。

Conclusion: 结果支持在标准化电极布局、封闭语料库设定下进行短时校准的个性化，且检查点初始化相比随机初始化对优化收敛与精度有显著收益。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG)-based silent speech interfaces are limited by cross-user variability and calibration burden. We study a limited-data setting in which each of 27 speech-typical participants contributed less than 0.5 h of data (21.3 min on average) across Aloud and Mimed speech. Within a closed 50-sentence corpus, we used leave-one-subject-out evaluation, initializing from a released single-subject checkpoint, pretraining on non-held-out participants, and fine-tuning on the target participant. This pipeline achieved 21.7% character error rate (CER) and 31.9% word error rate (WER), compared with 49.3% CER without target-subject calibration and 68.0% CER for direct checkpoint fine-tuning. Multi-subject pretraining from random initialization followed by fine-tuning reached 44.9% CER and did not converge under the fixed schedule in 5 of 27 folds, indicating substantial optimization and accuracy benefits from checkpoint initialization. Macro-averaged CER declined from 74.4% with one pretraining participant to 21.7% with 26. Three minutes of target-subject calibration achieved 20.5% CER and 31.7% WER, with no statistically significant difference from the full approximately 13-min pool (21.7% CER and 31.9% WER). A subject-specific adapter provided no detectable benefit. Excluding the five evaluation sentences from all sEMG model-training data increased CER and WER to 78.6% and 99.9%. These results support short-calibration personalization in a standardized-montage, closed-corpus setting.

</details>


### [257] [FairLMs: A Turnkey Library for Fairness in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21296)
*Jiale Zhang,Michael Larionov,Zichong Wang,Zhipeng Yin,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairLMs 是一个 Python 库，通过显式声明模型能力与输入需求，将语言模型公平性研究中的偏见测量、缓解方法与证据评估统一整合，支持组件组合、方法对比与工作流扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型公平性研究涵盖偏见测量、缓解方法应用以及评估所依赖证据的审查。现有工具功能互补但接口各异，将其组合需先协调模型接口、证据格式、访问约束与结果类型，之后才能检查适用性或比较方法，导致整合成本高。

Method: 提出 FairLMs，一个 Python 库，通过显式声明模型能力与输入需求来连接上述活动。它提供 33 个内在与外在指标、14 个跨四类干预的缓解组件、14 个数据集与评分工具诊断、针对三种 Transformer 架构及所支持托管补全 API 的适配器，以及基准加载器。声明在执行前被检查，结果携带获得时的配置信息。

Result: FairLMs 支持兼容组件的组合、在统一协议下比较方法，并可将工作流扩展至新模型与数据集；其执行前声明检查与结果配置记录保障了适用性核验与可复现性。

Conclusion: FairLMs 通过声明式接口统一了公平性测量、缓解与评估诊断，降低了工具整合与对比的门槛，源码已开源于 https://github.com/FairLMs/FairLMs。

Abstract: Fairness research on language models involves measuring bias, applying mitigation methods, and examining the evidence on which an evaluation rests. Existing tools offer complementary functionality through different interfaces, so combining them requires reconciling model interfaces, evidence formats, access constraints, and result types before applicability can be checked or methods compared. We introduce \textbf{FairLMs}, a Python library that connects these activities through explicit declarations of model capabilities and input requirements. It provides 33 intrinsic and extrinsic metrics, 14 mitigation components spanning four intervention categories, 14 dataset and scoring-instrument diagnostics, adapters for the three Transformer architectures and supported hosted completion APIs, and benchmark loaders. Declarations are checked before execution and results carry the configuration under which they were obtained, so that compatible components can be combined, methods compared under a common protocol, and workflows extended to new models and datasets. The source code is available at: https://github.com/FairLMs/FairLMs.

</details>


### [258] [Fast And Accurate Text Content File Type Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21306)
*Manu Nandan,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于识别文本内容文件（尤其是源代码）类型的神经网络模型，在准确率和速度上均优于现有工具（如Magika）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各类组织普遍需要一种能基于文件内容识别文件类型的工具，这在网络安全领域尤为重要，因为魔数（magic numbers）和文件扩展名并不可靠。现有工具虽有实用性，但仍存在改进空间：基于模型的工具（如Magika）在计算负载与检测时间上表现欠佳，而使用编程语言构造的文件解析工具则在检测准确率上有待提升。

Method: 提出一种神经网络模型，用于识别文本内容文件的类型，特别是源代码文件。

Result: 在开源文件上的实验表明，该模型在文本内容文件类型识别上的平均准确率更高，同时检测速度约为Magika的4倍，模型体积还小28%。

Conclusion: 所提神经网络模型在文本内容文件（尤其是源代码）类型识别任务中，相比现有可用工具兼具更高的准确率与更快的速度，并具有更小的模型体积。

Abstract: A common requirement across organizations is to have a tool that can identify file types based on their contents, particularly in the cybersecurity domain where magic numbers and file extensions can not be trusted. While existing tools work well in practice, there is plenty of room for improvement either in terms of computational load and time for detection in the case of model based tools like Magika or in terms of accuracy of detection in the case of file parsing tools that use programming language constructs. In this study, we propose a neural network model for identification of types of text content files, especially source code, that is more accurate and faster than other available tools. Our experiments on open-source files indicate that it is not only more accurate on average for text-content file-type identification, but also approximately four times faster than Magika, while being 28% smaller in size.

</details>


### [259] [An Introduction to Compression-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21309)
*John Hurwitz,Edward Raff,Charles K. Nicholas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述并形式化了"利用压缩进行机器学习"的各种策略，提出并实证验证了一个基于压缩的机器学习设计框架，发现该类方法在常规任务上与基线相当，在恶意软件检测上明显更强，且调整设计选择可带来最高 0.62 的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损压缩算法（如 gzip）与自回归模型之间存在"看似循环"的双向依赖：前者可经归一化压缩距离（NCD）或最小描述长度（MDL）原理转化为机器学习方法，后者可经熵编码转化为无损压缩方法。这一关联在现代人工智能与机器学习中尚存未被充分发掘的潜力，亟需系统梳理与形式化。

Method: 作者综述并形式化了历史上利用压缩服务于机器学习的多种策略，进而提出一个面向"基于压缩的机器学习"的设计框架，并通过实证实验对该框架进行验证（涉及对不同设计选择的消融与对比）。

Result: 实证表明：基于压缩的方法与常规基线具有竞争力，在恶意软件（malware）任务上则明显更强；同时，改变框架中的设计选择可带来最高 0.62 的准确率增益。

Conclusion: 压缩与机器学习之间的双向转化关系具有实际价值。所提出的设计框架可有效指导基于压缩的机器学习方法，其性能可与传统方法抗衡并在特定领域（如恶意软件检测）占据优势，且设计选择的合理调优能显著提升精度。

Abstract: Any lossless compression algorithm (like gzip) may be converted into a machine learning method, via either Normalized Compression Distance or the Minimum Description Length principle. Any auto-regressive model may be converted into a lossless compression method via entropy coding. This seemingly circular dependence has unrealized potential in modern artificial intelligence and machine learning, and we survey and formalize the various strategies that have been used to leverage compression for machine learning. We introduce and empirically validate a design framework for compression-based ML, finding compression-based methods competitive with conventional baselines and decisively stronger on malware. We find that varying these design choices yields accuracy gains of up to 0.62.

</details>


### [260] [Deep Reinforcement Learning with Buffered Quantile Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21327)
*Mohammad Alipour-vaezi,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Deep-BQRL：一种无模型的分布式强化学习框架，将“缓冲分位数（buffered quantile）”目标扩展到神经函数逼近，从而在连续/高维场景下实现可解释的风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分位数的强化学习可通过优化累积回报分布的指定分位数实现可解释的风险敏感决策，但点分位数目标存在两个困难：回报分布微小扰动会导致分位数突变；精确的分位数敏感规划需要计算代价高昂的分布式优化。缓冲分位数通过对目标水平以下相邻分位数取平均，提供更平滑的替代目标，同时保留点分位数目标；然而现有方法仍是基于模型的，依赖显式回报分布规划，难以扩展到小规模表格问题之外。

Method: Deep-BQRL 是无模型分布式强化学习框架：直接从采样转移中学习条件回报分位数；从学习到的分位数函数的相关区域构造缓冲动作分数；利用集成（ensemble）分歧引导探索；并采用增强输入表示，使学习到的策略能够响应轨迹信息，而无需显式复现精确规划所需的分位数—状态递归。

Result: 在资产出售最优停止问题和 slippery FrozenLake 上，将 Deep-BQRL 与基于模型的 UCB-BQRL、表格型 PPO 和 TRPO 比较。在资产出售任务中，Deep-BQRL 在报告的目标水平上取得比 PPO 和 TRPO 更小的平均累积点分位数策略差距，而 UCB-BQRL 保持最小差距。学习到的停止决策随目标分位数变化，展示了该方法风险敏感行为的可解释性。

Conclusion: Deep-BQRL 将缓冲分位数学习从基于模型、表格型设置推广到无模型、神经函数逼近设置，在风险敏感最优停止任务中优于表格型 PPO/TRPO，并保持决策随目标分位数变化的可解释性；与基于模型的 UCB-BQRL 相比仍有差距。

Abstract: Quantile-based reinforcement learning provides an interpretable approach to risk-sensitive decision-making by optimizing a prescribed quantile of the cumulative-return distribution. Despite this appeal, learning under a point quantile objective is challenging: quantiles can change abruptly under small perturbations of the return distribution, and exact quantile-sensitive planning requires computationally demanding distributional optimization. Lower-buffered quantiles alleviate the former difficulty by averaging neighboring quantiles immediately below the target level, providing a smoother surrogate while preserving the underlying point-quantile objective. Existing methods based on this principle, however, remain model-based and rely on explicit return-law planning, limiting their applicability beyond small tabular problems. We develop Deep-BQRL, a model-free distributional reinforcement-learning framework that extends buffered-quantile learning to neural function approximation. The method learns conditional return quantiles directly from sampled transitions, constructs buffered action scores from the relevant region of the learned quantile function, and uses ensemble disagreement to guide exploration. An augmented input representation allows the learned policy to respond to trajectory information without explicitly reproducing the quantile-state recursion required by exact planning. Experiments on an asset-selling optimal-stopping problem and slippery FrozenLake compare Deep-BQRL with model-based UCB-BQRL and tabular PPO and TRPO implementations. In asset selling, Deep-BQRL attains smaller mean cumulative point-quantile policy gaps than PPO and TRPO at the reported target levels, while UCB-BQRL retains the smallest gaps. The learned stopping decisions also vary with the target quantile, providing an interpretable illustration of the method's risk-sensitive behavior.

</details>


### [261] [Routine Blood Tests Outperform CRP for Distinguishing Bacterial From Viral Infection in Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21332)
*Mihaela Demireva,Zhecho Mitev,Djuna Chinareva-Klimentova,Svetoslav Ivanov,Georgi Nalbantov,Dimitar Mitev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 回顾性研究基于906例2–14岁儿童急性感染患者的血常规（CBC）、CRP及年龄数据，训练多种监督分类模型以区分病毒与细菌感染；含全部特征的XGBoost模型最佳，样本外AUC为81.7%、敏感度70.8%、特异度79.2%，且所有模型AUC均优于仅基于CRP的决策规则，提示CBC具有增量预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童急性感染以病毒或细菌为主，但两者临床鉴别困难，导致儿科医生倾向经验性使用抗生素，加剧抗菌药物耐药。CRP单独判断感染类型能力有限，因此需要评估CBC在现有CRP基础上能否提供额外预测信息。

Method: 采用回顾性研究设计，纳入2022–2026年间906例2–14岁、经检测确诊为病毒或细菌感染的儿科患者；纳入标准还要求具备CBC结果和CRP测量值。以CBC参数、CRP及年龄作为输入特征，构建多种监督分类模型，并以AUC、敏感度和特异度评价模型性能；同时与仅基于CRP的决策规则进行比较。

Result: 表现最佳的是包含全部特征的XGBoost模型，样本外AUC为81.7%，敏感度70.8%，特异度79.2%。所有训练的模型在AUC上均优于仅基于CRP的决策规则模型。

Conclusion: CBC对鉴别儿童病毒与细菌感染具有额外预测价值，但单独依靠CBC或CRP仍不足以支持临床决策。抗生素处方应综合包括CBC在内的多种因素，其中部分因素尚未纳入常规实践；未来需进一步开展外部验证并评估临床可用性与阈值选择。

Abstract: Acute infectious diseases are among the leading causes of medical consultations and hospitalizations in children worldwide. These infections are predominantly caused by viruses or bacteria, yet differentiating between the two remains a common clinical challenge. As a result, pediatricians often default to the safer option of prescribing antibiotics contributing to the growing problem of antimicrobial resistance. The objective is to assess the additional predictive value of CBC towards determining the current infection. This retrospective study used data from 906 pediatric patients aged between 2 and 14 years who were tested positive either for viral or bacterial infection between 2022 and 2026. Inclusion criteria further required availability of CBC results and CRP level measurements. These laboratory parameters as well as age were used as input features for several supervised classification models. Model performance was evaluated using AUC, sensitivity and specificity. The best performing model is XGBoost, which included all features, achieving out of-sample performance of AUC of 81.7% and sensitivity of 70.8%, specificity of 79.2%. All trained models outperform a CRP-based only decision-rule model in terms of AUC. We suggest that the decision to prescribe antibiotics should be based on a number of factors, including but not limited to CBC, some of which are not currently incorporated into routine practice.

</details>


### [262] [IntBMoE: Integrating Block-Level Conditioning into Expert Composition for Full-Participation Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21346)
*Ran Cheng,Longfei Xu,Zheng Liu,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 IntBMoE：一种块条件化的 MoE 架构，通过「稠密专家组合 + 稀疏块执行」将 token 的参与度、执行量与参数实体化三个量彻底解耦；在图像分类、语言建模、序列推荐上验证有效，并已落地高德生成式推荐系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 设计无法独立控制三个相互纠缠的度量：(1) 参与度（participation）——单个 token 的输出实际融合了多少个专家的知识；(2) 执行量（execution）——实际参与计算的专家数，决定计算成本；(3) 实体化（materialization）——需要构建并存储的专家级参数集数量，决定显存/内存成本。稀疏路由虽能压低执行与实体化，却牺牲参与度（每个 token 只有少数专家贡献）；稠密输出混合能恢复满参与度，但执行量随专家数线性增长；参数合并（parameter-merging）把执行量固定为单专家，却使实体化数量随路由决策数增长。三者存在此消彼长的权衡，缺少一种能同时兼顾「满知识参与 + 低计算 + 低存储」的方案。

Method: IntBMoE 采用「稠密专家组合 + 稀疏块执行」的块条件化 MoE：(1) 块（block）来自一个小型可学习的码本（codebook），每个条目对应一个块，因此块的总数由码本固定、与输入无关，从而将实体化规模限定为常数级；(2) 在每个内部层，用一个轻量级超网络（hypernetwork）将该层专家池中的全部专家基融合成一个组合专家，故单个组合专家即承载整个专家池的知识，实现满参与度；(3) 路由仅将每个 token 分派到少数几个块，使实际执行保持稀疏，计算成本低；(4) 进一步提出双路径残差门控（Dual-Path Residual Gating, DPRG），通过乘法门控将两条独立组合出的路径耦合起来，提升表达能力。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，IntBMoE 相较多种代表性稀疏与稠密 MoE 基线取得一致性提升；在语言建模与序列推荐任务上的额外实验验证了其跨模态/跨任务泛化能力。工程侧已全量部署于高德（AMap）的生成式推荐系统，在 60ms 延迟预算下服务数亿用户，在线 A/B 测试取得 2.4% 的相对 UVCTR 提升。代码已开源于 https://github.com/AMAP-ML/DreamX-Rec/。

Conclusion: IntBMoE 通过码本化的块构造与超网络专家融合，在不增加执行与实体化成本的前提下恢复了全专家参与度，实现了参与度、执行量、实体化三者的解耦与解耦带来的性能增益；其工业级落地（低延迟、大规模在线收益）表明该范式不仅具备学术价值，也具备实际生产可用性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales capacity, but existing designs cannot set three quantities independently. For a single token, participation is how many experts contribute knowledge to its output, execution is how many are actually computed (compute cost), and materialization is how many expert-sized parameter sets must be built and stored (memory cost). Sparse routing keeps execution and materialization low, but shrinks participation: for each token, only a few experts contribute. Dense output-mixing restores full participation, but its execution grows with the number of experts. Parameter-merging keeps execution at one expert, but its materialization grows with the number of routing decisions. We propose IntBMoE, a block-conditioned MoE that decouples all three by pairing dense expert composition with sparse block execution. Its blocks come from a small learned codebook, one per entry. At each internal layer, a lightweight hypernetwork merges all expert bases in that layer's pool into one composed expert. Participation is full, because every composed expert draws on the entire pool. Execution stays sparse, because a router sends each token to only a few blocks. Materialization is bounded, because the codebook, not the input, fixes how many blocks exist. Dual-Path Residual Gating (DPRG) further couples two independently composed paths through multiplicative gating. Experiments on image classification show consistent gains over representative sparse and dense MoE baselines. Additional experiments on language modeling and sequential recommendation validate its generalization beyond vision. IntBMoE is fully deployed in AMap's generative recommendation system, serving hundreds of millions of users under a 60ms latency budget, with a 2.4% relative UVCTR gain in online A/B testing. Our code is available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/DreamX-Rec/.

</details>


### [263] [Knowledge-Graph-Augmented Chronos-2 for HEC-RAS Surrogate Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21381)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出KG-Chronos-2，将冻结的Chronos-2时间序列基础模型与水利工程知识耦合，用于HEC-RAS水位代理预报。在固定基准上，其事件均衡RMSE为0.246970，相比冻结Chronos-2、DCRNN风格模型和循环GeoFNO分别降低14.13%、29.38%和39.54%，并取得最低活动窗口与最终超前RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型具备通用预测能力，但可能缺乏具体水利工程项目的几何、拓扑与历史状态知识，直接用于HEC-RAS水位代理预报时精度受限；因此需要研究将项目知识耦合进冻结基础模型能否提升热启动代理预报性能。

Method: 提出KG-Chronos-2：以冻结的Chronos-2为预测器，引入精确状态残差解码、图条件历史检索和输入对齐校正三种项目知识耦合机制。任务特定拟合使用2008年模拟。对比方法包括持续性模型、残差LSTM、项目条件循环GeoFNO、水利DCRNN风格模型和冻结Chronos-2。评估覆盖2011与2002年模拟的64个固定24小时窗口，共享几何下71个河段、4675个横断面。

Result: KG-Chronos-2事件均衡RMSE为0.246970（原生水位单位），较冻结Chronos-2降低14.13%，较水利DCRNN风格模型降低29.38%，较循环GeoFNO降低39.54%；相对冻结Chronos-2的事件均衡RMSE差值的95%分层自助法区间为[-0.075177, -0.016317]，不包含零。六个系统中，KG-Chronos-2的活动窗口RMSE和最终超前RMSE最低。

Conclusion: 在固定基准上，将冻结时间预测器与项目知识耦合可有效提升HEC-RAS热启动水位代理预报精度。结果支持基础模型+工程知识融合路线，但结论目前受限于特定基准、共享几何和固定评估窗口，跨项目、跨几何泛化仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: We investigate whether coupling a time-series foundation model to hydraulic project knowledge improves surrogate forecasting of HEC-RAS water-surface elevation (WSE). We present KG-Chronos-2, which combines a frozen Chronos-2 predictor with exact-state residual decoding, graph-conditioned historical retrieval, and input-aligned correction. We compare the method with persistence, a residual LSTM, project-conditioned recurrent GeoFNO, a hydraulic DCRNN-style model, and frozen Chronos-2. Task-specific fitting uses the 2008 simulation. Evaluation covers 64 fixed 24-hour windows from the 2011 and 2002 simulations at 4,675 cross sections in 71 reaches on a shared geometry. KG-Chronos-2 achieves event-balanced root-mean-square error 0.246970 in native WSE units. It reduces RMSE by 14.13% relative to frozen Chronos-2, 29.38% relative to the hydraulic DCRNN-style model, and 39.54% relative to recurrent GeoFNO. The 95% hierarchical-bootstrap interval for its event-balanced RMSE difference from frozen Chronos-2 is [-0.075177, -0.016317]. KG-Chronos-2 also achieves the lowest active-window and final-lead RMSE among the six completed systems. These results support coupling a frozen temporal predictor to project knowledge for warm-start HEC-RAS forecasting on the fixed benchmark.

</details>


### [264] [Probabilistic Forecasting of Business Process Executions with Neural Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21382)
*Jiaxin Yuan,Daniela Grigori,Han van der Aa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将预测性过程监控中的下一活动与剩余时间预测建模为带标记时序点过程（marked temporal point process）的生成式序列建模，显式处理时间戳并列（ties），用Transformer编码器+混合解码器以精确对数似然训练，从而在单次前向传播中获得完整预测分布，并在十个公开日志上兼顾点精度、校准与锐度及推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务型系统运营者依赖对运行执行未来走向的预测，但预测只有在可靠性已知时才可行动。主流深度学习方法为判别式、确定性模型，只输出单一的下一个活动与单一剩余时间估计，缺乏可推理的分布；同时真实事件日志常违反简单点过程假设，连续事件频繁具有相同时间戳，需要显式处理时间并列问题。

Method: 将问题转化为带标记时序点过程的生成式序列建模，联合密度定义下一标记及其事件间时间，天然给出预测分布。针对真实日志中的时间戳并列，显式处理ties；模型架构为Transformer编码器结合事件间时间上的混合解码器（mixture decoder over inter-event times），并以精确对数似然进行训练。

Result: 在十个公开日志上，该模型在点精度上匹配判别式基线，在剩余时间分布的校准（calibration）与锐度（sharpness）上优于基线，并且推理成本最低：完整预测分布可在单次前向传播中获得，无需采样。

Conclusion: 将带标记时序点过程与Transformer混合密度解码相结合，可在保持点预测精度的同时提供更可靠、更锐利的剩余时间预测分布，并显著提升推理效率，为服务型系统运营提供可行动的概率预测。

Abstract: Operators of service-based systems act on forecasts of how a running execution will continue, and such a forecast is actionable only if its reliability is known. Mainstream deep-learning models for this task are discriminative and deterministic: they emit a single next activity and a single remaining-time estimate, without a distribution to reason over. We instead cast the problem as generative sequence modelling with marked temporal point processes, which define a joint density over the next mark and its inter-event time and therefore deliver predictive distributions by construction. Real event logs violate the simple-point-process assumption these models rest on, since consecutive events frequently carry identical timestamps; we handle such ties explicitly and combine a transformer encoder with a mixture decoder over inter-event times, trained by exact log-likelihood. On ten public logs, the resulting model matches discriminative baselines on point accuracy, dominates them on the calibration and sharpness of remaining-time distributions, and is the cheapest at inference, since a full predictive distribution is obtained in a single forward pass without sampling.

</details>


### [265] [Tracing the Evidence Behind Zero-Shot Time-Series Forecasting: A Source-First Taxonomy and Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21425)
*Delun Kong,Wanyun Ling,Chenxi Liu,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张零样本时间序列预测不应仅以“是否更新目标参数”定义，而应被视为一种证据访问声明，并提出以证据来源优先的分类框架来审计零样本预测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本时间序列预测定义只关注目标特定参数是否更新，但未规定系统可使用何种证据；冻结大语言模型提示、预训练时间序列模型和检索增强预测器都可满足“不更新”条件，却依赖不同迁移证据，导致基准比较不可审计。

Method: 提出“来源优先”分类法：先区分三类主要证据来源——冻结LLM先验复用、参数化时间序列预训练、检索增强外部记忆；再与实现架构分离。来源确定后，进一步提出四个审计问题：任务接口、预测对象与评分、预测时上下文、资源预算。

Result: 该框架将零样本时间序列预测重新定义为证据访问主张，并给出可操作的审计维度，用于识别不同系统在上下文、记忆和预算上的未披露差异。

Conclusion: 零样本时间序列预测排行榜应同时报告证据边界和接口假设，而不仅是分数，从而使基准进展反映可迁移的预测能力，而非未披露的上下文、记忆或资源预算变化。

Abstract: Zero-shot time-series forecasting (TSF) is often described as forecasting without target-specific parameter updates, but that training-status condition does not specify what evidence the system may use. A frozen language model prompted with serialized values, a time-series model pretrained on broad forecasting corpora, and a retrieval-augmented forecaster may all satisfy the no-update condition while drawing on different transferable evidence. This paper argues that zero-shot TSF should therefore be governed as an evidence-access claim. We propose a source-first taxonomy that separates three primary evidence sources---frozen LLM prior reuse, parametric time-series pretraining, and retrieval-augmented external memory---from the architectures that implement them. After the source is identified, four additional audit questions remain: task interface, forecast object and scoring, prediction-time context, and resource budget. The resulting agenda is to make zero-shot leaderboards auditable by reporting evidence boundaries and interface assumptions alongside scores, so that benchmark progress reflects transferable forecasting capability rather than undisclosed changes in context, memory, or budget.

</details>


### [266] [Decision-Focused Learning for Mean-Variance Portfolio Optimization via KKT-Based Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21427)
*Kensei Nosaka,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对均值-方差组合优化中“预测误差最小化”与下游决策质量不一致的问题，提出将下层MVO的KKT最优性条件嵌入上层学习问题的单层优化框架，在保留预算与卖空约束的同时实现可求解的决策聚焦学习（DFL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段方法先预测收益再优化组合，训练目标为预测误差，与最终组合决策质量不一致。已有DFL方法虽直接优化决策损失，但依赖代理损失或约束松弛，导致训练与评估时的约束MVO存在结构错配。因此，作者希望在保持约束结构的同时，缩小训练与评估之间的差距。

Method: 提出单层优化 formulation：将下层均值-方差组合优化问题的KKT最优性条件作为上层学习问题的约束/正则，从而把下层决策最优性嵌入预测模型训练。该形式显式保留预算约束和卖空约束，并保持对标准非线性优化求解器的可处理性。

Result: 在两个具有不同相关结构的真实ETF资产池上进行滚动窗口实验，所提方法在多个投资指标上取得最佳表现；同时验证了所提出正则化带来的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将下层MVO的KKT条件融入决策聚焦学习的单层优化框架，可在不松弛关键约束的前提下提升组合决策质量，在真实ETF数据上优于现有方法。但摘要未给出具体指标数值、统计显著性及不同市场条件下的稳健性细节。

Abstract: Mean-variance portfolio optimization (MVO) is a central framework in data-driven asset management. A widely adopted approach is a two-stage framework that first predicts expected returns and then solves the optimization problem based on these predictions, with the predictive models trained by minimizing prediction errors. However, this objective of prediction is not aligned with the quality of the downstream portfolio decision. Decision-focused learning (DFL), which directly minimizes the downstream decision loss within the learning process, has thus emerged as a promising direction. However, existing DFL approaches to MVO rely on surrogate losses or constraint relaxations for tractability, creating a structural mismatch between predictive model training and the constrained MVO solved at evaluation. We propose a single-level optimization formulation that incorporates the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions of the lower-level MVO into the upper-level learning problem. This formulation explicitly preserves the budget and short-sale constraints while remaining tractable for standard nonlinear optimization solvers. Rolling-window experiments on real-world ETF (Exchange Traded Funds) data across two asset universes with different correlation structures show that our method achieved the best performance on multiple investment metrics and also demonstrated performance improvement due to the proposed regularization.

</details>


### [267] [Optimal Randomized Proper Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21445)
*Zachary Chase,Idan Mehalel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在线学习函数类 $\mathcal{H}$ 的随机正确学习算法，其最优期望错误界为 $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})\log T)$，其中 $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ 为 Littlestone 维数，$T$ 为时间步长。该结果将 Daskalakis 与 Golowich（STOC 2022）的 $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})\log^6 T)$ 上界改进为仅差一个普适常数的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习理论中，Littlestone 维数 $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ 是刻画函数类可学习性的核心组合复杂度。已有工作表明随机正确学习算法可实现关于 $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ 的某种多项式对数依赖，但上界与最优界之间仍存在显著差距。本文旨在确定随机正确学习的最优期望错误界，解决该问题的对数因子优化，并刻画其紧性。

Method: 摘要未具体展开证明技术。论文通过理论分析在线学习与随机正确学习算法，证明期望错误界上界为 $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})\log T)$，并论证该界对最坏情况函数类在普适常数意义下最优。

Result: 核心结果为：随机正确学习算法的最优期望错误界为 $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})\log T)$。该结果改进了此前最优的 $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})\log^6 T)$（Daskalakis 与 Golowich，STOC 2022），并将对 $T$ 的对数依赖从 $\log^6 T$ 降低为 $\log T$；对最坏情况函数类，该上界在常数因子意义下最优。

Conclusion: 本文给出了在线学习函数类中随机正确学习算法的近乎紧确的期望错误界，显著推进了该问题的复杂度刻画。其结论表明，Littlestone 维数与 $\log T$ 的乘积是刻画最优随机正确学习错误界的基本量，并为后续在线学习上界与下界研究提供了更精确的基准。

Abstract: We prove that the optimal expected mistake bound of online learning a function class $\mathcal{H}$ by a randomized proper learning algorithm is $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log T)$, where $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ is the Littlestone dimension of $\mathcal{H}$ and $T$ is the time horizon. Our result improves upon the previously best known bound of $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log^6 T)$ given by Daskalakis and Golowich (STOC 2022), and is optimal up to a universal constant for worst-case classes.

</details>


### [268] [Understanding LLM Quantization through Activation-Guided Compensation and Orthogonal Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21450)
*Yamato Narita,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LLM训练后W4A4权激活量化误差做精确分解，将其拆为“激活引导的权重补偿项”与“正交残差”，并用持续通道离群量与常规激活量给出残差上界，据此推导随机Hadamard旋转、符号选择与通道缩放的实用准则；在8个Llama/Mistral模型上以无反向传播配置取得与梯度训练SpinQuant相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W4A4训练后权激活量化可显著降低大语言模型内存与推理开销，但激活离群值会破坏有效量化分辨率。已有权重优化、通道缩放、正交旋转等方法虽能缓解问题，却未清晰说明各自处理的是哪类误差分量，以及这些分量之间的关系，因此缺乏统一理论指导。

Method: 作者对局部权激活量化误差进行精确分解，得到激活引导的权重补偿项和正交残差项；利用持续通道级离群量与常规激活量对正交残差建立上界。该分解区分了可由权重补偿处理的误差与必须通过变换设计处理的误差。基于残差上界，进一步推导随机Hadamard旋转、符号选择、通道缩放的实用准则：随机符号抑制持续离群通道间的建设性干涉；采样多种符号模式改进变换选择；二阶矩平衡导出L2缩放规则，进一步松弛可恢复SmoothQuant式L∞缩放。

Result: 在八个Llama与Mistral模型上评估无反向传播的配置，性能与经梯度训练的SpinQuant具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该误差分解与残差界为W4A4后训练量化提供了统一分析框架，解释了权重补偿与变换设计的分工，并给出可操作的旋转、符号选择与缩放准则；无需梯度训练即可达到接近或相当于SpinQuant的效果。

Abstract: Post-training weight-activation quantization reduces the memory and inference costs of large language models, but aggressive W4A4 quantization remains difficult because activation outliers degrade effective quantization resolution. Although weight optimization, channel-wise scaling, and orthogonal rotation mitigate this problem, the error components they address and their relationship remain unclear. Using an exact decomposition of local weight-activation quantization error into an activation-guided weight compensation term and an orthogonal residual, we bound the residual using persistent channel-wise outlier and regular activation quantities. This decomposition clarifies which error components can be addressed by weight compensation and which require transformation design. We then use the residual bounds to derive practical guidelines for applying randomized Hadamard rotation, sign selection, and channel scaling. In particular, the analysis explains how random signs suppress constructive interference among persistent outlier channels, how sampling multiple sign patterns can improve transformation selection, and how second-moment balancing leads to an $L_2$ scaling rule while a further relaxation recovers SmoothQuant-style $L_\infty$ scaling. We evaluate these guidelines through backpropagation-free configurations across eight Llama and Mistral models, obtaining performance competitive with gradient-trained SpinQuant.

</details>


### [269] [Efficient Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21457)
*Heinke Hihn,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PainNAS：一种基于块、受泄漏控制的NAS方法，将LOSO评估中每受试者独立搜索改为受试者间共享搜索，使搜索次数由N降至B（B≪N），在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上以更少参数和FLOPs取得相当的被试级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络广泛用于自动疼痛评估，但架构设计仍主要依赖人工；将NAS引入LOSO评估时，完全嵌套实现需N次独立架构搜索，按近似线性训练成本计复杂度为O(N²)，计算代价过高。

Method: PainNAS采用基于块的、受泄漏控制的搜索策略，在受试者之间共享NAS运行；通过将搜索次数从N减少到B（B≪N），避免完全嵌套LOSO带来的重复搜索开销，同时控制数据泄漏。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上，PainNAS取得与基线相当的被试级准确率，同时显著减少模型参数与FLOPs。

Conclusion: 共享式、块级且泄漏受控的NAS可在LOSO疼痛评估中有效替代昂贵的完全嵌套搜索，在保持准确率的同时提升计算与模型效率。

Abstract: Deep neural architectures are widely used for signal processing in automated pain assessment systems. However, architecture design has remained largely a manual task despite the potential efficiency benefits of Neural Architecture Search (NAS). Embedding NAS in a Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation is computationally demanding because a fully nested implementation requires $N$ independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$. We propose a block-based, leakage-controlled approach that shares NAS runs between subjects, reducing the number of searches from $N$ to $B$, where $B \ll N$, dubbed PainNAS. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, PainNAS yields comparable subject-level accuracy with substantially fewer parameters and FLOPs.

</details>


### [270] [What Must Survive? Exact Task-Information--State Frontiers for Resource-Sufficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21523)
*Ronald Katende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在下游任务未知时压缩系统的必要保留状态量，刻画了任务信息在状态形成前以有限字母表（advice）形式提供时，状态可由低秩划分缩减的精确前沿 p*(K)，并给出近似奇异值前沿、共同核下界、直和律及NP难结论，附软注意力、数字孪生与层次多任务三例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统常在下游任务尚未完全确定时就需被压缩，因此需要回答两个问题：此时必须保留多少状态，以及有限的前置任务信息（advice）能节省多少状态。论文旨在量化"提前获得多少任务信息"与"所需保留状态量"之间的权衡关系。

Method: 对有限线性任务族，任务消息在状态形成前揭示、精确任务在之后揭示。给定规模为 K 的 advice 字母表，用对状态空间 U 的划分 P（|P|≤K）定义前沿 p*(K)=min_{P} max_{C∈P} rank(T_C)，b 比特前沿取 K=min(2^b,|U|)。此外推导近似奇异值前沿、共同核（common-core）下界与精确直和（direct-sum）律，并证明寻找最优 advice 划分是强 NP 难的（在每个固定正逼近容差下仍成立）。

Result: 给出三个实例：(1) 良态 softmax 注意力构造在 9 比特区分 512 个延续之一时得到精确的 524,288→1,024 坐标前沿；(2) 域分解数字孪生导出"界面加局部状态"律及异质区域的加权划分问题；(3) 层次多任务模型给出两阶段前沿，3 比特将所需状态从 3136 降到 448 坐标，进一步任务信息逼近不可约的 328 坐标单任务下界。

Conclusion: 前置任务信息通过使各划分块内联合任务算子具有低秩，从而缩减所需保留状态；前沿由最优划分下的最大块秩决定，最优划分问题在计算上强 NP 难，且该困难性对任意固定逼近容差依然存在。

Abstract: A system may be compressed before its downstream task is fully known. We ask how much retained state is then necessary and how much can be saved by limited advance task information.
  For a finite family of linear tasks, a task message is revealed before state formation and the exact task only afterwards. For an advice alphabet of size $K$, the exact frontier is \[ p^*(K)= \min_{\substack{\Pcal\text{ partition of }\U\\|\Pcal|\le K}} \max_{C\in\Pcal}\rank(T_C), \] with the $b$-bit frontier obtained by setting $K=\min(2^b,|\U|)$. Thus advance task information reduces state through partitions whose joint task operators have low rank.
  We also give an approximate singular-value frontier, a common-core lower bound and exact direct-sum law, and strong NP-hardness of finding an optimal advice partition. The hardness persists at every fixed positive approximation tolerance.
  Three examples illustrate the result. A well-conditioned softmax attention construction gives an exact $524{,}288\to1{,}024$ coordinate frontier when nine bits resolve one of $512$ continuations. A domain-decomposed digital twin yields an interface-plus-local-state law and a weighted partition problem for heterogeneous regions.
  A hierarchical multi-task model gives a two-stage frontier in which three bits reduce the required state from $3136$ to $448$ coordinates, with further task information approaching the irreducible $328$-coordinate single-task floor.

</details>
